Reflection spectroscopy of a single-sided cavity yields a single-atom internal cooperativity of $C_{\mathrm{in}}=5.6(1.0)$ and collective coupling following $\sqrt{N}$ scaling up to $N=5$ atoms, with homogeneous coupling at antinodes over $\sim$200 $\mathrmμ$m along the nanofiber. Thermal tuning of a fiber Bragg grating provides in situ control of the cavity outcoupling rate over two orders of magnitude from the undercoupled to the overcoupled regime, relevant for various atom-cavity protocols. We confirm compatibility with reconfigurable atom arrays by demonstrating 99.95(6)\% imaging fidelity and trap lifetimes comparable to free-space array, at 1 $\mathrmμ$m from the nanofiber axis. Moreover, the diameter-engineered antireflection design of the nanofiber enables tweezer-based atom transport across the nanofiber with no measurable loss or heating at 4.5 $\mathrmμ$m above the nanofiber, supporting a two-layer architecture for the nanofiber-integrated atom array. From the demonstrated atom-cavity coupling, atom capacity, and cavity mode profile, we project time-multiplexed remote atom-atom entanglement generation at 40 kHz, with prospects for further scaling through technical enhancements and channel multiplexing.

</details>


### [108] [Dynamical, thermal, and ground-state multiparameter quantum metrology: Fundamental limits and their attainability in magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25977)
*Riccardo Pedroni,Víctor Izquierdo,Advay Burte,Paolo Abiuso,John Calsamiglia,Martí Perarnau-Llobet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将单参数估计的精度界限推广到多参数同时估计，涵盖动力学、热态与基态三种框架，确立可达条件，并针对矢量磁强计给出紧致界与最优多体控制哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有精度界限（如量子Cramér-Rao界等）多以单参数估计为主，而多参数同时估计存在参数间非对易等额外限制；同时，矢量磁强计等实际任务需要同时估计多个磁场分量，因此需建立统一的多参数界限及可达性条件。

Method: 考虑形式为 H_θ = H_θ^P + H^C 的哈密顿量，其中 H_θ^P 编码未知参数、H^C 为控制项；分别在（i）动力学幺正编码、（ii）热平衡态、（iii）基态三种计量框架下，将单参数界推广至多参数同时估计，并推导其饱和（可达）条件；随后以矢量磁强计为实例，求解多体控制哈密顿量 H^C 的最优形式。

Result: 获得了三种计量框架下多参数同时估计的推广界限及可达条件；针对矢量磁强计，导出磁场各分量同时估计的紧致精度界，并识别出能够使其饱和的最优多体控制哈密顿量 H^C。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了幺正动力学、热平衡态与基态三种情形下矢量磁强计的基本精度极限，为多参数量子计量及最优控制设计提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: We consider the simultaneous estimation of multiple parameters in Hamiltonians of the form $H_{\vecθ}= H_{\vecθ}^P + H^C$, where $H_{\vecθ}^P$ encodes the unknown parameters and $H^C$ is a control term. We consider three basic paradigmatic frameworks for metrology: (i) dynamical, where the parameters are encoded unitarily, (ii) thermal, and (iii) ground state. We generalize previous bounds for single-parameter estimation to simultaneous multiparameter estimation for all three metrological settings and establish the conditions required for their saturability. We then focus on vector magnetometry, deriving tight bounds for the simultaneous estimation of magnetic-field components and identifying optimal many-body control Hamiltonians $H^C$ that saturate them. These results establish the fundamental limits of vector magnetometry for unitary dynamics, thermal equilibrium states, and ground states.

</details>


### [109] [A dual-region ytterbium tweezer apparatus with an in-vacuum nanofiber cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25245)
*Shunichiro Hashimoto,Hideki Ozawa,Yusuke Hisai,Takao Aoki,Ryotaro Inoue*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种用于在含真空内纳米光纤腔的科学腔中，以光镊捕获单粒子费米子同位素镱-171（¹⁷¹Yb）的双区域实验装置。通过纵向移动光学黏胶脉冲输运原子，在科学腔中减速并重新捕获于磁光阱，随后将单原子装载到距纳米光纤约50 μm的2×2光镊阵列中，测得1/e寿命为9.5(7) s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真空内纳米光纤腔附近实现单原子光镊操控，需要兼顾科学腔周围的高数值孔径光镊光学访问空间与足够的原子供给。单一区域架构难以同时满足光学访问和原子源需求，因此需要将原子源与科学腔分离的双区域设计。

Method: 构建双区域实验装置：源区域距科学腔约240 mm，以保留科学腔周围的高数值孔径光镊光学通道；采用基于纵向移动光学黏胶的脉冲输运序列向科学腔发射原子脉冲，在科学腔中减速并重新捕获于磁光阱；随后将单原子装载至距真空内纳米光纤约50 μm的2×2光镊阵列。

Result: 成功将¹⁷¹Yb单原子装载到距纳米光纤约50 μm的2×2光镊阵列中，测得1/e寿命为9.5(7) s。验证了双区域架构能为真空内纳米光纤腔附近的单原子光镊操作提供充足的¹⁷¹Yb原子。

Conclusion: 双区域架构可有效兼顾科学腔周围的光学访问需求与原子供给，为在真空内纳米光纤腔附近开展单原子光镊实验提供了可行平台，具有应用于腔量子电动力学等研究的潜力。

Abstract: We present a dual-region experimental apparatus for trapping single atoms of the fermionic isotope ytterbium-171 (${}^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$) with optical tweezers in a science chamber containing an in-vacuum nanofiber cavity. A source region is located approximately $240\,\mathrm{mm}$ from the science chamber, preserving optical access around the chamber for high-numerical-aperture tweezer optics. A pulsed transport sequence based on longitudinal moving molasses launches atomic pulses toward the science chamber, where they are decelerated and recaptured in a magneto-optical trap. We subsequently load single atoms into a $2\times2$ tweezer array located approximately $50\,\mathrm{μm}$ from the nanofiber and measure a $1/e$ lifetime of $9.5(7)\,\mathrm{s}$. These results demonstrate that the dual-region architecture provides sufficient ${}^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$ atoms for single-atom tweezer operation near an in-vacuum nanofiber cavity.

</details>


### [110] [Hyperbolic Restricted Boltzmann Machine Neural Quantum State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26032)
*H. L. Dao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出首个非欧几里得非自回归神经量子态——双曲受限玻尔兹曼机(HRBM)，在量子Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型中优于欧氏RBM，能精确重建完整纠缠谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体律纠缠量子系统对传统欧氏神经量子态(NQS)的表征能力构成挑战，而双曲几何具有指数级体积，可能更自然地表示体律量子系统。

Method: 构造双曲受限玻尔兹曼机(HRBM)，作为一种非欧几里得、非自回归的NQS，并在量子Sherrington-Kirkpatrick(QSK)模型的变分蒙特卡洛(VMC)框架下进行训练与评估。

Result: 在系统规模N=14至N=24（希尔伯特空间增大512倍）范围内，HRBM在基态能量优化、Renyi-2与von Neumann绝对纠缠熵重建误差上均优于RBM；HRBM能在15个数量级范围内忠实再现QSK完整纠缠谱，而RBM持续高估次主导模式。此外，RBM类NQS在QSK体律系统中随希尔伯特空间指数增长呈多项式标度。

Conclusion: 双曲非自回归NQS因其底层双曲几何的指数体积特性，可能在表示体律量子系统方面优于传统欧氏NQS，为概念验证提供了依据。

Abstract: We construct the first type of non-Euclidean non-autoregressive neural quantum state (NQS) in the form of the hyperbolic Restricted Boltzmann Machine (HRBM), which is studied in the variational Monte-Carlo (VMC) setting of the Quantum Sherrington-Kirkpatrick (QSK) model whose ground state exhibits volume-law entanglement. Across a 512-fold increase in the Hilbert space dimension corresponding to a system size increase from $N=14$ to $N=24$, HRBM NQS robustly outperforms its Euclidean version, the RBM NQS, in terms of better ground state energy optimization as well as lower Renyi-2 $S_2$ and von Neumann $S_{vN}$ absolute entanglement entropy reconstruction errors. More importantly, for all QSK system sizes, HRBM NQS demonstrates a superior expressivity in faithfully reproducing the entire entanglement spectrum of the QSK model from the top eigenvalues down to the tail end across 15 orders of magnitude, while RBM NQS consistently overestimates the sub-dominant modes. This work furnishes a proof-of-concept demonstrating that hyperbolic non-autoregressive NQS ansatzë, thanks to the exponential volume of the hyperbolic geometry underlying their constructions, might be more natural at representing volume-law quantum systems than conventional Euclidean NQS. Furthermore, an interesting byproduct of this work is the polynomial scaling result of RBM-type NQS ansatzë in the QSK volume-law system as the Hilbert space increases exponentially.

</details>


### [111] [Operator Score Matching for Learning Quantum Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25246)
*Shreya Shukla,Abhijith Jayakumar,Andrey Y. Lokhov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子版本的分数匹配方法——算子分数匹配（Operator Score Matching），无需计算配分函数即可从低温热态测量中高效学习量子哈密顿量参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从低温热态测量中学习量子哈密顿量是量子物理的基础问题。现有方法（基于半定优化或配分函数计算）复杂度高，可扩展性受限，因此需要一种避免配分函数、计算上更高效的框架。

Method: 借鉴经典分数匹配思想，利用算子代数中广义导数与分部积分概念，构建量子类比；提出算子分数匹配损失函数，通过简单梯度下降恢复哈密顿量参数，避免计算难处理的归一化常数（配分函数）；采用嵌套对易子展开的有限深度截断实现计算。

Result: 在横场Ising模型、XXZ自旋链、Fermi-Hubbard模型以及格点φ⁴理论的吉布斯态上进行数值实验，利用嵌套对易子展开的有限深度截断实现了参数的稳健高效恢复。

Conclusion: 算子分数匹配是一种实用、无需配分函数的量子哈密顿量学习框架，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Learning quantum Hamiltonians from low-temperature thermal state measurements is a fundamental problem in quantum physics. Scalability of existing methods is limited by the complexity of semidefinite optimization problems or partition function computation. Here, we develop a quantum analog of classical score matching method that exploits generalized notions of derivatives and integration by parts in operator algebras. We introduce a new \emph{Operator Score Matching} loss function that recovers Hamiltonian parameters by a simple gradient descent without the need to compute intractable normalization constants. Numerical experiments on the Gibbs states of the transverse-field Ising model, XXZ spin chain, Fermi-Hubbard model, and lattice $φ^4$ theory demonstrate efficient parameter recovery using finite-depth truncations of nested commutator expansions. Our results establish Operator Score Matching as a practical, partition-function-free framework for quantum Hamiltonian learning.

</details>


### [112] [In-orbit operation of a programmable quantum photonic processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25248)
*Simon Steiner,Peter Schiansky,Antonius Scherer,Riccardo Albiero,Mathias Dragosits,Zhenghao Yin,Martin Mauser,Patrik Zahálka,Raphael Pimenta,Cristóbal Melo,Cédric Léonard,Abhiram Rajan,Niki Di Giano,Antonino Caime,Roberto Osellame,Francesco Ceccarelli,Daniel Martínez,Tobias Guggemos,Iris Agresti,Philip Walther*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了在纳米卫星上运行的、基于六模式通用集成光路的可编程量子光子平台，处理双光子并观测到双光子干涉，首次将量子光态作为在轨计算资源使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星任务受体积、重量和功耗的严格限制，而量子光子学可在固定硬件资源下，利用光子干涉增强处理大量在轨数据的机器学习模型。但挑战在于：除生成单光子外，光子必须保持相互不可区分，而这一脆弱条件在火箭发射、强热漂移和辐射的空间任务环境中极难维持。因此，尽管光量子态已在轨用于保密通信和基础测试，却从未被用作计算资源。

Method: 在纳米卫星上部署可编程量子光子平台，采用六模式通用集成光路处理两个光子；通过编程不同的酉变换并将光子调至不可区分状态。

Result: 观测到双光子干涉，实现了非经典光的在轨产生、操控与探测。

Conclusion: 该工作将天基技术拓展至在轨量子辅助计算，例如对地观测数据的本地编码，或作为分布式量子网络的节点。

Abstract: Quantum technologies promise computational capabilities beyond the reach of classical systems. A forward-looking application lies in satellite missions, which increasingly depend on onboard computing under stringent constraints on size, weight and power. Quantum photonics is particularly attractive here: photon interference can enhance the machine-learning models needed to process large onboard data volumes, at fixed hardware resources. However, harnessing this interference requires more than generating single photons, as they must remain mutually indistinguishable: a fragile condition that is hard to maintain within the technically demanding framework of a space mission, which includes a rocket launch, strong thermal drifts, and radiation. This is why quantum states of light, though already generated and transmitted in orbit for secure communication and fundamental tests, have never been used as a computational resource. Here, we report a programmable quantum photonic platform operating on a nanosatellite, processing two photons in a six-mode universal integrated circuit. By programming distinct unitaries and tuning the photons into indistinguishability, we observe two-photon interference, establishing the on-board generation, manipulation and detection of non-classical light. This extends space-based technologies towards in-orbit quantum-assisted computing, for instance local encoding of Earth-observation data, or nodes in a distributed quantum network.

</details>


### [113] [Entanglement Embezzlement from Diffusive Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26362)
*Shi-Xin Zhang,Shuo Liu,Yu-Qin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文揭示了纠缠挪用（entanglement embezzlement）的守恒律机制：在固定U(1)电荷的典型随机纯态中，电荷偏置可将O(√L)电荷涨落转化为O(√L)可借纠缠，而半填充处的粒子-空穴对称性使该贡献完全消失；在电荷守恒随机电路中，扩散在log Schmidt-rank坐标上以t^{1/4}尺度展宽，从而在体积律熵饱和后继续激活纠缠挪用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠挪用研究"局域操作与经典通信下能借用多少纠缠、同时以较小误差归还原态"这一操作资源，但其在多体态中的机制尚不清楚。作者希望（1）找到控制该资源的普适机制，特别是在固定U(1)电荷的随机纯态中；（2）理解该资源如何在动力学（电荷守恒随机电路）中生成。

Method: 1) 在固定U(1)电荷的典型随机纯态中，通过分析Schmidt谱在log Schmidt-rank坐标上的概率质量重分布，证明有限误差转换律；对半填充与非半填充情形分别给出对称性论证。2) 在电荷守恒随机电路中，结合流体力学分析（刻画扩散）与大规模replica张量网络数值计算，追踪操作相关分布在log Schmidt-rank坐标上的演化。

Result: (a) 非半填充时，电荷偏置将O(√L)的电荷涨落转换为O(√L)的可借纠缠量；半填充时粒子-空穴对称性完全抵消该贡献。(b) 动力学上，扩散以t^{1/4}尺度展宽log Schmidt-rank坐标为操作相关分布，并提升可借纠缠量；该效应在主导的体积律熵饱和后仍持续。

Conclusion: 纠缠挪用受守恒律支配：U(1)电荷偏置作为一种守恒量梯度，可被“兑换”为可操作纠缠，而粒子-空穴对称性则删除该通道。电荷输运在体积律熵饱和之后仍持续重组纠缠谱并激活挪用，说明纠缠熵的饱和并不意味着纠缠资源结构停止演化。

Abstract: Entanglement embezzlement asks how much entanglement can be borrowed from a many-body state by local operations and classical communication while returning that state with a small error. We uncover a conservation-law mechanism that redistributes the dominant probability mass of the Schmidt spectrum in the logarithmic Schmidt-rank coordinate and thereby controls this operational resource. For typical random pure states at fixed U(1) charge, we prove a finite-error conversion law: away from half filling, the charge bias converts $O(\sqrt L)$ charge fluctuations into $O(\sqrt L)$ borrowable entanglement, whereas particle--hole symmetry at half filling removes this contribution entirely. We then show how the resource develops dynamically in charge-conserving random circuits. Combining hydrodynamic analysis with large-scale replica tensor-network calculations, we find that diffusion broadens the operationally relevant distribution in the logarithmic Schmidt-rank coordinate on the scale $t^{1/4}$ and increases the amount of entanglement that can be borrowed. Charge transport therefore continues to reorganize the entanglement spectrum and activate embezzlement after the leading volume-law entropy has saturated.

</details>


### [114] [Exploring Asymmetric QEC Code Concatenation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25251)
*Sayam Sethi,Maxwell Poster,Aditi Awasthi,Willers Yang,Joshua Viszlai,Jonathan Mark Baker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文形式化并研究了[[4,1,2]] Iceberg码级联结构中的Hadamard形变空间，提出六种形变策略，在码容量噪声模型下比较，发现其中一种策略能在逻辑记忆实验中实现近乎对称的X/Z逻辑错误率并同时降低总逻辑错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联量子纠错码可在不寻找新码的情况下提升联合码的有效距离，Iceberg码[[4,1,2]]因资源开销最低而常被用作构建块。但其X与Z校验数量不对称，级联后导致X与Z可观测之间的逻辑错误率偏斜。通过Clifford形变各子块可缓解该不对称，但可能的Hadamard形变空间随级联层数呈双指数增长，因此需要对该空间进行形式化与系统研究。

Method: 形式化Iceberg码级联的Hadamard形变空间，提出六种具体的形变策略，并在码容量噪声模型（含有偏置与无偏置两种情形）下，使用级联最大似然软信息译码器对各策略进行比较评估。

Result: 在所研究的六种策略中，有一种策略在逻辑记忆实验中实现了近乎一致的X与Z逻辑错误率，同时降低了总逻辑错误率（即X与Z错误率之和）。

Conclusion: 通过形式化形变空间并提出并比较多种具体策略，证明了合理选择Hadamard形变策略可有效缓解Iceberg码级联结构中的X/Z逻辑错误率不对称，并同时改善整体逻辑错误率，为级联量子纠错码的设计提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Concatenated quantum error-correcting codes have recently gained popularity because they improve the effective distance of a joint code without requiring the discovery of new codes with desirable parameters. A common building block for such studies is the $[[4,1,2]]$ Iceberg code, the smallest error-detecting code and therefore the one with the lowest resource overhead. However, it has an asymmetric number of $X$ and $Z$ checks, and concatenating it produces skewed logical error rates (LERs) between the $X$ and $Z$ observables. This asymmetry can be mitigated by Clifford-deforming the individual blocks of the concatenation, but the space of possible Hadamard deformations grows doubly exponentially with the number of concatenation levels. We formalize this deformation space and investigate it by proposing six concrete deformation strategies, which we compare under a code capacity (with and without bias) noise model using a concatenated maximum-likelihood soft-information decoder. Among the strategies we study, one one of them achieves near-identical $X$ and $Z$ LERs in a logical-memory experiment while simultaneously reducing the total LER (the sum of the $X$ and $Z$ error rates).

</details>


### [115] [Lattice Lindbladian simulation by patching and merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26372)
*Kaoru Mizuta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于格点Lindbladian局域性的量子算法：稀疏耗散系统实现近最优门数O(Nt·polylog(Nt/ε))；一般有限程相互作用与耗散系统模拟含时观测量的门数为O((Nt)^{4/3}·polylog(Nt/ε))，采样复杂度Θ(ε^{-2})，为已知最小系统尺寸依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散量子多体系统（局域Lindbladian）动力学模拟是量子计算的基础任务。Hamiltonian模拟已接近最优门数，但Lindbladian模拟的可比结果仍缺失。本文旨在利用格点Lindbladian的局域性，缩小这一差距。

Method: 基于两种利用局域性的技术：1）Patching（拼接）：将耗散动力学分解为子系统上的动力学，误差指数小，推广了Haah-Hastings-Kothari-Low近最优Hamiltonian模拟算法的核心思想；2）Merging（合并）：将反向耗散动力学吸收到演化的其他部分，显著降低准概率采样开销。对稀疏耗散（含边界驱动）系统建立近最优算法；对一般有限程相互作用与耗散的格点系统，构造模拟时间演化观测量的算法。

Result: 稀疏耗散系统：门数O(Nt·polylog(Nt/ε))，达近最优。一般格点Lindbladian：每样本门数O((Nt)^{4/3}·polylog(Nt/ε))，采样复杂度Θ(ε^{-2})；在保持对1/ε多项式对数依赖的算法中，系统尺寸依赖为已知最小。

Conclusion: 结果表明局域性可被充分利用以实现耗散多体动力学的快速量子模拟，为预测非平衡现象、制备目标量子态等应用开辟了道路。

Abstract: Simulating the dynamics of dissipative quantum many-body systems governed by local Lindbladians is a fundamental task in quantum computation. While the near-optimal gate count has been achieved for Hamiltonian simulation, comparable results for Lindbladian simulation remain elusive. In this work, we develop quantum algorithms for lattice Lindbladians by exploiting their locality. First, we consider sparsely dissipative systems, in which the dissipation is sparsely located, including boundary-driven systems. We establish a near-optimal quantum algorithm for their dynamics with gate count $O(Nt \, \mathrm{polylog}(Nt/\varepsilon))$, where $N$ is the system size, $t$ is the evolution time, and $\varepsilon$ is the allowable error. We then consider generic lattice Lindbladians with finite-range interactions and dissipation, and develop an algorithm for simulating time-evolved observables with gate count $O((Nt)^{4/3} \, \mathrm{polylog}(Nt/\varepsilon))$ per sample with the sampling complexity $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$. The gate count has the smallest known dependence on the system size among algorithms retaining polylogarithmic dependence on $1/\varepsilon$. Our algorithms are based on two techniques that exploit locality: patching and merging. Patching decomposes dissipative dynamics into dynamics on subsystems with exponentially small error, generalizing a key idea underlying the Haah-Hastings-Kothari-Low algorithm for near-optimal Hamiltonian simulation. Merging absorbs reversed dissipative dynamics into other parts of the evolution, substantially reducing the overhead associated with quasi-probabilistic sampling. These results demonstrate that locality can be fully exploited to achieve fast quantum simulation of dissipative many-body dynamics, opening the way to applications such as predicting nonequilibrium phenomena and preparing desirable quantum states.

</details>


### [116] [A Quantum Phase-based Comparator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25262)
*Alessandro Berti,Alessandro Poggiali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出量子相位比较器（QPC），用相对相位编码操作数，借助哈达玛门干涉比较两个值，给出硬编码与寄存器级联两种实现，代价为1个辅助量子比特、常数门数或深度随t线性，并可通过相位缩放与自适应倍增实现对数级测量次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子比较器基于可逆逻辑作用于基编码整数，电路宽度随精度（比特数）增长，资源开销大。作者希望改用相位编码来回避宽度随精度膨胀的问题，实现更紧凑的比较电路。

Method: 将两个带相反符号的相位置于一对哈达玛门之间，并用固定偏移使干涉中心化，使测量到0的概率恰在第一个相位较小时低于1/2。实现一：硬编码值写入两个相位门参数，代价1量子比特、5个门。实现二：寄存器驱动的级联，通过二进制加权受控相位门从两个t比特寄存器取值，额外消耗1量子比特，深度随t线性；级联使相位由寄存器内容线性诱导，故单电路可处理操作数对的叠加。读出为有偏硬币而非确定比特，作者量化了给定相位间隔下决策所需测量次数。将两个相位乘以整数可无代价地扩大决策裕度，自适应倍增策略将放大转化为测量次数随间隔倒数对数增长。

Result: 硬编码实现：1量子比特、5个门完成比较。寄存器实现：寄存器外仅多1量子比特，深度线性于t，且可处理叠加态操作数对。读出为有偏硬币，测量次数随相位间隔增大而减小；相位整数缩放不增加宽度或深度即扩大裕度；自适应倍增使测量次数相对于间隔倒数呈对数增长。

Conclusion: QPC以相位编码替代基编码，避免了比较器宽度随精度增长的问题，提供紧凑的常数代价与线性深度实现，并可通过相位放大与自适应倍增高效获得高置信度比较结果。

Abstract: Quantum comparators decide the order of two operands. They rely on reversible logic acting on basis-encoded integers, so their width grows with the precision. We introduce the Quantum Phase-based Comparator (QPC), which compares two values carried in relative phases instead. The circuit places the two phases, entered with opposite signs, between a pair of Hadamard gates, and a fixed offset centers the interference, so that the probability of measuring zero falls below one half exactly when the first phase is the smaller. We give two implementations. With hard-coded values, the two values are written into the parameters of two phase gates, and the comparison costs one qubit and five gates. With the register-driven cascade, the values are drawn from two $t$-qubit registers through binary-weighted controlled-phase gates, at a cost of one qubit beyond the registers and depth linear in $t$; since the cascade induces each phase linearly from the register content, one circuit handles a superposition of operand pairs. The readout yields a biased coin rather than a definite bit, and we quantify the shots that a decision takes at a given phase separation. Scaling both phases by an integer widens the decision margin at no cost in width or depth, and an adaptive doubling schedule turns this amplification into a shot count logarithmic in the inverse separation.

</details>


### [117] [Hilbert-space fragmentation selects channels of strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26453)
*Mingdi Xu,Kaixiang Lu,Daoguang Li,Lei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在带粒子种类分辨耗散的一维 $t$-$J_z$ 链中，研究者分析了强-弱自发对称破缺（SWSSB）在电荷守恒扇区碎裂为动力学不相连碎片时的行为，并证明典型固定组分碎片中不存在长程保真度与 Rényi-1 序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明 SWSSB 可在最大混合电荷扇区中出现，即使普通带电关联消失。但动机在于：当电荷扇区因动力学约束碎裂成多个互不相通的碎片时，这种序是否仍存在、由什么量决定，尚不清楚。

Method: 作者考虑一维 $t$-$J_z$ 链并引入种类分辨退相干，使每个碎片由守恒的“自旋词”标记。通过分析相隔有限区间之间的带电转移，给出保持自旋词不变所需的中间自旋周期性约束；并在全态两点关联意义下，针对任意有限区间内所有范数有界的带电算子进行证明。进一步引入横向交换项连接碎片，考察其对稳态的影响。

Result: 在典型固定组分碎片中，长程保真度与 Rényi-1 序均不存在；该结论对任意预设有限区间内所有范数有界带电算子一致成立。若碎片的自旋词具有本原周期 $k$，则支持一个 $k$ 粒子通道，其精确长距离值为 $ν^k(1-ν)^k/k^2$，且少于 $k$ 个格点的带电算子无法承载该序。横向交换在保持全局守恒量的同时连接碎片，并在有限尺寸下恢复整个扇区的稳态。

Conclusion: 守恒的自旋词决定了哪些局域通道能够支持混合态序；SWSSB 在碎片化扇区中的存在性由碎片的周期结构与可承载的带电算子尺度共同决定。

Abstract: Strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) can occur in a maximally mixed charge sector even when ordinary charged correlations vanish. We study this order when a charge sector splits into dynamically disconnected fragments. In a one-dimensional $t$-$J_z$ chain with species-resolved dephasing, each fragment is labeled by a conserved spin word. A charged transfer between separated finite intervals preserves that word only if the intervening spins satisfy a periodicity constraint. In the full-state two-point sense, we use this constraint to prove the absence of long-range fidelity and Rényi-1 order in typical fixed-composition fragments, uniformly over all norm-bounded charged operators in any prescribed finite interval. A fragment with primitive spin-word period $k$ supports a $k$-particle channel with exact long-distance value $ν^k(1-ν)^k/k^2$, and no charged operator on fewer than $k$ sites can carry this order. A transverse exchange connects the fragments while preserving the global charges and restores the full-sector steady state at finite size. The conserved spin word therefore selects the local channels that can support mixed-state order.

</details>


### [118] [Effective Study of Superconducting Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25296)
*Carlos Raul Javier Valdez,Hector Hugo Hernandez Hernandez,Guillermo Chacon-Acosta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用矩量子力学+Gaussian截断非微扰地处理transmon超导电路，把完整cos非线性重求和为闭式有效哈密顿量，给出量子缀饰频率并对标Mathieu精确解，比较三种耗散描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: transmon常用的Kerr/Duffing近似仅在大相位振幅下失效，需要一种保留Josephson势周期性、有界性且非微扰的有效理论，并能刻画Gaussian近似适用范围与耗散处理的正确性。

Method: 采用momentous quantum mechanics形式，对量子矩层系作Gaussian closure，把cos非线性重求和为半和形式的有效哈密顿量1/2[V(φ+φ_s)+V(φ−φ_s)]；推导裸JJ、cavity–JJ系统及耗散谐振子的有效运动方程；给出量子缀饰频率；并用switch symplectic结构量化Bateman动力学。

Result: Kerr近似作为特例被恢复；有效动力学与Mathieu函数对角化的精确结果对标，量子宽度G^{2,0}(t)比⟨φ̂⟩(t)更灵敏地标示Gaussian闭包有效边界；Bateman描述保持Heisenberg界、具闭式矩解，并在弱阻尼下复现Lindblad基准，误差界为(λ/ω_1)^2 φ_zpf^2。

Conclusion: Gaussian-closed moment formalism为transmon提供非微扰、保持周期性与有界性的有效描述；Bateman量化是弱阻尼下与Lindblad等价且可解析求解的耗散方案。

Abstract: We apply the momentous quantum mechanics formalism to the non-perturbative study of superconducting circuits in the transmon regime, deriving effective equations of motion for the bare Josephson junction (JJ), the cavity--JJ system, and a dissipative resonator. A Gaussian closure on the hierarchy of quantum moments resums the full cosine nonlinearity into the closed-form effective Hamiltonian $\tfrac{1}{2}[V(φ+φ_s)+V(φ-φ_s)]$, which is non-perturbative and preserves the periodicity and boundedness of the Josephson potential at all phase amplitudes; the standard Kerr (Duffing) approximation is recovered as a special case. We derive the quantum-dressed frequency $ω_{\rm eff}=Ω_p\sqrt{\cos(φ_{\rm zpf}/φ_0)}$, and benchmark the effective dynamics against exact Mathieu-function diagonalization, with the quantum width $G^{2,0}(t)$ providing the most sensitive diagnostic of the closure's validity and marks the boundary of the Gaussian approximation more sharply than $\langle\hatφ\rangle(t)$ does. We compare the Caldirola--Kanai, Bateman, and Lindblad descriptions: the Bateman dynamics, quantized with a switched symplectic structure, preserves the Heisenberg bound, admits exact closed-form moment solutions, and reproduces the Lindblad benchmark for weak damping, i.e., within the analytic error bound $(λ/ω_1)^2\,φ_{\rm zpf}^2$.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum Fisher information of driven-dissipative systems from Keldysh path integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26462)
*Malik Jirasek,Igor Lesanovsky,Viktoria Noel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种场论方法，将量子费舍尔信息（QFI）与Keldysh路径积分联系起来，通过对量子场进行受控展开得到QFI的半经典表达式，并在三个复杂度递增的系统中验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动-耗散多体量子系统可用于量子增强传感，非经典关联有望使测量精度超越标准量子极限。评估这种量子优势的关键指标是QFI，但在多体情形下计算QFI需要知道完整的系统-环境状态，这极具挑战性。因此需要一种适用于多自由度系统的方法来计算QFI。

Method: 采用场论方法，特别是将QFI与Keldysh路径积分相联系；通过对所谓量子场进行受控展开，得到QFI的半经典表达式。并以三个复杂度递增的系统进行基准测试：驱动-耗散谐振子、边界时间晶体（在大系统尺寸下变为半经典）以及二维集体发射原子阵列。

Result: 成功建立了QFI与Keldysh路径积分之间的联系，获得了QFI的半经典表达式，并在三个系统中进行了验证；评估了半经典描述成立的参数区间，并证明其可处理超出精确方法能力范围的系统尺寸。

Conclusion: 该场论框架为多体驱动-耗散系统的QFI计算提供了可行途径，半经典描述在特定参数区间有效，并能扩展到精确方法难以处理的更大系统尺寸。

Abstract: Driven-dissipative many-body quantum systems can be exploited in quantum enhanced sensing protocols. Here, non-classical correlations may allow one to reach a measurement precision that surpasses the standard quantum limit. The relevant figure of merit for assessing such quantum advantage is the quantum Fisher information (QFI). In a many-body setting its computation is challenging as it requires knowledge of the full system-environment state. Here we make use of a field-theoretical approach, which is particularly well suited for systems with many degrees of freedom. In particular, we show that the QFI can be linked to a Keldysh path-integral. This route leads to a semiclassical expression of the QFI, which is obtained through a controlled expansion in the so- called quantum fields. We benchmark the framework on three systems of increasing complexity: the driven-dissipative harmonic oscillator, the boundary time crystal - which becomes semiclassical at large system sizes - and a two-dimensional array of collectively emitting atoms. We assess in which parameter regimes the semiclassical description holds and gives access to system sizes beyond the reach of exact methods.

</details>


### [120] [The Failure of Simultaneous Quantum Typicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25726)
*Benoit Collins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了同时量子典型性失效的精确阈值：混合态三方、纯态四方；对四量子比特Higuchi–Sudbery态，满足三重叠边缘纯度界且熵余量足够小的序列与其张量幂渐进完美可区分（迹距离趋于2），并在邻域内保持。证明将同时量子平滑与$\CP^1$上的多项式集中联系，经对称性化为$\Sym^n(\mathbb C^2)$上的框架不等式，用Bernstein–Markov估计与次调和障碍控制成本，给出指数级阻碍。结果否证了Dutil（2011）多方量子典型性猜想及Drescher–Fawzi与Colomer–Winter的单次同时最小熵平滑猜想，并结合小系统正面结果确定同时典型性失效的最小方数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定同时量子典型性（simultaneous quantum typicality）究竟在多少方系统上会失效，是量子信息与统计力学中关于典型性与平滑熵的核心问题。Dutil（2011）的多方量子典型性猜想以及Drescher–Fawzi、Colomer–Winter的单次同时最小熵平滑猜想均预测此类典型性普遍成立，但缺乏反例与精确阈值。本文旨在给出失效的精确方数阈值，并构造反例以检验上述猜想。

Method: 对四量子比特Higuchi–Sudbery态构造满足三重叠边缘预期纯度界、且具有足够小固定正熵余量的序列，证明其与输入张量幂的迹距离趋于最大值2；再通过追踪一方（tracing out one party）得到混合三体例子。证明技术上将同时量子平滑与$\CP^1$上的多项式集中相连接；利用对称性将问题归约为$\Sym^n(\mathbb C^2)$上的框架不等式，其中线性熵亏损与多项式集中的二次成本相竞争；使用Bernstein–Markov估计与次调和障碍（subharmonic barrier）控制该成本，从而得到对同时典型性的指数级阻碍。

Result: 确立了同时量子典型性失效的精确阈值：混合态为三方，纯态为四方。对四量子比特Higuchi–Sudbery态，任意满足三重叠边缘纯度界且固定正熵余量足够小的序列，其与输入张量幂渐进完美可区分，迹距离趋于最大值2；该最大分离性在整个四量子比特态空间的某个开邻域内以共同的正常数余量持续成立。追踪一方后得到混合三体反例。由此否证了Dutil的2011年多方量子典型性猜想，以及Drescher–Fawzi和Colomer–Winter的单次同时最小熵平滑猜想；结合较小系统的正面结果，确定了同时典型性可能失效的最小方数。

Conclusion: 同时量子典型性并非普遍成立：其在混合态三方、纯态四方即可能失效，且以最大迹距离（趋于2）和开邻域稳定性的强形式出现。所发展的多项式集中—框架不等式—Bernstein–Markov/次调和障碍方法为处理同时平滑与典型性提供新工具。该工作否证了多个已提出的典型性/同时平滑猜想，并与小系统正面结果共同刻画了失效阈值。

Abstract: We establish exact thresholds for the failure of simultaneous quantum typicality: three parties for mixed states and four for pure states. For the four-qubit Higuchi--Sudbery state, every sequence satisfying the expected purity bounds on three overlapping marginals, with sufficiently small fixed positive entropy slack, is asymptotically perfectly distinguishable from the tensor powers of the input: its trace distance from them tends to the maximum value $2$. This maximal separation persists, with a common positive slack, throughout an open neighborhood in the full four-qubit state space. Tracing out one party yields a mixed tripartite example.
  The proof connects simultaneous quantum smoothing with polynomial concentration on $\CP^1$. Symmetry reduces the problem to a frame inequality on $\Sym^n(\mathbb C^2)$, where a linear entropy deficit competes with a quadratic cost of polynomial concentration. Bernstein--Markov estimates and a subharmonic barrier control this cost, yielding an exponential obstruction to simultaneous typicality.
  These results disprove Dutil's multiparty quantum typicality conjecture, formulated in 2011, and the one-shot simultaneous min-entropy-smoothing conjectures of Drescher--Fawzi and Colomer--Winter. Together with the positive results for smaller systems, they determine the smallest numbers of parties at which simultaneous typicality can fail.

</details>


### [121] [Superradiant Mpemba Relaxation in a Dicke Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25359)
*Matheus G. H. Santos,Hugo Sanchez,Italo M. de Araújo,Luis Felipe A. da Silva,Miled H. Y. Moussa,Norton G. de Almeida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在集体衰减的二能级系综中，Mpemba效应可与超辐射发射共存：初始更远离稳态的高能态在半群演化中反超更接近稳态的态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mpemba效应（初态越远离稳态反而越快弛豫）通常与独立衰减或简单热浴过程相联系。本文动机是在具有集体辐射动力学（超辐射/超荧光）的开放量子系统中考察这种反常排序是否仍然存在，并厘清其物理起源。

Method: 在对称Dicke流形中研究零温集体衰减，衰减为粒子数级联过程，速率Γn(N−n+1)。构造稀疏态对ρ_A=|⌈2N/3⌉⟩⟨⌈2N/3⌉|与ρ_B=(|0⟩⟨0|+|N⟩⟨N|)/2，用能量与迹距离追踪两者相对排序，并对比独立衰减情形，考察尺寸N=6–60及峰值强度标度。

Result: 尽管A初始能量更高、离基态更远，它在能量和迹距离上都反超B，因为A起始于Dicke阶梯中更易辐射的区域。两态均出现大于独立发射体参考值的发射峰。交叉现象在N=6至60的全部尺寸下持续存在，峰值强度呈现接近N^2的有效标度；同一态对在独立衰减下无交叉。

Conclusion: 集体辐射动力学是Mpemba效应出现的原因：超辐射区域导致更远离稳态的态更快弛豫，从而使反常排序与超辐射发射共存。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect occurs when a state initially farther from stationarity overtakes a closer one during relaxation. We show that this anomalous ordering can coexist with superradiant emission in a collectively damped ensemble of two-level systems. In the symmetric Dicke manifold, zero-temperature decay is a population cascade with rates $Γn(N-n+1)$. We construct the sparse family $ρ_A=|\lceil2N/3\rceil\rangle\langle\lceil2N/3\rceil|$ and $ρ_B=(|0\rangle\langle0|+|N\rangle\langle N|)/2$. Although $A$ is initially more energetic and more distant from the stationary ground state, it crosses $B$ in both energy and trace distance because it starts in a more radiative region of the Dicke ladder. Both states develop emission peaks larger than their independent-emitter references. The crossing persists for all examined sizes from $N=6$ to $60$, while the peak intensities show an effective scaling close to $N^2$. The same pair has no crossing under independent decay, identifying collective radiative kinetics as the origin of the effect.

</details>


### [122] [Transversal non-Clifford gates on good quantum locally testable codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26691)
*Yiming Li,Zimu Li,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将门框架应用于新的良好 qLTC 构造，在量子 LDPC 码与量子局部可测码上同时实现参数渐近最优的非平凡横向逻辑多控 Z 门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子 LDPC 码与量子局部可测码上同时实现参数渐近最优且非平凡的横向逻辑门是量子纠错领域的核心难题，已有构造（如 almost-good 码）基于超图乘积空间，难以直接推广到良好 qLTC。

Method: 采用覆盖空间方法，在有限算术立方复形上构造非零的 cup 积配对；将配对表示为 Moore 行列式乘积中的系数，并通过二部多重图特化证明其多项式非消失性；随后构造覆盖空间，得到一族渐近的良好 qLTC，其拉回配对诱导所需的非平凡横向逻辑作用。

Result: 成功在良好 qLTC 上诱导出非平凡的横向逻辑多控 Z 门，且参数达到渐近最优，基空间为算术立方复形而非超图乘积。

Conclusion: 该工作将门框架与良好 qLTC 构造相结合，利用算术立方复形上的 cup 积配对与覆盖空间方法，实现了参数渐近最优的非平凡横向逻辑多控 Z 门。

Abstract: We achieve nontrivial transversal logical multi-controlled-$Z$ gates with asymptotically optimal parameters simultaneously on quantum low-density parity check codes and quantum locally testable codes, by applying the gate framework of [arXiv:2604.01874] to the recent good qLTC construction of [arXiv:2609.20780]. To this end, we use the covering space method to construct a nonzero cup product pairing on a finite arithmetic cubical complex. This differs from the previous construction of almost-good codes, whose base space is a hypergraph product. We express the pairing as a coefficient in a product of Moore determinants and prove polynomial nonvanishing by a bipartite multigraph specialization. We then construct covering spaces to obtain an asymptotic family of good qLTCs on which the pulled-back pairing induces the desired nontrivial transversal logical action.

</details>


### [123] [Low-rank propagation for tridiagonalizable open quantum systems: near-linear scaling with system size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25368)
*Roman Ovsiannikov,Kurt Jacobs,Andrii G. Sotnikov,Denys I. Bondar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种确定性算法，将Lindblad动力学模拟的存储与单步计算复杂度从随希尔伯特空间维度D的二次增长降为线性（固定秩下），通过低秩向量集合传播、三对角分裂算符步与短时Kraus分支的集合秩截断实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度矩阵规模随希尔伯特空间维度D呈二次增长，是模拟大规模开放量子系统的核心障碍，亟需消除该二次瓶颈的算法。

Method: 面向哈密顿量含时对角部分加基重排后呈三对角形式的Lindblad动力学，采用低秩向量集合表示量子态，以三对角分裂算符步进行传播，并对短时Kraus分支执行集合秩截断，使固定秩下内存与每步开销对D线性。

Result: 在驱动氮空位-腔模型中，秩16即可将全密度矩阵可观测量复现至相对误差低于1e-5；在D约500时已比QuTiP快至多两个数量级，且运行时间近似线性增长。

Conclusion: 该确定性算法在特定结构（对角加三对角）的Lindblad动力学下有效突破密度矩阵二次复杂度瓶颈，兼顾高精度与近线性可扩展性。

Abstract: The quadratic growth of the density matrix with Hilbert-space dimension D is the central obstacle to simulating large open quantum systems. We introduce a deterministic algorithm that eliminates it for Lindblad dynamics whose Hamiltonian consists of a time-dependent diagonal part plus terms that are tridiagonal after reordering the basis. The state is a low-rank ensemble of vectors, propagated by tridiagonal split-operator steps and ensemble rank truncation of short-time Kraus branches, so that memory and cost per step are linear in D at fixed rank. For a driven nitrogen-vacancy-cavity model, rank 16 reproduces full density-matrix observables to relative error below $10^{-5}$, the method is up to two orders of magnitude faster than QuTiP already at $D \approx 500$, and its runtime scales nearly linearly.

</details>


### [124] [Evidence for a low-gap component in compressively stressed niobium thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26714)
*J. T. Paustian,Maciej Olszewski,Kiichi Okubo,Lingda Kong,B. L. T. Plourde,Ethan G. Arnault,Caleb W. Fink,Valla Fatemi,Ivan V. Pechenezhskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 溅射Nb薄膜的微波损耗随双轴压应力增强，出现低能低能隙激发，输运测量无法反映。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双轴薄膜应力如何影响Nb薄膜的微波响应，检验常规输运表征是否足以预测微波器件性能。

Method: 对系列不同双轴应力的溅射Nb薄膜进行输运测量（Tc、剩余电阻比）与微波谐振器测量，并用单相Mattis-Bardeen模型及附加低能隙分量模型拟合温度依赖损耗与频移。

Result: 各应力样品Tc（9.0±0.2K）与剩余电阻比几乎相同；高压缩应力薄膜谐振器温度依赖损耗更大、频移更大。单相MB拟合给出有效临界温度2.4±0.2K，约为输运值的1/4；数据更符合附加低能隙分量，其贡献随压应力增加。

Conclusion: 常规输运测量会忽略对应力敏感的低能激发，而这些激发显著影响微波性能，微波表征对超导薄膜应力评估必要。

Abstract: We investigate the microwave response of sputtered Nb films as a function of biaxial film stress. Although transport measurements show nearly identical superconducting transition temperatures ($9.0\pm 0.2$K) and residual resistance ratios across the stress series, microwave resonators fabricated from highly compressive films exhibit enhanced temperature-dependent loss and larger frequency shifts. A conventional single-phase Mattis--Bardeen description yields an effective critical temperature of $2.4\pm0.2$K, approximately a factor of four below the value obtained from transport measurements. We show that the data are instead consistent with the presence of an additional low-gap component whose contribution increases with compressive stress. These results show that conventional transport measurements can overlook stress-dependent low-energy excitations that strongly influence microwave performance.

</details>


### [125] [When are bosonic Gaussian states classical to learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26705)
*Senrui Chen,Antonio Anna Mele,Francesco Anna Mele,John Preskill*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文从学习理论视角研究玻色高斯态何时"经典可学"，发现可学性由热涨落驱动的平滑交叉：冷态（近真空协方差）层析需Ω(n^3)副本，本质非经典；暖态（热涨落超过真空噪声）仅需Θ(n^2 min(n,1+ν^{-1}))副本，ν=Ω(1)时降至经典量级Θ(n^2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是回答物理学基本问题——量子系统的经典行为何时涌现——并聚焦于玻色高斯态这一自然框架，因为它同时刻画经典场行为与光的量子特性。作者进一步将问题转化为学习理论问题：n模玻色高斯态在什么条件下能像学习经典2n元高斯分布那样，用同样少的样本和同样简单的操作完成学习。

Method: 采用学习理论/量子层析的样本复杂度分析框架。以协方差矩阵Σ刻画态：冷态条件为Σ≤(1/2+O(1/n))I，暖态条件为Σ≥(1/2+ν)I（ν>0）。分析限制在单副本（非纠缠）测量下的层析，并考察允许少副本纠缠测量时硬度的持续性；暖态一方则给出紧的副本数上界，并由简单、非自适应、无纠缠的外差（heterodyne）测量实现。

Result: (1) 冷态非经典：当协方差满足Σ≤(1/2+O(1/n))I（近真空协方差）时，单副本测量层析根本需要Ω(n^3)副本，严格超过学习经典高斯分布的样本复杂度Θ(n^2)；该硬度即使允许少副本纠缠测量也持续存在。(2) 暖态经典：当热涨落超过真空噪声，即Σ≥(1/2+ν)I（任意ν>0）时，单副本层析需N=Θ(n^2 min(n,1+ν^{-1}))副本，该界紧，且由简单、非自适应、无纠缠的外差测量达到；特别地当ν=Ω(1)时样本复杂度降至Θ(n^2)，与经典情形一致。

Conclusion: 论文紧致刻画了玻色高斯态可学性中的量子-经典交叉，揭示了基础物理与统计学习理论之间的新联系，并对实际传感实验具有启示意义。

Abstract: A fundamental question in physics is: When does classical behavior emerge from quantum systems? Bosonic Gaussian states provide a natural setting to explore this quantum-classical boundary, as they capture both the classical field behavior and the intrinsic quantum nature of light. Here, we address this problem from a learning-theoretic perspective by asking: When are bosonic Gaussian states classical to learn? That is, under what conditions (if any) can an n-mode bosonic Gaussian state be learned with as few samples, and with operations as simple, as are needed to learn a classical 2n-variate Gaussian distribution? We establish a smooth crossover in learnability governed by the state's thermal fluctuations:
  - Cold Gaussian states are non-classical to learn: When the covariance matrix satisfies $Σ\le(\frac12+O(\frac1n))I$, i.e. close to the vacuum covariance, tomography under single-copy (i.e., non-entangled) measurements fundamentally requires $Ω(n^3)$ copies, strictly exceeding the sample complexity $Θ(n^2)$ of learning classical Gaussian distributions. We show that this hardness persists even when few-copy entangled measurements are allowed.
  - Warm Gaussian states are classical to learn: When thermal fluctuations exceed the vacuum noise, parameterized by $Σ\ge(\frac12+ν)I$ for any parameter $ν>0$, we prove that single-copy tomography requires $N=Θ\left(n^2\min(n,1+ν^{-1})\right)$ copies. This bound is tight and is achieved by simple, non-adaptive, unentangled heterodyne measurements. Crucially, for $ν=Ω(1)$, the sample complexity drops to $Θ(n^2)$, matching the classical case.
  Our results tightly characterize a quantum-to-classical crossover in the learnability of bosonic Gaussian states, reveal a novel connection between fundamental physics and statistical learning theory, and have implications for real-world sensing experiments.

</details>


### [126] [Complementarity Test with Unmeasured, Permanently Inaccessible Path Markers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25382)
*Paul Gauthier,Sahil Patel,Sean Doan,Galan Moody*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用量子擦除器将路径信息的编码与其后续测量和因果可及性分离，关闭了以往互补性检验中遗留的坍缩-局域性漏洞。经由平直ΛCDM透射模型预测，73-82%的闲置光子将永久传播而不形成路径记录。未条件化的信号探测未显示统计显著干涉变化，归一化后其发射诱导条纹的95%置信上界为0.16（相对完全恢复）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往互补性检验中，路径标记都会被测量且保持因果可及，导致坍缩-局域性漏洞仍未关闭；作者旨在通过将路径信息的编码与后续测量及因果可及性隔离，来弥补这一漏洞。

Method: 采用量子擦除器方案，将闲置光子（作为纠缠信号光子在干涉仪中的唯一路径标记）发射到向外零测地线上，同时联合态仍处于两条干涉路径备选的相干叠加态。使用平直ΛCDM透射模型估计这些闲置光子不被吸收、散射或形成环境路径记录的概率。

Result: 模型预测73-82%的被发射闲置光子将永久传播而不被吸收、散射或形成环境路径记录；对这些幸存者而言，路径标记永久未被测量，且其传播路径排除了与干涉记录的后续因果接触。未条件化的信号探测未显示统计显著的发射诱导干涉变化，符合标准量子理论预测。归一化至模型幸存者后，任何发射诱导条纹的95%置信上界为0.16（相对完全恢复）。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在路径标记始终未被测量且与干涉记录不存在因果接触的条件下，未观察到统计显著的干涉变化，为关闭坍缩-局域性漏洞提供了实验支持，并给出0.16的严格置信上界。

Abstract: In prior complementarity tests, path markers were measured and remained causally accessible, leaving the collapse-locality loophole open. We address this loophole using a quantum eraser that isolates the encoding of path information in the joint quantum state from its later measurement and causal accessibility. We launch idler photons -- the sole path markers for entangled signal photons in an interferometer -- on outgoing null trajectories while the joint state remains in a coherent superposition of both interferometer path alternatives. Our flat $Λ$CDM transmission model predicts that 73-82% of launched idlers will propagate forever without absorption, scattering, or environmental path-record formation. For these modeled survivors, each path marker remains perpetually unmeasured, and propagation along its outgoing trajectory precludes later causal contact with its interferometric record. Unconditioned signal detections show no statistically significant launch-induced change in interference, as standard quantum theory predicts. Normalized to the modeled survivors, the 95% confidence upper bound on any launch-induced fringe is 0.16 of full restoration.

</details>


### [127] [Explicit channels with unbounded gains in classical communication using entangled inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26743)
*Hao-Chung Cheng,Peixue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造了一类显式有限维量子信道族，其乘积态编码可达经典通信速率趋于零，而仅在成对信道输入间使用纠缠时速率无界增长，实现了Hastings概率构造的去随机化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子信道容量理论中，纠缠对经典通信的作用是关键问题。已有Hastings的概率构造表明纠缠可带来无界优势，但缺乏显式构造，本文旨在给出确定的、可显式描述的信道族以证实该现象。

Method: 将确定性的qudit Clifford酉算符与二值测量及经典前馈相结合；通过对测量与前馈过程的精心设计，仅借助矩估计即可导出所需的单拷贝与双拷贝输出熵界，无需底层酉族具有强收敛性。

Result: 构造出显式有限维量子信道族：使用乘积态码字与集体译码时，最优经典通信速率趋于零；而当仅在成对信道输入间利用纠缠时，可达速率无界增长。

Conclusion: 该结果为Hastings的概率性构造成立提供了显式去随机化版本，表明纠缠相较乘积态编码可产生无界的经典通信速率增益。

Abstract: We construct an explicit family of finite-dimensional quantum channels for which the optimal classical communication rate achievable with product-state codewords and collective decoding tends to zero, while rates achievable using entanglement only within pairs of channel inputs grow without bound.
  Our construction combines deterministic qudit Clifford unitaries with a binary measurement and classical feedforward, providing a derandomization to Hastings' probabilistic construction. The key ingredient is a careful design of measurement and feedforward process that yields the required one-copy and two-copy output entropy bounds from moment estimates alone, without requiring strong convergence of the underlying unitary family.

</details>


### [128] [On the Complexity of Finding Decoherence Free Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26769)
*Evan Borras*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将"判定马尔可夫开放量子系统是否存在无退相干子空间"问题纳入计算复杂性框架，证明其在局域性k≥5时为QMA难，且k-Local Lindbladian问题对QMA完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无退相干子空间是开放量子系统的稳态结构，能保持量子相干性，在量子信息科技中有广泛应用；但其存在性判定的计算复杂度此前尚不清楚，需要明确其可解性边界。

Method: 在时间无关Lindblad主方程描述的马尔可夫开放量子系统框架下形式化无退相干子空间存在性问题；引入k-Local Lindbladian问题以刻画Lindbladian动力学下纯度衰减率计算的难度；将对Kitaev时钟哈密顿量构造推广到开放量子系统，把量子线路的执行编码进同时包含纯态与混合历史态的Lindbladian稳态子空间，该子空间依编码线路的输出而混合。

Result: 当局域性k≥5时，无退相干子空间存在性判定问题是QMA难的（且具有完美完备性），k-Local Lindbladian问题则是QMA完全的。

Conclusion: 判定一般马尔可夫开放量子系统是否具有无退相干子空间即使对量子计算而言也是难解的，表明该问题具有内在的复杂性障碍。

Abstract: Decoherence free subspaces are a steady-state structure of the open quantum system which preserves quantum coherence between the states lying with in it and thus has found a variety of applications throughout quantum information science and technology. In this paper we study the computational complexity of deciding whether an open quantum system admits a decoherence free subspace or not. More specifically we study this problem with in the context of Markovian open quantum systems, governed by the time-independent Lindblad master equation. Along the way we introduce the $k$-Local Lindbladian problem, which captures the difficulty of computing purity decay rates under Lindbladian dynamics. We show that both problems are hard for the complexity class Quantum Merlin Arthur (QMA) when the locality $k \geq 5$, with the first under perfect completeness and the second being complete for QMA. Our hardness construction generalizes Kitaev's clock Hamiltonian construction to the open quantum system setting by encoding the execution of a quantum circuit into the steady subspace of a Lindbladian containing both pure and mixed history states. This subspace is then mixed depending on the output of the encoded circuit. Our results suggest that deciding whether a generic Markovian open quantum system admits a decoherence free subspace is intractable even for quantum computation.

</details>


### [129] [A scalable entanglement distribution framework for hybrid quantum networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25571)
*Yaqi Zhao,Kan He,Jinchuan Hou,Yongtao Zhang,Nicolò Lo Piparo,Xiangyi Meng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的混合纠缠分发方案，通过将离散-连续变量（DV-CV）接口处的混合纠缠交换与浓缩映射为统一的串并联图规则，实现了跨异构硬件平台的纠缠分发，并在串并联拓扑中获得优于传统点对点方案的端到端纠缠性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子网络的实现依赖于跨异构硬件平台的高效纠缠分发。然而传统量子网络框架多局限于单一编码方案（仅依赖离散变量DV或连续变量CV），这种分割式架构限制了大规模系统的容量与互操作性。因此需要一种能够桥接DV与CV域的混合纠缠分发方案。

Method: 将DV-CV接口处的混合纠缠交换（entanglement swapping）与纠缠浓缩（concentration）映射到一套统一的串联与并联图规则上，构建可扩展的混合纠缠分发方案。

Result: 该混合方案相较传统点对点生成最大纠缠态的方式展现出潜在性能优势，在串并联拓扑结构中实现了增强的端到端纠缠。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计资源高效、可解释的未来量子互联网架构提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The realization of a scalable quantum network (QN) hinges on the efficient distribution of entanglement across heterogeneous hardware platforms. While traditional QN frameworks are often restricted to single-encoding schemes, relying exclusively on either discrete (DV) or continuous (CV) variables, such segregated architectures limit the capacity and interoperability of large-scale systems. Here, we propose a scalable hybrid entanglement distribution scheme that bridges these domains by mapping hybrid entanglement swapping and concentration at DV-CV interfaces onto a unified set of series and parallel graph rules. We demonstrate that our hybrid approach exhibits a potential performance advantage over traditional point-to-point generation of maximally entangled states, achieving enhanced end-to-end entanglement within series-parallel topologies. These results provide a robust theoretical foundation for designing resource-efficient, interpretable architectures for the future quantum internet.

</details>


### [130] [Efficient Ion-Photon Quantum Interface with Long-Working-Distance Exceeding 26 mm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25598)
*Jungwoo Choi,Inhyuk Oh,Chanyang Im,Gibeom Son,Junki Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文设计并表征了一种用于离子阱量子网络的离子-光子接口，工作距离达26.15 mm，数值孔径为0.5，由六片商用球面透镜优化间距构成，整体光纤耦合光子探测效率为(1.29±0.09)%，可长时间稳定运行并保持非经典光子统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子阱量子网络依赖高效的离子-光子接口，而基于物镜的自由空间收集系统需在高光子收集效率与足够工作距离之间取得平衡，以便与离子阱硬件实际集成。

Method: 设计了一种长工作距离（26.15 mm）、高数值孔径（0.5）的离子-光子接口，核心是由六片商用球面透镜按优化间距组成的光子收集物镜，并对其进行表征，包括整体光纤耦合光子探测效率测量、长时间光纤耦合稳定性测试以及光子相关测量。

Result: 接口实现了从离子到探测器(1.29±0.09)%的整体光纤耦合光子探测效率；在无主动重对准的情况下数小时内保持光纤耦合；光子相关测量显示光纤耦合荧光存在明显的反聚束，证实非经典光子统计得以保持。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种适用于离子阱量子网络实验的长工作距离光子收集接口的实用实现方案。

Abstract: Trapped-ion quantum networks rely on efficient ion-photon interfaces, while objective-based free-space collection systems must balance high photon collection with sufficient working distance for practical integration with ion-trap hardware. Here, we design and characterize an ion-photon interface with a long working distance of 26.15 mm and a high numerical aperture of 0.5. The core of the interface is a photon-collection objective consisting of six commercial spherical lenses with optimized spacing. The interface achieved an overall fiber-coupled photon-detection efficiency of (1.29$\pm$0.09)% from the ion to the detector and maintains fiber coupling over several hours without active realignment, supporting stable operation of the ion-to-fiber interface. Photon-correlation measurements show clear antibunching in the fiber-coupled fluorescence, confirming the preservation of non-classical photon statistics. These results demonstrate a practical implementation of a long-working-distance photon collection interface for trapped-ion quantum network experiments.

</details>


### [131] [When Quantum Meets AI: Quantum Methods for Machine Learning and Machine Learning Methods for Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25641)
*Tak Hur*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文从"量子机器学习"与"机器学习用于量子系统"两个方向交叉研究：前者提出神经量子嵌入、基于Hilbert-Schmidt内积的DQC1训练目标及基于间隔的泛化分析；后者提出基于Mamba的表面码神经解码器，并将随机重构解释为切空间岭回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算与人工智能的交叉存在两类核心问题：一是如何借助量子方法提升机器学习模型在噪声硬件上的表示与泛化能力；二是如何用机器学习方法提升量子系统（如量子纠错解码、神经量子态）的效率与精度。论文旨在从学习表示、统计控制与硬件约束三方面刻画二者的相互作用。

Method: 量子机器学习方向：(1) Neural Quantum Embedding，学习增大嵌入类系综间迹距离的数据表示，降低与嵌入相关的经验风险上界；(2) 基于Hilbert-Schmidt内积的训练目标，将该方法扩展到单量子比特确定性量子计算(DQC1)，并在NMR量子处理器上验证；(3) 基于间隔(margin)的泛化分析，将量子神经网络性能与量子态区分联系起来。量子系统方向：(4) 基于Mamba的表面码神经解码器，与复现的Transformer基线对比；(5) 将随机重构(SR)解释为切空间岭回归，提出多移位随机重构。

Result: (1) 所提嵌入在噪声量子硬件上提升分类性能；(2) 训练目标在DQC1与NMR处理器上得到验证；(3) 在所研究基准中，间隔分布比参数量指标更可靠地预测泛化；(4) Mamba解码器在内存实验中与Transformer基线相当，推理开销随码距的缩放由四次降为二次，并在显式解码器诱导噪声模型下取得更低逻辑错误率与更高有效阈值；(5) 多移位SR相对固定移位SR降低检查点局部验证残差与更新方差，但计算开销增加。

Conclusion: 通过学习表示、统计控制与硬件约束三条线索，论文展示了量子计算与机器学习之间的双向交换机制，并在分类、解码与神经量子态优化任务上验证了相应方法的有效性。

Abstract: This thesis studies the intersection of quantum computing and artificial intelligence in two directions: quantum methods for machine learning and machine learning methods for quantum systems. For quantum machine learning, Neural Quantum Embedding learns data representations that increase the trace distance between embedded class ensembles, lowering an embedding-dependent bound on empirical risk and improving classification on noisy quantum hardware. A training objective based on the Hilbert-Schmidt inner product extends this approach to deterministic quantum computation with one qubit (DQC1) and is demonstrated on an NMR quantum processor. A margin-based generalization analysis then connects quantum neural network performance to quantum state discrimination. In the studied benchmarks, margin distributions predict generalization more reliably than parameter-count metrics. For quantum systems, a Mamba-based neural decoder for surface codes matches a reproduced Transformer baseline in memory experiments while reducing inference-cost scaling from quartic to quadratic in code distance. Under an explicit decoder-induced-noise model, it achieves lower logical error rates and a higher effective threshold. For neural quantum states, stochastic reconfiguration is interpreted as tangent-space ridge regression, with its diagonal shift controlling the bias-variance trade-off under finite Monte Carlo sampling. Multi-shift stochastic reconfiguration reduces checkpoint-local validation residuals and update variance relative to fixed-shift SR, at additional computational cost. Together, these contributions show how learned representations, statistical control, and hardware constraints shape the exchange between quantum computing and machine learning.

</details>


### [132] [Schrödingerization for quantum linear systems problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25660)
*Long Zhang,Yin Yang,Yue Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出二维高维量子线性系统问题的Schrödingerization算法，用Duhamel原理将时间积分嵌入非齐次对流方程，结合Fourier投影与第二次Schrödinger化，免除独立LCU步骤，达到O(κ_A log(1/ε))查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LC-Schrödingerization方法将解表示为齐次对流响应的时间积分，并通过演化时间上的线性酉组合（LCU）实现该积分，需独立的LCU步骤且演化时间依赖目标精度。作者希望简化实现、消除对精度的时间依赖并减少查询开销，同时避免变量时间振幅放大。

Method: 利用Duhamel原理把积分改写为带零初值的非齐次对流方程；在对流变量上做Fourier投影，并在第二个辅助变量上做Schrödingerization，从而无需单独LCU步骤直接实现解。联合选择核函数与恢复过程，使演化时间与精度无关、L^2核归一化一致有界，并在固定区间上精确恢复。建立周期化与离散化误差界，分析区间恢复概率，并配合块预处理。

Result: 在精确oracle访问下，给定有效的常数因子解范数估计，算法对每个原始输入oracle使用O(κ_A log(1/ε))次查询，成功概率为常数，ℓ²态误差不超过ε；矩阵查询界在范数界紧时与标准最坏情形缩放匹配。UnitaryLab在正定与不定系统上的模拟验证了解精度及区间恢复带来的概率增益。

Conclusion: 该Schrödingerization框架通过Duhamel原理、Fourier投影与双辅助变量Schrödinger化，实现了精度无关演化时间与线性条件数依赖的量子线性求解器，无需变量时间振幅放大，查询复杂度达到O(κ_A log(1/ε))，数值模拟支持其正确性与优势。

Abstract: We develop a Schrödingerization algorithm for quantum linear systems problems in two higher dimensions. The earlier LC-Schrödingerization approach represents the solution as a time integral of a homogeneous convection response and implements this integral by a linear combination of unitaries (LCU) over evolution times. We instead use Duhamel's principle to incorporate the integral into an inhomogeneous convection equation with zero initial data. Fourier projection in the convection variable and Schrödingerization in a second auxiliary variable then realize the solution without the separate LCU step. A joint choice of the kernel and recovery procedure gives an evolution time independent of the target accuracy, a uniformly bounded $L^2$ kernel normalization, and accurate recovery on a fixed interval. We establish the periodization and discretization bounds and analyze the interval recovery probability. With block preconditioning, we retain a single logarithmic precision factor and obtain linear condition-number dependence without variable-time amplitude amplification. Under exact oracle access and given a valid constant-factor solution-norm estimate, the algorithm uses $\mathcal O(κ_A\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries to each original input oracle, with constant success probability and $\ell^2$ state error at most $\varepsilon$. The matrix-query bound matches the standard worst-case scaling when the supplied norm bounds are tight. UnitaryLab simulations on positive-definite and indefinite systems demonstrate the solution accuracy and probability gain from interval recovery.

</details>


### [133] [Post-selected enhancement of phase sensitivity in a two-path interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25673)
*Ryuya Fukuda,Katsuki Tanaka,Masataka Iinuma,Holger F. Hofmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将后选择计量应用于双路径干涉仪，通过把相位灵敏度转移至光子圆偏振，激活低概率输出端口集中的Fisher信息，获得的相位斜率相当于四光子NOON态，从而直接比较单光子后选择与多光子干涉的相位灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单光子干涉的相位灵敏度受标准量子极限限制。后选择计量可将全部Fisher信息集中于低概率的后选择结果中，从而突破该极限。本文旨在将这一原理应用于标准双路径干涉仪，以验证其提升相位灵敏度的可行性。

Method: 在标准双路径干涉仪中采用后选择方案，通过将相位敏感性转移到光子的圆偏振上，激活原本位于低概率输出端口的Fisher信息，并测量后选择干涉条纹的相位斜率。

Result: 所获得的后选择干涉条纹相位斜率相当于四光子NOON态的相位斜率，表明在相位灵敏度上达到了与四光子NOON态相当的效果。

Conclusion: 后选择计量可在单光子干涉中实现与四光子NOON态相当的相位灵敏度，从而直接比较了多光子干涉与单光子后选择两种方案的性能，证明了后选择方法作为资源节约型高精度相位测量手段的有效性。

Abstract: Post-selected metrology can exceed the standard quantum limit of single photon interference by concentrating all of the available Fisher information in a subset of post-selected outcomes. Here we apply this principle to a standard two-path interferometer, activating the Fisher information concentrated in the low probability output port by transferring the phase sensitivity to the circular polarizations of the photon. We obtain a phase gradient of the post-selected interference fringe that corresponds to that of a four-photon NOON state, providing a direct comparison of the performance of multi-photon interference with single-photon post-selection.

</details>


### [134] [$\mathsf{BQP} \subseteq \mathsf{IP}$ Does Not Relativize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25680)
*Adam Bouland,Andrew Huang,Anand Natarajan,Itay Shalit,Avishay Tal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一个相对化图灵机模型下的oracle，使得 $\mathsf{BQP} \not\subseteq \mathsf{IP}$，首次实现了 $\mathsf{IP}$ 与 $\mathsf{MIP}$ 的oracle分离，其核心是基于Forrelation问题的不可交互证明性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子复杂性理论中长期存在一个悬而未决的问题：$\mathsf{BQP}$ 是否包含于 $\mathsf{IP}$。若包含，则意味着量子计算可被经典交互式协议（甚至高效地）验证，这将深刻影响量子验证的理论基础。本文旨在给出否定性证据，并解释为何推动高效、无条件可靠的经典量子验证如此困难。

Method: 论文构造了一个oracle，通过证明Forrelation问题（判断布尔函数 $f$ 是否与其 Fourier 谱相关的 $g$ 相关）虽然在量子查询模型下可高效求解，却不存在多项式通信量且验证者仅用多项式查询的经典交互式协议。证明技术包含：(i) 将 Avg-Max 电路（刻画oracular情形下交互式证明能力）用具有小的一阶、二阶导数的凸函数逼近的结构性结果；(ii) 一种新颖分析，证明 Aaronson 和 Ambainis 提出的 Forrelation 分布能欺骗此类函数。

Result: 存在一个oracle，使得 $\mathsf{BQP} \not\subseteq \mathsf{IP}$。结合 Aaronson 等人的近期工作，该结果首次给出了 $\mathsf{IP}$ 与 $\mathsf{MIP}$ 之间的oracle分离，回答了一个可追溯至 Fortnow 博士论文的问题。此外，该结果表明任何针对 $\mathsf{BQP}$ 的prover-efficient经典交互式协议必须依赖非相对化技术。

Conclusion: 本文在相对化模型中确立了 $\mathsf{BQP} \not\subseteq \mathsf{IP}$ 以及 $\mathsf{IP} \neq \mathsf{MIP}$（oracle分离），表明经典交互式证明无法高效验证量子计算能力。这为双重高效、无条件可靠的经典量子验证缺乏进展提供了一种部分解释，并凸显了非相对化方法的重要性。

Abstract: We construct an oracle relative to which $\mathsf{BQP} \not\subseteq \mathsf{IP}$, resolving a long-standing open question in quantum complexity theory. Together with recent work due to Aaronson et al., our work also gives the first oracle separation between $\mathsf{IP}$ and $\mathsf{MIP}$, answering a question dating back to Fortnow's thesis. Our separation is based on the Forrelation problem, where given Boolean functions $f$ and $g$, the goal is to determine if $f$ is correlated with the Fourier spectrum of $g$. While this task is solvable by a query-efficient quantum algorithm, we show that it admits no classical interactive protocol with polynomial communication and a polynomial-query verifier.
  Our proof is based on (i) a new structural result showing how to approximate Avg-Max circuits (which are well-known to capture the power of interactive proofs in the oracular setting) by convex functions with small first and second derivatives and (ii) a novel analysis establishing that the Forrelation distribution suggested by Aaronson and Ambainis fools such functions.
  Our results imply that any prover-efficient classical interactive protocol for $\mathsf{BQP}$ must rely on non-relativizing techniques. This might serve as a partial explanation for the lack of progress towards doubly-efficient, unconditionally sound classical verification of quantum computation.

</details>


### [135] [Post-Selection-Based Stochastic Quantum Battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25699)
*Aman Verma,Roson Nongthombam,Amarendra K. Sarma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了基于连续测量和后选择非厄米量子电池（三能级系统）的充电动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索连续测量与后选择机制如何影响量子电池的充电性能，并揭示测量轴与驱动协议之间的相互作用。

Method: 通过后选择无量子跳跃（|1⟩→|0⟩）的轨迹，将三能级系统动力学有效限制在二能级流形；在σ_x和σ_y驱动协议下分析充电动力学，考察失谐、相干驱动、非线性和测量反作用的影响，并分析量子相干性的产生与动力学。

Result: 阐明了失谐、相干驱动、非线性和测量反作用对充电速率、遍历能（ergotropy）和量子相干性的作用，并展示其相较于通用二能级系统及Lindblad动力学的优势。

Conclusion: 揭示了测量轴与驱动协议对非厄米、高度可调可控量子电池充电性能的协同调控机制。

Abstract: Quantum batteries are quantum systems that store useful energy, which can subsequently be extracted to perform work. In this work, we investigate the charging dynamics under continuous measurements and post-selection-based non-Hermitian quantum battery realized by a three-level system. By post-selecting the trajectories with no quantum jump $|1\rangle \rightarrow |0\rangle$, the dynamics is effectively confined to a two-level manifold. We analyze the resulting charging dynamics under both $σ_x$- and $σ_y$-driving protocols and investigate the roles of detuning, coherent driving, non-linearity, and measurement backaction. The generation and dynamics of quantum coherence, which plays a pivotal role in quantum battery, are also analyzed under post-selection. The advantage and comparison of our results over those of a generic two-level system and with Lindblad dynamics are also highlighted. Our work explores the interplay between the measurement axis and the driving protocols that govern the charging rate, ergotropy, and quantum coherence of the non-Hermitian, highly tunable, and controllable quantum battery.

</details>


### [136] [Enhanced Atomic Magnetometry with a Pinched Spin State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25714)
*Tingxuan Guo,Xiangyu Li,Yang Li,Kaifeng Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用m=0附近对称的射频谱，实现压缩态(|F,0>)横向磁场响应增强，在87Rb中观测到最高2.7倍增强，并在紧凑抗弛豫镀膜多通池中实现双轴零场磁强计，室温下3–100 Hz灵敏度达13–23 fT/√Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子自旋传感器灵敏度受探针态量子Fisher信息(QFI)的根本限制；压缩态|F,0>横向场单粒子QFI比拉伸态|F,F>高(F+1)倍，理论上对应√(F+1)的场不确定度改善，但该优势此前难以实际获取。

Method: 通过工程设计使射频传感谱关于m=0对称，从而获取压缩态的QFI优势；在87Rb (F=2)体系中进行验证，并采用紧凑的0.8 cm^3抗弛豫镀膜多通池构建单光束、双轴、零场磁强计。

Result: 理论预测(F+1)倍磁场响应增强；在87Rb (F=2)中实测响应增强最高达2.7倍；磁强计在室温下3–100 Hz频段灵敏度达13–23 fT/√Hz，体积仅0.8 cm^3。

Conclusion: 该方案可实际获取压缩态的QFI优势，实现高响应增强与紧凑单光束双轴零场磁强计，在室温下达到fT/√Hz量级灵敏度。

Abstract: The sensitivity of an atomic spin sensor is fundamentally constrained by the quantum Fisher information (QFI) of the probe state. The pinched state, $|{F,0}\rangle$, has an $(F+1)$-fold larger single-particle QFI for transverse-field sensing than the stretched state, $|{F,F}\rangle$, corresponding to a predicted $\sqrt{F+1}$ reduction in the spin-projection-noise-limited field uncertainty. We show that this advantage can be accessed in an rf sensing scheme by engineering a spectrum symmetric about $m=0$, yielding an ideal $(F+1)$-fold enhancement of magnetic-field response. For $^{87}\mathrm{Rb}$ with $F=2$, we observe up to 2.7-fold response enhancement. Implementing this approach in a compact $0.8~\mathrm{cm}^3$ anti-relaxation-coated multipass cell, we realize a single-beam, dual-axis, zero-field magnetometer, achieving $13$--$23$~$\mathrm{fT}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ over $3$--$100$~Hz at room temperature.

</details>


### [137] [Photon-Statistics Control of Strong-Field Rydberg-State Excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25753)
*Tao Jiang,Jinlei Liu,Haopu Dou,Lingyi Zhao,Peng Xiang,Liangjun Peng,Renyu Wang,Jing Zhao,Xiaowei Wang,Yue Lang,Zengxiu Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示光子统计量是调控强场束缚态形成的新维度和机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强场物理中束缚态（尤其Rydberg态）的形成机制已有较成熟描述，但光子统计量作为量子光学自由度如何影响多光子激发、隧穿回俘获和末态对称性尚不明确，需要建立量子光学与强场过程的统一描述。

Method: 将量子光学相空间表述（如光子数分布/准概率分布刻画的光场统计）与含时薛定谔方程（TDSE）数值计算相耦合，模拟不同光子统计场下的强场动力学；并提出量子统计回俘获模型，以系综诱导的相对路径相位色散解释回俘获抑制。

Result: 场涨落通过展宽AC-Stark位移共振促进多光子Rydberg激发；但抑制隧穿介导的回俘获，即使电离增强；回俘获抑制归因于系综诱导的相对路径相位色散；光子统计量还改变Rydberg流形的宇称，将场统计与回俘获相干性及末态对称性相联系。

Conclusion: 光子统计量构成强场束缚态形成的可控新维度，并建立场统计—回俘获相干性—末态对称性的关联。

Abstract: Photon statistics provides a new control dimension for strong-field bound-state formation. Using a quantum-optical phase-space formulation coupled to time-dependent Schrödinger-equation calculations, we show that field fluctuations enhance multiphoton Rydberg excitation by broadening AC-Stark-shifted resonances, but suppress tunneling-mediated recapture even as ionization increases. A quantum-statistical recapture model attributes the latter to ensemble-induced dispersion of relative path phases. Photon statistics also reshapes Rydberg-manifold parity, linking field statistics to recapture coherence and final-state symmetry.

</details>


### [138] [An asymmetric atom-photon architecture for device-independent quantum key distribution over 25\,km](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25795)
*Jonas Meiers,Christian Haen,Max Bergerhoff,Pascal Baumgart,Tobias Bauer,Christoph Becher,Jürgen Eschner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了基于事件就绪的非对称原子-光子架构的器件无关量子密钥分发（DIQKD）实验。在25 km卷绕电信光纤上，通过双重量子频率转换与主动偏振稳定，实现了单个囚禁40Ca+离子与单光子间的纠缠分发，获得CHSH参数|S|=2.75，超过正密钥率阈值2.362。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DIQKD无需信任测量设备即可保证安全性，但将其扩展到光纤网络需同时满足高质量纠缠、可靠预示与忠实光子传输三项要求，本文正是为应对这些协同挑战而设计实验。

Method: 采用事件就绪的非对称原子-光子架构：一端为单个囚禁40Ca+离子，另一端为在远端站测量的单光子；通过双重量子频率转换与主动偏振稳定，在25 km卷绕电信光纤全链路上保持原子-光子量子关联，并进行CHSH不等式测量。

Result: 测得CHSH参数|S|=2.75（+0.16/-0.15），超过所用DIQKD模型下正渐近密钥率所需阈值|S|=2.362；在同一渐近模型下，10908个探测到的Bell态中保守估计可提取69个密钥比特。

Conclusion: 该架构验证了在光纤链路中实现DIQKD的可行性，为异构、可兼容量子中继的量子网络中的DIQKD提供了技术路线。

Abstract: Device-independent quantum key distribution (DIQKD) can guarantee security without trusting the internal workings of the measurement devices, but extending it to fiber networks demands high-quality entanglement, reliable heralding, and faithful photon transmission simultaneously. Here, we address these requirements in an event-ready asymmetric atom-photon architecture implemented over $25\,\mathrm{km}$ of spooled telecom fiber. A single trapped $^{40}\mathrm{Ca}^{+}$ ion forms one party of the protocol while a single transmitted photon measured at the remote station forms the other. Double quantum frequency conversion and active polarization stabilization preserve the atom-photon quantum correlation across the full link. We obtain a Clauser-Horne-Shimony-Holt parameter of $|S|=2.75^{+0.16}_{-0.15}$, exceeding the threshold $|S|=2.362$ required for a positive asymptotic secret-key fraction under the DIQKD model used. Within the same asymptotic model, this corresponds to a conservative estimate of 69 secret-key bits out of 10908 detected Bell states. The demonstrated architecture establishes a route towards DIQKD in heterogeneous, repeater-compatible quantum networks.

</details>


### [139] [Exponential Quantum Advantage in Testing Fourier Dimensionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25816)
*Kenny Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究布尔函数傅里叶维度的性质测试问题：判定函数傅里叶维度是否至多为k，还是ε-远离该性质。作者给出了Θ(k)的量子测试器，并证明经典测试器的上界为Õ(2^{k/2}/ε)，从而确立了该问题的指数级量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布尔函数的傅里叶维度是一个重要的结构性质。此前已知该问题的经典下界为Ω(2^{k/2})，但经典上界与之存在差距，且量子查询复杂度尚不清楚。论文旨在确定该性质测试问题的量子与经典复杂度，并探究是否存在量子优势。

Method: 针对傅里叶维度至多k的性质测试任务，作者设计了查询复杂度为Θ(k)的量子性质测试算法；同时构造了查询复杂度为Õ(2^{k/2}/ε)的经典性质测试算法，用于改进经典上界。

Result: 量子性质测试器的复杂度为Θ(k)；经典测试器的复杂度为Õ(2^{k/2}/ε)。结合已有的经典下界Ω(2^{k/2})，表明该经典下界基本紧致，同时量子与经典复杂度之间呈指数级差距。

Conclusion: 傅里叶维度性质测试问题存在指数级量子优势：量子测试器仅需Θ(k)次查询，而经典测试器需要约2^{k/2}次。该结果同时闭合了经典复杂度的上下界。

Abstract: A boolean function $f$ has Fourier dimension $k$ if its nonzero Fourier coefficients span a subspace of dimension $k$. We consider the property testing task of determining whether a function has Fourier dimension at most $k$, or is $ε$-far from being so. We show that there is a $Θ(k)$ quantum property tester for this problem. With Gopalan \etal's classical lower bound of $Ω(2^{k/2})$ for this task, this demonstrates an exponential quantum advantage for this task. We complement this result with a $\tilde{O}(2^{k/2}/ε)$ classical tester, improving the best known upper bound and thus showing the prior lower bound is essentially tight.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum survival in hybrid pursuit dynamics - a lion chasing a lamb problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25844)
*P. Held,İ. Yalçınkaya,F. Pegoraro,J. Lammers,F. Schlue,K. Jiráková,S. Barkhofen,B. Brecht,M. Štefaňák,V. Potoček,A. Gábris,I. Jex,C. Silberhorn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用光子时间复用量子行走平台，研究量子行走者（羊羔）与经典追捕者（狮子）构成的混合量子-经典追捕问题，发现提高经典追捕者迁移率反而在较宽参数范围内提升量子行走者存活率，展示了经典随机控制对量子特性的增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量与经典随机性通常会破坏相干性，但若能精确控制子系统被监测的位置与时机，则可能将测量转化为可调控的资源。作者希望研究测量、经典随机控制与量子相干性之间的相互作用，并检验经典随机性是否可能反而助力而非抑制量子特性。

Method: 构建光子时间复用量子行走系统，具备快速可重构能力以实现精确位置测量。设计混合量子-经典追捕模型：量子行走者（lamb）在一维线上演化，经典追捕者（lion）执行惰性随机行走；每条狮子轨迹驱动羊羔一个不同的相干且范数衰减的演化，通过狮子位置处的测量实现"捕获"，再对所有记录轨迹取平均以合成退相干过程。

Result: 反直觉地发现，提高狮子迁移率并不会单调压制羊羔存活率；在较宽的跳跃概率范围内，羊羔面对移动捕食者的存活率反而高于面对静止捕食者的情况。实验直接观测到这一局部增强现象。

Conclusion: 该工作直接观测到一种显著的量子特性，该特性因经典随机控制的介入而得到增强而非被抑制，表明精确控制的测量与经典随机性可作为调控量子相干性的资源，为混合量子-经典系统中的量子特性调控提供了新视角。

Abstract: Measurement and classical randomness usually degrade coherence; controlling where and when a subsystem is monitored can instead make them a tunable resource. We introduce a photonic time-multiplexed quantum walk whose fast reconfigurability enables precise position measurements, allowing us to study this interplay in a hybrid quantum-classical pursuit problem: A quantum walker (lamb) evolves on a line while a classical pursuer (lion) performs a lazy random walk. Each lion trajectory drives a distinct coherent, norm-reducing evolution of the lamb, with capture implemented by measurements at the lion's positions; averaging over the recorded trajectories then synthesizes a decoherent process. Counterintuitively, increasing the lion's mobility does not monotonically suppress survival: over a broad range of hopping probabilities, the lamb survives better than against an immobile predator. Directly observing this local enhancement, we demonstrate a manifestly quantum feature aided rather than hindered by classical stochastic control.

</details>


### [141] [Universal Fidelity Law for Linear-Optical Entangling Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25861)
*Haim Nakav,Ofer Firstenberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为基于干涉的线性光学纠缠门推导出一个通用保真度定律，用Hong-Ou-Mandel可见度和二阶相干度两个标准光源指标表达，并在主动同步CNOT门实验及已发表数据上无自由参数验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不完美光子源会降低基于干涉的线性光学纠缠门性能，但缺乏一个统一、可预测的评估框架，难以跨平台比较光源质量对门性能的影响。

Method: 将多光子分量建模为噪声光子与信号光子之间的任意重叠，推导门的保真度定律；在主动同步CNOT门实验上进行测试，并与不同物理平台的已发表结果对比，全程无自由参数。

Result: 不同信号-噪声重叠会带来不同的误差结构（对应不同物理平台），但所有情况都遵循同一保真度定律；该定律在自研实验与文献数据上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 该框架将光源表征转化为光子量子计算的统一基准，可用于跨平台评估与优化光子源及纠缠门性能。

Abstract: Imperfect photon sources degrade the performance of interference-based linear-optical entangling gates. We derive a universal fidelity law for these gates in terms of two standard source metrics: Hong-Ou-Mandel visibility and second-order coherence. We model the multiphoton component as an arbitrary overlap between noise and signal photons. While different values of the signal-noise overlap produce different error structures, associated with different physical platforms, all result in the same fidelity law. We test the model on our actively synchronized CNOT-gate experiment and against published results across different platforms, with no free parameters. This framework turns source characterization into a unified benchmark for photonic quantum computing.

</details>


### [142] [On the Computational Complexity of Guided Berry Phase Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25929)
*Gabriel Waite*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在给定引导态经典描述且承诺其与基态有重叠的前提下，判定参数化2-local量子比特哈密顿量的Berry相位是BQP完全的，并给出1-local的高效可计算性，从而确立复杂度相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定Berry相位问题的计算复杂度边界：明确何种哈密顿量族下Berry相位计算困难或可行，刻画1-local与2-local之间的复杂度转变。

Method: 将通常用于局部哈密顿量微扰gadget归约的Schrieffer–Wolff变换推广至参数化哈密顿量族；基于Oliveira–Terhal及Schuch–Verstraete的微扰gadget归约框架，适配参数化相互作用并控制模拟误差界，构建参数化模拟器哈密顿量以逼近目标族的Berry相位。

Result: 判定参数化2-local量子比特哈密顿量的Berry相位为BQP完全；结论可扩展至加权Heisenberg相互作用及2D方格/三角晶格几何；并显式证明1-local哈密顿量族的Berry相位可以逆多项式精度高效计算。

Conclusion: Berry相位的计算复杂度在1-local与2-local哈密顿量族之间存在相变：1-local可高效计算，而2-local在引导态承诺下BQP完全。

Abstract: We prove that deciding the Berry phase for parameterised 2-local qubit Hamiltonians is BQP-complete when presented with a classical description of a guiding state, promised to overlap with the ground state of the system. Our results extend to systems with weighted Heisenberg interactions and when restricted to a 2D square or triangular lattice geometry. The techniques we develop leverage the Schrieffer--Wolff transformation, typically used in the construction of perturbative gadget reductions for local Hamiltonian problems, extending it to parameterised families of Hamiltonians. We demonstrate that there exists a choice of parameterised simulator Hamiltonians whose Berry phase well-approximates that of a parameterised target family. Using the perturbative gadget reduction framework of Oliveira and Terhal and of Schuch and Verstraete, we adapt the arguments to parameterised interactions and demonstrate the error bounds in the resulting simulation can be controlled. Additionally, we provide an explicit proof that families of 1-local Hamiltonians have a Berry phase that can be efficiently computed to inverse-polynomial precision. This establishes a complexity transition between 1-local and 2-local Hamiltonian families.

</details>


### [143] [Spin-Orbit-Mediated Magnetization Transfer in the Fermi-Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25936)
*Jialiang Tang,Ruoqian Xu,Gloria Platero,Yue Ban,Xi Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在半填充Fermi-Hubbard模型中，瞬态自旋轨道耦合(SOC)可打破总磁化守恒约束，将对称性保护的能级交叉转化为避免交叉，从而打开有限能隙通道，实现磁化扇区转移；结合GRAPE与变分量子电路可进一步将转移加速至绝热时标以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无SOC时，总磁化守恒将系统动力学限制在初始磁化扇区内，使反铁磁关联态在Zeeman斜坡下无法进入极化铁磁扇区；本文旨在寻找突破这一对称性施加的动力学约束的可控机制。

Method: 在Fermi-Hubbard模型（一维与二维晶格）中引入瞬态SOC，耦合原本不相连的磁化扇区；采用梯度上升脉冲工程(GRAPE)与变分量子电路进行最优控制，以加速磁化扇区转移。

Result: 瞬态SOC成功将对称性保护的能级交叉转变为避免交叉，形成有限能隙的磁态转换通道；GRAPE和变分量子电路两种方法均在一维和二维Fermi-Hubbard晶格中实现了快速磁化转移，且时标短于绝热时间。

Conclusion: 瞬态SOC可作为克服对称性施加的动力学约束的可控催化剂，而最优控制手段决定了磁化扇区转移所需的时标。

Abstract: We demonstrate spin--orbit-coupling (SOC) catalysis of magnetization-sector transfer in the half-filled Fermi--Hubbard model. Without SOC, conservation of total magnetization confines the dynamics to the initial sector, preventing an antiferromagnetically correlated state from reaching the polarized ferromagnetic sector under a Zeeman ramp. A transient SOC control lifts this obstruction by coupling otherwise disconnected magnetization sectors and converting symmetry-protected crossings into avoided crossings, thereby opening a finite-gap pathway for magnetic-state conversion. We further accelerate the SOC-enabled dynamics beyond the adiabatic timescale using gradient-ascent pulse engineering (GRAPE) and a variational quantum circuit. Both approaches achieve rapid magnetization transfer in one- and two-dimensional Fermi--Hubbard lattices. Our results establish transient SOC as a controllable catalyst for overcoming symmetry-imposed dynamical constraints, while optimal control sets the timescale for the resulting magnetization-sector transfer.

</details>


### [144] [Bridge of $Ψ$'s: Quantum Circuit Optimization with Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25947)
*Lino S. Hofstetter,Lia Yeh,Prakash Murali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于薛定谔桥的生成模型BOPS，直接从示例中学习量子电路优化变换，在Clifford+T电路上实现门数与深度约2.46倍与2.45倍的几何平均缩减，超越九个基线优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子电路优化依赖固定重写规则库或刚性代数流程，泛化性与优化空间受限。作者希望验证生成模型能否直接从示例中学习"等价电路到更优电路"的变换，从而为量子编译栈引入可学习的优化范式。

Method: 提出BOPS（Bridge of Ψ's），一种基于薛定谔桥的生成模型，配备定制去噪器架构，学习从源电路到等价优化电路的映射。训练数据通过反向应用重写规则构造：对已知低成本目标逆向生成输入，使其对现有优化器而言较难，同时保证每个输入存在已知的更低成本目标。

Result: 在留出的8量子比特×64深度Clifford+T电路上，BOPS在几何平均意义下将门数缩减约2.46倍、深度缩减约2.45倍，优于全部九个基线优化器。这是首个桥接量子电路与前沿机器学习方法的生成模型。

Conclusion: 结果表明生成式学习可直接用于量子电路等价优化，无需依赖固定规则库，为量子编译栈沿多维度开展学习式优化开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Quantum circuit optimization replaces a circuit with an equivalent one of fewer gates and lower depth, reducing execution cost and error rate. We ask whether a generative model can learn this transformation directly from examples, rather than selecting from a fixed rewrite library or rigid algebraic routines. We present Bridge of $Ψ$'s (BOPS), a generative model based on Schrödinger bridges, using a custom denoiser architecture, that learns a transformation from a source circuit into an equivalent optimized circuit. We train it on data constructed to be hard for existing optimizers, by applying rewrite rules backwards so that each input has a known lower-cost target. On held-out 8 qubits $\times$ 64 depth Clifford+$T$ circuits, BOPS reduces gate count by $2.46\times$ and depth by $2.45\times$ in geometric mean, outperforming all nine baseline optimizers. This constitutes the first generative model bridging quantum circuits and frontier machine learning methods, opening up the quantum compilation stack to learned optimization along multiple axes.

</details>


### [145] [Maximum-Likelihood Amplitude Estimation for Quantum Monte Carlo Integration on Trapped-Ion Hardware: Convergence, Noise-Floor Saturation, Depth-Dependent Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25992)
*Aditi Lal,Alex Khan,Rut Lineswala,Abhishek Chopra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对IonQ离子阱处理器上的最大似然振幅估计（MLAE）量子蒙特卡洛管线进行了系统实验基准测试，发现无噪声极限接近Heisenberg标度，但该优势在真实硬件上无法保持，误差饱和于固定底噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子振幅估计理论上可将蒙特卡洛积分的误差标度从经典采样律 ε∝N^{-1/2} 提升到Heisenberg极限 ε∝N^{-1}，但这一优势能否在当前硬件上存续是一个未决的经验问题，需要进行系统性实验验证。

Method: 在IonQ Forte-1上开展60次硬件实验（5与9量子比特、每深度100-200次采样、线性与指数放大调度、约113设备小时），并在IonQ Aria-1与Forte-1噪声模型上于5、9、19量子比特进行100次实验。管线由受控-R_y旋转态制备、基于Grover的振幅放大与最大似然推断组成，以 b_max^{-1}∫_0^{b_max} sin^2 x dx（b_max=π/5）为基准。

Result: 无噪声极限下达到 ε∝N^{-0.88}，接近Heisenberg标度参考，在oracle调用增加133倍时误差降低54-74倍；但所有含噪后端均饱和于 (2-5)×10^{-2} 的误差底噪，Forte-1线性调度拟合指数为0.04-0.15，厂商噪声模型上 α≤0；超过最优浅深度后，更深放大不带来持续精度增益而回落至噪声底噪。Forte-1硬件在每个深度 m≥2 均优于IonQ自身的Aria-1噪声模型，表明厂商噪声模型是MLAE精度的不良预测器。

Conclusion: 对不确定性量化而言，当前设备上的有效工作点是选择浅层、经调度调优的放大深度，使最深线路落在 p≈1/2 附近，而非在相干预算允许范围内选取最深的调度。

Abstract: Quantum Amplitude Estimation promises to accelerate Monte Carlo integration by replacing the classical $\varepsilon \propto N^{-1/2}$ sampling law with the Heisenberg-limited $\varepsilon \propto N^{-1}$ scaling, but whether any part of that advantage survives on present-day hardware is an empirical question. We report a systematic experimental benchmark of a Maximum-Likelihood Amplitude Estimation QMC pipeline on IonQ trapped-ion processors, comprising 60 hardware trials on IonQ Forte-1 across five settings(5 & 9 qubits, 100-200 shots per depth, linear & exponential amplification schedules, ~113 device-hours), together with 100 trials on IonQ Aria-1 & Forte-1 noise models at 5, 9 & 19 qubits. The pipeline combines controlled-$R_y$ rotational state preparation, Grover-based amplitude amplification and maximum-likelihood inference and is benchmarked against $b_{\max}^{-1}\!\int_0^{b_{\max}}\!\sin^2\!x\,\mathrm{d}x$ with $b_{\max}=π/5$ ,given results emerge. First, the noiseless limit it attains $\varepsilon \propto N^{-0.88}$, close to the Heisenberg-scaling reference, reducing the error 54-74x over a 133x increase in oracle calls. Second, scaling does not survive on hardware. Every noisy backend saturates at an error floor of (2-5)x 10^{-2}, with fitted exponents of 0.04-0.15 on Forte-1 linear schedules and $α\le\!0$ on the vendor noise models: beyond the optimal shallow depth, however, deeper amplification provides no sustained accuracy gain and instead returns to the noise floor. Forte-1 hardware also outperformed IonQ's own Aria-1 noise model at every depth $m \ge 2$, indicating that vendor noise models are bad predictors of MLAE accuracy. The practical implication for UQ is that on current devices the useful operating point is a shallow, schedule-tuned amplification depth chosen so that the deepest circuit lands near p~1/2 not the deepest schedule the coherence budget allows.

</details>


### [146] [Fisher-Information Geometry Linking Schrodinger Dynamics, Bipartite Correlations, and Decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26159)
*J. Sumaya-Martinez and*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子理论中区分并联系 Fisher 信息的两种角色：作为 Schrödinger 动力学变分重构中的经典概率泛函，以及参数化密度算符的量子 Fisher 度量。作者从 Hamilton–Jacobi 系综作用量出发恢复生成量子势的 Fisher 项，分析两体态的量子 Fisher 信息矩阵（QFIM），并给出纯态与混合两比特态中优化交叉 QFIM 与 concurrence 的关系，最后在双路径干涉仪中给出最优相位 Fisher 信息 I_phi^opt = V^2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fisher 信息在量子理论中出现在两个数学上不同的场景：经典概率泛函框架下的 Schrödinger 动力学变分重构，以及参数化密度算符的量子 Fisher 度量。作者希望在不将二者等同的前提下，探讨这两种角色能在多大程度上建立联系，并把概率梯度能量、两体关联几何、纠缠与退相干导致的局部相位灵敏度损失纳入统一的数学框架，同时保留经典 Fisher 信息、QFIM 几何、Bell 非局域性与全局相位量子化之间的区别。

Method: 方法上，作者从 Hamilton–Jacobi 系综作用量出发，恢复产生量子势的 Fisher 项并分离其对动能期望的贡献；随后分析两体态的量子 Fisher 信息矩阵（QFIM）。对于任意纯两比特态，计算归一化局域生成元下非对角 QFIM 元素的优化幅值，并考虑集体相位生成元；将优化纯态量通过凸顶（convex roof）延拓到任意混合两比特态，并与 Wootters concurrence 比较。此外，对 Werner 态直接评估混合密度算符上的 QFIM，比较原始优化交叉 QFIM 与凸顶量之间的差距；最后在带 which-way 标记的双路径干涉仪中计算局部输出统计可得的最优相位 Fisher 信息。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对任意纯两比特态，归一化局域生成元下非对角 QFIM 元素的优化幅值等于 concurrence，集体相位生成元给出 FQ = 4 C^2；(2) 将优化纯态量经凸顶延拓后，对任意混合两比特态与 Wootters concurrence 得到精确恒等式；(3) 直接评估混合密度算符的 QFIM 则不同：Werner 态的原始优化交叉 QFIM 为 2 p^2/(1 + p)，在可分离区域内仍非零，原始量与凸顶量之间的差距刻画了该族中不被纠缠捕捉的关联几何；(4) 在带 which-way 标记的双路径干涉仪中，局部输出统计可得的最优相位 Fisher 信息为 I_phi^opt = V^2。

Conclusion: 作者将概率梯度能量、两体关联几何、纠缠以及退相干引起的局部相位灵敏度损失置于一个共同的数学框架中，同时明确区分经典 Fisher 信息、QFIM 几何、Bell 非局域性与全局相位量子化。凸顶延拓后的优化交叉 QFIM 与 concurrence 的精确恒等式表明纠缠可由该几何量刻画，而原始混合态 QFIM 与凸顶量之间的差距则揭示了可分离区域内仍存在的、非纠缠性关联几何。
