Abstract: Fisher information appears in quantum theory in two mathematically distinct settings: as a classical probability functional in variational reconstructions of Schrodinger dynamics and as the quantum Fisher metric of parameterized density operators. Here we examine how far these roles can be connected without identifying the two objects. Starting from a Hamilton-Jacobi ensemble action, we recover the Fisher term that generates the quantum potential and isolate its contribution to the kinetic-energy expectation. We then analyze the quantum Fisher information matrix (QFIM) of bipartite states. For any pure two-qubit state, the optimized magnitude of the off-diagonal QFIM element for normalized local generators equals the concurrence, while a collective phase generator yields FQ = 4 C^2. Extending the optimized pure-state quantity by a convex roof gives an exact identity with Wootters concurrence for arbitrary mixed two-qubit states. This differs from evaluating the QFIM directly on a mixed density operator: for Werner states the raw optimized cross-QFIM is 2 p^2/(1 + p) and remains nonzero inside the separable region. The gap between the raw and convex-roof quantities therefore isolates, for this family, correlation geometry not captured by entanglement. Finally, in a two-path interferometer with a which-way marker, the optimal phase Fisher information available from local output statistics is I_phi^opt = V^2. These results place probability-gradient energy, bipartite correlation geometry, entanglement, and decoherence-induced loss of local phase sensitivity in a common mathematical setting while preserving the distinctions between classical Fisher information, QFIM geometry, Bell nonlocality, and global phase quantization.

</details>


### [147] [Channel-Constrained Information Scheduling for Quantum Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26273)
*Qipeng Qian,Yuntao Qian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"信道约束的信息调度"，通过可从当前状态记录经指定信道到达的源盲纯态精化来组织混合态量子扩散的逆问题，定义信道约束信息时钟 V_ξ 并用递归反演构造可实现等信息调度，给出其数学性质与二元/高维的锐利结构刻划，并在 qutrit 实验上验证调度近乎均衡化多步负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合态量子扩散的逆问题中，按信息量（每步后源信息剩余量）组织调度虽自然，但密度层面的目标未必对应从当前实现可达的轨迹；即密度层级目标与可从当前状态到达的轨迹之间存在不可达性 Gap，需要一种既遵循信道约束又可实现的信息调度框架。

Method: 提出信道约束信息调度：每一步在当前状态-分辨记录可达的范围内，对源盲纯态精化做优化，且必须经由指定信道；由此定义信道约束信息时钟 V_ξ，其反函数选取达到目标信息水平的最少破坏步；用递归反演构造可实现的等信息调度，以最小化 Bayes 逆预测中最坏源信息负担；对 V_ξ 建立达到性、连续性、单调性、凸性与有限支撑，并给出精确的原始/对偶表述；进一步给出锐利结构刻划：二元 qubit 退极化轨迹达到密度层级的 Belavkin–Staszewski 包络，而所有 d≥3 存在二元非对易族，表现出严格的静态-动态分离；并做 qutrit 实验。

Result: 建立 V_ξ 的达到性、连续性、单调性、凸性、有限支撑及精确原始/对偶表述；给出锐利结构刻划：二元 qubit 退极化轨迹达到密度层级 Belavkin–Staszewski 包络，而每个 d≥3 均存在二元非对易族呈现严格静态-动态分离；qutrit 实验显示所提调度器几乎均衡化实现的多步负担，且学习到的逆预测器能恢复预测的分配。

Conclusion: 信道约束信息调度为混合态量子扩散提供了可实现的等信息逆调度方案：通过信道约束信息时钟及其反演，能在 Bayes 逆预测中最小化最坏源信息负担，并具备良好的分析性质与精确对偶；低维情形可精确达到经典信息包络，高维存在严格的静态-动态分离，实验验证了负担均衡与分配可恢复性。

Abstract: Information-based diffusion schedules organize the reverse problem by prescribing how much source information remains after each forward step. For mixed-state quantum diffusion, density-level targets need not correspond to trajectories reachable from the current realization. We introduce \emph{channel-constrained information scheduling}: each step optimizes over source-blind pure-state refinements reachable from the current state-resolved record through the prescribed channel. This defines a channel-constrained information clock $V_ξ$, whose inverse selects the least-corrupting step that reaches a target information level. Recursive inversion constructs a realizable equal-information schedule minimizing the worst source-information burden in Bayes reverse prediction. We establish attainment, continuity, monotonicity, convexity, finite support, and exact primal/dual formulations for $V_ξ$. We further give a sharp structural characterization: binary-qubit depolarizing trajectories attain the density-level Belavkin--Staszewski envelope, whereas every $d\ge3$ admits binary noncommuting families with a strict static--dynamic separation. Qutrit experiments show that the proposed scheduler nearly equalizes the realized multi-step burden, and learned reverse predictors recover the predicted allocation.

</details>


### [148] [Effective-Hamiltonian Quantum Solvers for Differential Equations: Alternative Constructions and Function Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26330)
*Annie E. Paine*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文扩展了将微分方程编码为量子基态问题的框架，涵盖非齐次约束、源项、非线性方程及不同函数编码方式，并分析其对求解性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将微分方程编码为半正定有效哈密顿量的基态问题是一种有前景的量子求解思路，但原有均一化框架在非零初边值条件、源项、非线性方程以及函数表示方式等方面存在局限，需进一步扩展并厘清其实际权衡。

Method: 方法包括：利用已知非零参考条件将多个非零初值、边值和数据条件纳入均一化形式 A|f⟩=0；分析基于 A|f⟩=|b⟩、哈密顿量 H_b=A†(I−|b⟩⟨b|)A 的替代形式，通过增广系统引入源项与约束；针对非线性方程，研究张量积表示带来的非物理基态简并，并考虑哈密顿量约束与拟设层面限制；最后构建两种哈密顿量构造的网格值振幅编码版本并与谱系数编码对比。

Result: 通过线性与非线性示例，评估了哈密顿量形式与函数表示选择对解恢复、谱隙、简并及读出方式的影响。

Conclusion: 这些结果拓展了基于基态的量子微分方程求解器的适用范围，同时明确了其主要实际权衡。

Abstract: Differential equations can be encoded as ground-state problems by constructing a positive-semidefinite effective Hamiltonian whose minimum-energy state represents the solution. We extend this framework in several directions. First, we show how multiple nonzero initial, boundary, and data conditions can be incorporated into the homogeneous formulation $A | f\rangle=0$ using a known nonzero reference condition. We then analyse an alternative formulation based on $A |f\rangle = | b\rangle$, with Hamiltonian $H_b=A^\dagger(I-|b\rangle\langle b|)A$, which incorporates source terms and nonzero constraints through an augmented system. For nonlinear differential equations, we examine the unphysical ground-state degeneracy introduced by tensor-product representations and consider both Hamiltonian constraints and ansatz-level restrictions for targeting physically valid product states. Finally, we develop grid-value amplitude-encoded versions of both Hamiltonian constructions and compare them with spectral coefficient encoding. Through linear and nonlinear examples, we assess how the choice of Hamiltonian formulation and function representation affects solution recovery, spectral gap, degeneracy, and readout. These results broaden the applicability of ground-state-based quantum differential-equation solvers while clarifying their principal practical trade-offs.

</details>


### [149] [Non-Markovian Charging of a Micromaser Quantum Battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26341)
*N. Massa,D. Ferraro,F. Cavaliere,G. Benenti,M. Sassetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文理论研究非马尔可夫性在量子电池充电中的作用，以Micromaser为模型，通过将腔模耦合到记忆辅助比特来引入并调控记忆效应，数值表明非马尔可夫记忆效应可提升充电容量与稳定性，并可在电路量子电动力学平台实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非马尔可夫性（记忆效应）是否能作为量子电池充电的资源，并刻画其相对于马尔可夫情形的优势。

Method: 以Micromaser为模型：能量存储在谐振腔模中；将该模式耦合到一个二能级记忆辅助比特以引入并控制记忆效应；该辅助比特依次与一束二能级充电器通过成对交换相互作用发生部分交换（partial swap）；采用数值分析比较非马尔可夫与马尔可夫情形。并论证该方案可在电路量子电动力学（circuit QED）的固态平台实现。

Result: 非马尔可夫记忆效应作为一种资源，能同时增强器件的充电容量（charging capacity）和稳定性，优于对应的马尔可夫情形。

Conclusion: 非马尔可夫性可被用作提升量子电池性能的资源；所提出的Micromaser充电方案与当前最先进的电路量子电动力学固态平台兼容，具备实验实现可行性。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the role played by non-Markovianity in the charging of a quantum battery. We focus on the case of a Micromaser, where the energy is stored in a resonant cavity mode and memory effects are introduced and controlled by coupling this mode to a memory ancilla, described as a two-level system, that in turn sequentially interacts with a stream of two-level chargers through pairwise exchange interactions leading to a partial swap. Through numerical analysis, we show that non-Markovian memory effects act as a resource that enhances both the charging capacity and the stability of this device with respect to the Markovian counterpart. We conclude our study showing that the proposed scheme can be implemented in state-of-the-art solid-state platforms for circuit quantum electrodynamics.

</details>


### [150] [An Operator-Theoretic Model of Entanglement Percolation in Series-Parallel Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26367)
*Kan He,Jinchuan Hou,Yaqi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对大规模量子网络中纠缠分布（ED）问题，指出渗流理论方法依赖数值模拟与近似算法、缺乏可解释性与可控性，转而采用算子理论构建串并联量子网络中纠缠渗流的数学框架，并分析其中的若干关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模量子网络中实现纠缠分布是量子通信的基础性理论框架，但面临重大挑战。渗流理论被视为潜在解决方案，然而其在ED中主要依赖数值模拟和近似算法，对大规模网络中ED过程的诸多现象（包括阈值附近的涌现现象）缺乏可解释性与可控性。

Method: 采用算子理论（operator theory）方法，为串并联量子网络（series-parallel QNs）中的纠缠渗流构建数学框架，并运用算子理论方法分析纠缠渗流过程中的若干关键问题。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值或定量结果，仅表明通过算子理论方法对纠缠渗流过程中的若干关键问题进行了分析。

Conclusion: 算子理论有望为串并联量子网络中的纠缠渗流提供更具可解释性与可控性的数学框架，弥补传统渗流理论在ED分析中依赖数值模拟与近似算法的不足。

Abstract: The realization of entanglement distribution (ED) in large-scale quantum networks (QNs), as a fundamental theoretical framework of quantum communication, faces significant challenges. Percolation theory, drawn from statistical physics, has been identified as a potential solution to this problem. Nevertheless, when addressing ED, percolation theory primarily relies on numerical simulations and approximate algorithms. This approach often lacks interpretability and controllability for many phenomena occurring during the ED process in large-scale networks, including emergent phenomena near the threshold. To address these limitations, in this paper, we {focus on} leveraging operator theory to construct a mathematical framework for entanglement percolation in series-parallel QNs. Through the application of operator-theoretic methodologies, we analyze several critical issues within the entanglement percolation process.

</details>


### [151] [Photonic CZ gates based on few-photon scattering from emitter-cavity systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26429)
*Mateusz Duda,Pieter Kok*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析基于含二能级发射体的N个波导耦合腔的光子CZ门性能，推导单/双光子散射矩阵并计算门保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估基于波导耦合腔与二能级发射体系统的光子CZ门性能，明确单光子与双光子波包优化之间的内在权衡对门保真度的影响。

Method: 利用量子光学输入-输出形式推导单腔的单光子与双光子散射矩阵，并借助SLH形式推广至N>1个腔，进而计算CZ门保真度；考虑两个中心位于发射体-腔系统双光子跃迁上的高斯输入波包。

Result: 发现单光子与双光子波包形状优化之间存在权衡：单光子波包在大发射体-腔耦合率下保持良好（此时与单光子跃迁重叠很小），而双光子波包在较小耦合率（与腔衰减率乘以腔数相当）下获得所需的π相位移动。该权衡使所有输入态平均后的CZ门保真度限制在约60%。

Conclusion: 单光子与双光子波包优化之间的固有权衡将光子CZ门的总平均保真度限制在约60%，表明该方案存在根本性性能瓶颈。

Abstract: In this paper we analyze the performance of photonic CZ gates based on N waveguide-coupled cavities containing two-level emitters. We derive single- and two-photon scattering matrices for one cavity using the input-output formalism of quantum optics and for N>1 cavities using the SLH formalism, allowing us to compute the CZ gate fidelity. We consider two input Gaussian wave packets that are centered on a two-photon transition of the emitter-cavity system, and find a trade-off between optimizing the single-photon and two-photon wave packet shape. While the single-photon wave packet is preserved for large emitter-cavity coupling rates, where there is little overlap with the single-photon transitions, the two-photon wave packet acquires the necessary $π$ phase shift for smaller coupling rates, comparable to the cavity decay rate multiplied by the number of cavities. We find that this trade-off limits the CZ gate fidelity to approximately 60% when averaging over all input states.

</details>


### [152] [I Prove, Therefore I Am: Spatiotemporal Multi-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26448)
*Ziqing Guo,Fuyuki Kitagawa,Xiao Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出时空安全多方计算（spatiotemporal MPC），将MPC输入扩展到依赖物理位置、时间或轨迹的事实，并给出在UC框架下的安全性定义与构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MPC只处理私有数字输入的隐私与一致性，但许多功能需要输入依赖物理事实（如位置、时间、轨迹）。核心困难在于：在基于模拟的安全框架中，如何提取时空信息，并防止恶意方使用与其真实物理状态不一致的时空输入。

Method: 提出"时空知识论证"（arguments of spatiotemporal knowledge），遵循"I prove, therefore I am"原则：不直接用数学关系定义物理在场，而是通过能否完成一个可靠的时空验证协议来操作性地定义。提取器从成功证明者处恢复时空点，并利用该证明者成功完成辅助时空验证协议来认证其物理有效性。基于此定义UC安全的时空MPC，捕捉隐私、物理一致性与可组合性。

Result: 构造了达成该MPC概念的方案：首先构造UC安全的时空知识提交-证明协议——在CRS模型下基于LWE，对抗无预共享纠缠的量子证明者；在QROM下对抗具有无界预共享纠缠的量子证明者。利用这些协议，由半诚实后量子MPC获得UC安全时空MPC。

Conclusion: 该工作将MPC范式扩展至时空输入场景，建立了相应的模拟式安全定义，并给出了基于后量子假设的可组合构造，同时将框架扩展到支持经典时空输入的量子功能。

Abstract: Secure multiparty computation (MPC) enables mutually distrustful parties to compute on private digital inputs. We initiate the study of spatiotemporal MPC, extending this paradigm to functionalities whose inputs additionally depend on physical facts such as the parties' locations, times, or trajectories. Such protocols must simultaneously hide spatiotemporal information and ensure its physical consistency: a malicious party should not be able to make the functionality operate on a spatiotemporal input inconsistent with its actual physical state.
  The main conceptual challenge is to formulate extraction of spatiotemporal information within the simulation-based security framework. We introduce arguments of spatiotemporal knowledge, following the principle "I prove, therefore I am:" rather than defining physical presence directly through a mathematical relation, we define it operationally through the ability to complete a sound spatiotemporal verification protocol. Accordingly, an extractor recovers a spatiotemporal point from a successful prover and certifies its physical validity by using the extracted prover to succeed in an auxiliary spatiotemporal verification protocol. Building on this notion, we define universally composable (UC) security for spatiotemporal MPC, capturing privacy, physical consistency, and composability.
  We provide constructions achieving this new MPC notion. We first construct UC-secure commit-and-prove of spatiotemporal knowledge: in the CRS model under LWE against quantum provers without pre-shared entanglement, and in the QROM against quantum provers with unbounded pre-shared entanglement. Using these protocols, we obtain UC-secure spatiotemporal MPC from semi-honest post-quantum MPC. We also extend our framework to support quantum functionalities with classical spatiotemporal input.

</details>


### [153] [Tensor Network Simulation of Dynamic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26516)
*Innocenzo Fulginiti,Alessandro Poggiali,Christian Mendl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将基于DMRG的张量网络方法扩展至动态电路模拟，提出单路径随机采样与多路径分支集成两种策略应对测量分支指数增长，并在量子隐形传态、GHZ态制备及随机动态电路上进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态电路包含中段测量与经典控制操作，能增强量子程序表达能力，广泛用于量子纠错、态制备和分布式量子算法；但中段测量导致执行分支指数增长，使其经典模拟非常困难，亟需可扩展的模拟框架。

Method: 将面向量子电路模拟的DMRG（密度矩阵重整化群）张量网络方法扩展以支持动态电路操作；针对分支指数增殖，引入并对比两种策略——对单个测量结果采样的单路径随机方法，以及维护分支集合的多路径方法。

Result: 在标准动态电路（隐形传态协议、GHZ态制备）及随机动态电路上完成基准测试，分析了模拟精度与计算效率之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为动态电路的经典模拟提供了一个实用且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Dynamic circuits, which incorporate mid-circuit measurements and classically controlled operations, extend the expressive power of quantum programs and are central to applications such as quantum error correction, state preparation, and distributed quantum algorithms. However, their classical simulation is challenging due to the exponential growth of execution branches induced by mid-circuit measurements. In this work, we develop a tensor network approach for simulating dynamic circuits by extending a DMRG-based method for quantum circuit simulation to support dynamic circuit operations. To address the exponential proliferation of execution branches, we introduce and compare two strategies: a single-path stochastic approach that samples individual measurement outcomes, and a multi-path approach that maintains an ensemble of branches. We benchmark these methods on standard dynamic circuits, including the teleportation protocol and GHZ state preparation, as well as on random dynamic circuits. Our analysis explores the trade-offs between simulation accuracy and computational efficiency. These results provide a practical and scalable framework for the classical simulation of dynamic circuits.

</details>


### [154] [Magnetic tuning of quantum backflow: Schrödinger vs Pauli system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26548)
*Tomasz Radozycki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究均匀磁场中自旋1/2带电粒子在最低朗道能级近似下的概率回流，发现双模干涉可在薛定谔与泡利框架下产生负概率流，且磁场可作为可调控制参数增强回流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率回流是量子力学中违反经典直觉的现象，但在朗道系统中的行为尚不清晰，尤其在包含自旋自由度时轨道与自旋对概率流的贡献及磁场调控机制有待澄清。

Method: 采用最低朗道能级近似，分别在薛定谔方程和泡利方程框架下计算带电自旋1/2粒子的概率流；在泡利情形中显式分离轨道与自旋贡献；分析双模干涉、轨道失配与自旋进动之间的共振，并考察有限空间分辨率（粗粒化）的影响。

Result: 单模朗道态仅表现出微弱且不稳健的回流；双模干涉在薛定谔和泡利描述中均产生负概率流；泡利情形揭示出额外的自旋相关干涉机制；轨道失配与自旋进动之间的共振增强了回流；有限空间分辨率在现实粗粒化条件下不会抑制该效应。

Conclusion: 均匀磁场可作为同时调控轨道空间重叠与自旋动力学的可调参数，从而实现磁场控制的量子回流；朗道系统中的概率回流是一种可及的干涉现象。

Abstract: Probability backflow is investigated for charged spin-$1/2$ particles in a uniform magnetic field within the lowest-Landau-level approximation. While single-mode Landau states exhibit only weak and non-robust backflow, it is shown that two-mode interference gives rise to negative probability flux in both the Schrödinger and Pauli formulations. In the Pauli case, orbital and spin contributions to the probability current are explicitly separated, revealing an additional interference mechanism. The external magnetic field is found to act as a tunable control parameter, simultaneously controlling the spatial overlap of orbitals and the spin dynamics. In the two-mode regime, a resonance between orbital mismatch and spin precession leads to an enhancement of backflow. Finite spatial resolution is shown not to suppress the effect within realistic coarse-graining. These results establish magnetic-field-controlled quantum backflow in Landau systems as an accessible interference phenomenon.

</details>


### [155] [Power-resolved ergotropy and correlation redistribution in a bosonic quantum battery driven by a coherent-state superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26573)
*João P. d'El-Rey,Tailan S. Sarubi,Ana C. S. Costa,José G. G. de Oliveira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在小规模非高斯玻色子量子电池中建立了可提取功与纠缠之间的直接动力学联系，并借助精确可解结构刻画多体关联的再分配过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ergotropic-gap关系可作为量子关联的热力学见证，但在多体非高斯玻色系统中，可提取功与纠缠之间的直接动力学联系仍缺乏研究。

Method: 研究由相干态叠加驱动的玻色子量子电池，利用其无穷维希尔伯特空间中动力学仍保持的简单结构，精确求得功量与关联度量。

Result: 无源部分能量对应的可提取功损失由充电器-电池纠缠直接决定；该关系将充电功率分解为能量转移与电池能谱变化两部分。多体关联逐步从含充电器的关联再分配为电池内部关联。对称共振协议下，完全能量转移同时对应充电器-电池解纠缠与完全可提取储能，且电池内部多体关联最大；但单胞仍为混合态，表明全局与局域功提取存在差异。局域可提取功完全由能量相干性支撑。单光子损耗下解析结构仍成立，但环境关联使充电器解纠缠与最大功提取分离。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了非高斯玻色子量子电池中功、纠缠与多体关联的精确动力学关系，并区分了全局与局域功提取机制，为耗散环境下的量子热力学关联诊断提供了可解析的框架。

Abstract: Ergotropic-gap relations provide thermodynamic witnesses of quantum correlations, but a direct dynamical connection between extractable work and entanglement remains largely unexplored in multipartite non-Gaussian bosonic systems. Here, we establish such a connection for a bosonic quantum battery driven by a coherent-state superposition. Despite the infinite-dimensional Hilbert space, the dynamics retains a simple structure that allows the relevant work and correlation measures to be obtained exactly. We show that the loss of extractable work associated with the passive part of the battery energy is directly determined by charger-battery entanglement. This relation also separates the charging power into contributions from energy transfer and from changes in the battery spectrum. At the same time, multipartite correlations are progressively redistributed from charger-involving correlations to correlations internal to the battery. For a symmetric resonant protocol, complete energy transfer coincides with charger-battery disentanglement and fully extractable stored energy, while multipartite correlations within the battery are maximal. Individual cells, however, remain mixed, revealing a distinction between global and local work extraction. We further show that, in the regime considered, locally extractable work is entirely supported by energetic coherence. Under single-photon loss, the analytical structure survives, but environmental correlations separate charger disentanglement from maximal work extraction.

</details>


### [156] [Preselection-Encoded Weak-Value Interferometry for Optical-Rotation Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26583)
*Jinyi Li,Juncheng Zheng,Yuntao Shen,Jiashu Liang,Sicheng Pan,Dongmei Li,Hengyan Wang,Wenqiang Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出预选择编码弱值放大（PeWVA）方案，将待测量编码进探针偏振后再进入弱值干涉仪，实现传感与后选择读出分离，在探测器动态范围受限时提升光旋转测量性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光旋转测量依赖增加探测光子数来提升性能，但受探测器饱和与技术噪声限制；常规弱值放大的增益优势也常被实际读出条件所抵消，需要一种在探测器受限条件下提升读出的策略。

Method: 构建预选择编码弱值放大架构：先将待测物理量编码到探针偏振，再经下游弱值干涉仪后选择读出，使传感相互作用与读出分离，实现可调局域放大并降低到达探测器的光功率；随后进行Fisher信息分析，并分析相干杂散光与相位涨落的误差转换；最后以87Rb Mx原子磁强计为平台，分别使用跨阻放大与雪崩光电二极管进行实验验证。

Result: 理论上PeWVA不增加Fisher信息，但在探测器动态范围受限时具有实际优势；相干杂散光与相位涨落向等效旋转误差的转换被抑制。实验在探测器受限条件下显著改善磁场读出，并验证了对抗蒸气池诱导杂散光与干涉仪相位调制的鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: PeWVA是一种面向探测器受限条件的读出策略，可降低探测光功率同时提升对典型干涉微扰的容忍度，为弱值放大在实用光学传感中的部署提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Optical-rotation measurements benefit from increasing probe photon number, but their performance is often limited by detector saturation and technical noise in the optical readout. Here we introduce preselection-encoded weak-value amplification (PeWVA), in which the quantity of interest is encoded into the probe polarization before a downstream weak-value interferometer. This architecture separates the sensing interaction from the postselected readout, enabling tunable local amplification while reducing the optical power delivered to the detector. We show that PeWVA does not create additional Fisher information, but offers a practical advantage when detector dynamic range is limiting. We further analyze coherent stray light and phase fluctuations and show reduced conversion of these perturbations into equivalent rotation errors. Using an $^{87}$Rb $M_x$ atomic magnetometer, we experimentally demonstrate substantially improved magnetic-field readout under detector-constrained conditions using both transimpedance-amplified and avalanche photodiodes. The experiments also confirm reduced susceptibility to vapor-cell-induced stray light and interferometer phase modulation. These results establish PeWVA as a detector-constrained optical readout strategy with enhanced tolerance to representative interferometric perturbations.

</details>


### [157] [A New Method For Manipulating Circuits, Application To Quantum Adders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26591)
*William Schober,Scott Wesley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用受控量子电路操作技术，实现QFT加法器与Ripple-Carry加法器之间的门级转译，并提出一种无需辅助比特的Carry-Lookahead量子加法器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子加法器存在多种实现范式（如QFT型与Ripple-Carry型），但二者之间缺乏明确的等价转换手段，限制了电路复用与设计空间的统一比较；同时减少辅助比特开销也是量子电路设计的重要需求。

Method: 采用一种操纵受控量子电路的新技术，将基于Quantum Fourier Transform的加法器与基于经典可逆逻辑Ripple-Carry技术的加法器进行显式的门级转译（transpilation），从而实现两类加法器的相互转换；并据此构造一种可自然解释为Carry-Lookahead的量子加法器，其设计不使用任何ancilla。

Result: 成功给出两类量子加法器之间的显式门级转译方案；并提出一种无辅助比特的Carry-Lookahead型量子加法器。

Conclusion: 受控量子电路操作技术可作为统一不同量子加法器架构的有效工具，所提出的无ancilla Carry-Lookahead加法器为低开销量子算术电路设计提供了新选项。

Abstract: We use a new technique for manipulating controlled quantum circuits to convert between two distinct types of quantum adders, one based on the Quantum Fourier Transform and the other based on the Ripple-Carry technique from classical reversible logic. This conversion takes the form of an explicit gate-level transpilation. We also present a new quantum adder with a natural interpretation as a kind of Carry-Lookahead adder that uses no ancillas.

</details>


### [158] [Hamiltonian Learning and Certification via Eigenphase Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26596)
*Myeongjin Shin,Yu Tong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"本征相位工程"(eigenphase engineering)技术,用于在大的固定步长下学习 k-local 哈密顿量,达到海森堡极限精度,步长约等于理论上限 Θ(1/Λ),并将演化时间下界加强至 Ω_k(s^{1-1/(2k)}/ε)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习算法(基于动力学近似或迭代消除未知哈密顿量)需要随着精度提高而使用多项式级更短的演化步长,导致控制开销大。作者希望在允许近乎最大步长、仅做整数倍固定时间间隔前向演化(演化时间不宜过短)的条件下,仍实现海森堡极限的高精度学习。

Method: 核心方法为本征相位工程:不依赖动力学近似或迭代消除,而是把能量期望差编码进实际受控演化本征相位的微扰响应中,再通过相位估计与高阶外推提取信息,从而避免精度提升时步长需要多项式缩短。算法针对 s-稀疏、局域性 k、‖H‖_op≤Λ 的未知哈密顿量,恢复其泡利系数至 ℓ2 误差 ε。另一版本仅用单量子比特操作,保持相同步长,但有 √n 的额外开销。方法还支持容错的哈密顿量认证及指定泡利系数的估计(后者无需局域性和稀疏性假设)。

Result: 算法总演化时间为 Õ(s/ε),与已知最优标度匹配,同时允许接近最大步长 Õ(1/Λ)。下界加强为 Ω_k(s^{1-1/(2k)}/ε),使算法与最优仅差 Õ(s^{1/(2k)}) 因子。单量子比特版本保持相同步长,ℓ2 学习时间有 √n 额外开销。

Conclusion: 本征相位工程确立了以稀疏控制提取哈密顿量信息的一种通用方法,在大步长下实现海森堡极限学习,并附带认证与单系数估计等扩展能力。

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian learning for $k$-local Hamiltonians at large step size, using only forward time evolution at integer multiples of a fixed time interval. For this task, our new technique, which we call eigenphase engineering, achieves Heisenberg-limited learning with a step size that is optimal up to logarithmic factors. For an unknown $s$-sparse Hamiltonian with fixed locality $k$ and $\|H\|_{\mathrm{op}}\leqΛ$, our algorithm recovers its Pauli coefficients to $\ell_2$ error $\varepsilon$ in total evolution time $\widetilde{O}(s/\varepsilon)$, matching the best known scaling while allowing a near-maximal step size $\widetildeΘ(1/Λ)$. As an independent contribution, we strengthen the evolution-time lower bound to $Ω_k(s^{1-1/(2k)}/\varepsilon)$, placing our algorithm within a factor $\widetilde{O}(s^{1/(2k)})$ of being optimal. Eigenphase engineering departs from approaches based on dynamical approximation or iteratively canceling the unknown Hamiltonian. It encodes energy expectation differences in the perturbative response of eigenphases of the actual controlled evolution, then extracts them through phase estimation and high-order extrapolation. This avoids the need for polynomially shorter evolution steps as the target precision improves. A version using only single-qubit operations retains the same step size with a $\sqrt n$ overhead in $\ell_2$ learning time for $n$ qubits. The method also enables tolerant Hamiltonian certification and estimation of a specified Pauli coefficient, the latter without locality or sparsity assumptions. These results establish eigenphase engineering as a versatile approach to extracting Hamiltonian information with infrequent control.

</details>


### [159] [Geometric construction of optimal probe states in distributed multiparameter quantum sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26608)
*Hongzhen Chen,Lingna Wang,Haidong Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对分布式量子传感中的多参数量子计量问题，提出用生成元本征值向量的最小包围球和椭球几何构造最优探针态，球/椭球的中心与形状决定了各公共本征态布居需满足的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单参数情形下最优探针是本征值最大与最小对应的两个生成元本征态的等权叠加，但多参数时不同参数偏好不同探针，这一简单构造无法直接推广，因此需要为多参数估计系统性地设计最优探针态。

Method: 在分布式量子传感框架下，利用生成元相互对易、共享同一本征基的性质，将每个公共本征态对应为一个生成元本征值向量；通过构造这些向量的最小包围球（对应最大总量子Fisher信息）和最小包围椭球（对应最小加权协方差）来刻画最优探针。

Result: 证明了最小包围球决定最大总量子Fisher信息，最小包围椭球决定最小加权协方差；两种情况下凸体的边界标识了能贡献于最优探针的公共本征态，其中心与形状则给出了这些本征态布居必须满足的条件。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了设计最优探针态的系统性方法，将参数生成元的几何结构直接与多参数量子计量可达精度联系起来。

Abstract: The choice of probe state is essential to achieving ultimate precision in quantum metrology. For a single parameter, an equal superposition of eigenstates associated with the largest and smallest generator eigenvalues is optimal. This simple construction does not directly extend to multiple parameters, since different parameters can favor different probes. We address this difficulty through a geometric construction of optimal probes for multiparameter estimation in distributed quantum sensing. In this setting, the generators commute and share a common eigenbasis, so each common eigenstate defines a vector of generator eigenvalues. We show that the minimal enclosing balls and ellipsoids of these vectors determine the maximal total quantum Fisher information and the minimum weighted covariance, respectively. In both cases, the boundary of the convex body identifies the common eigenstates that can contribute to an optimal probe, and its center and shape specify the conditions that their populations must satisfy. Our results provide a systematic approach to designing optimal probe states, linking the structure of the parameter generators directly to the attainable precision in multiparameter quantum metrology.

</details>


### [160] [Error Correction Properties of Covariant Bosonic Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26660)
*Frederic St-Amand,Jean-Philippe Burelle,Baptiste Royer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于量子纠错的表示论框架，形式化多模对称玻色码的构造与分析，聚焦有限群，将QEC矩阵视为群表示的态射，利用Schur引理使不可约表示块消失，重建已有单模/双模玻色码，并基于SU(2)有限子群构造与分析三种新码，考察逻辑表示可约性的影响并优化种子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色码（如cat码、GKP码）是实现硬件高效容错的重要途径，且常由底层对称性组织。作者希望用表示论语言统一刻画多模对称玻色码的构造与纠错分析，从而系统化设计并评估此类码。

Method: 建立量子纠错的表示论框架：以有限群为核心，用物理表示、逻辑表示与初始种子态通过协变编码定义码字；将QEC矩阵视为群表示的态射，借助Schur引理使整块不可约表示贡献为零；据此构造并分析基于SU(2)有限子群的三种多模玻色码，并研究逻辑表示可约性的影响及种子态优化（近最优保真度）。

Result: 成功复现若干已知单模与双模玻色码；构造并分析三种基于SU(2)有限子群的码的纠错保护能力；揭示逻辑表示可约性对纠错性能的影响；通过近最优保真度优化种子态。

Conclusion: 表示论框架为多模对称玻色码提供了统一的构造与分析工具，Schur引理显著简化QEC矩阵结构，SU(2)有限子群可产生有前景的码，且逻辑表示可约性与种子态优化对性能至关重要。

Abstract: Bosonic codes offer a promising approach towards hardware-efficient fault tolerance, with many leading examples such as the cat-code and the GKP code organized by an underlying symmetry. We build on a representation-theoretic framework of quantum error correction to formalize the construction and analysis of multimode symmetric bosonic codes, focusing on finite groups. Here, a physical, logical representation and an initial seed state together define the codewords via a covariant encoding. A central observation is that QEC matrices can be viewed as morphisms of group representations, so that Schur's lemma forces entire irreducible representations blocks to vanish. We recover several known single- and two-mode bosonic codes and build and analyze the error protection capabilities of three codes based on finite subgroups of SU(2). We analyze the impact of reducibility of the logical representation and optimize the seed state using the near-optimal fidelity.

</details>


### [161] [Fluctuation-Driven Nonlinear Amplification of Quantum Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26674)
*Yuewei Song,Zhenghe Zhou,Shuai Wan,Hecheng Wang,Jinpeng Li,Bowen Liu,Yinhai Li,Chunhua Dong,Guangcan Guo,Chong Wang,Zhiyuan Zhou,Baosen Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用滤波放大自发辐射(ASE)泵浦自发四波混频，通过涨落驱动的非线性统计放大机制，将量子光源的零延迟二阶关联从2.01提升至7.58，同时保持宣告式单光子特性并支持时间-能量纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代光学中光子统计与强度涨落、关联对多光子相互作用的研究至关重要，需要具有显著关联的高质量光子源。传统方法多基于相干泵浦，而泵浦场自身的统计特性（涨落）作为量子光设计维度的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将相干泵浦框架推广至涨落场，采用滤波后的放大自发辐射(ASE)作为泵浦源驱动自发四波混频。在SiN微环谐振腔中进行实验，利用泵浦强度的非线性加权与玻色子聚束效应相结合，实现对量子统计的放大和时间关联的重塑。同时用同一ASE泵浦在硅波导中验证时间-能量纠缠。

Result: ASE泵浦使零延迟非条件二阶关联函数g^(2)(0)从2.01提升到7.58；Hanbury Brown-Twiss关联时间延长约2.4倍；超聚束量子源仍保持宣告式单光子行为，g_H^(2)(0)≈0.04；同一ASE泵浦在硅波导中实现原始Franson可见度89.84%的时间-能量纠缠。

Conclusion: 驱动场统计特性可作为量子光的设计维度，拓展了高阶关联和非线性物理的研究视野。

Abstract: Photon statistics have moved to the forefront of modern optics, as intensity fluctuations and correlations shape multiphoton interactions and reveal information beyond mean-intensity measurements. Developing high-quality photon sources with pronounced correlations is a fundamental necessity in these fields. Here we demonstrate fluctuation-driven nonlinear statistical amplification of quantum light in spontaneous four-wave mixing using filtered amplified spontaneous emission (ASE). Extending the coherent-pump framework to fluctuating fields, we show how nonlinear weighting of pump intensity combines with bosonic bunching to amplify quantum statistics and reshape temporal correlations. In a SiN microring, ASE pumping increases the zero-delay unconditional second-order correlation from 2.01 to 7.58 and extends the Hanbury Brown--Twiss correlation time by a factor of approximately 2.4. The super-bunched quantum source nevertheless retains heralded single-photon behaviour with $g_H^{(2)}(0)\simeq0.04$, while the same ASE pump supports time--energy entanglement in a silicon waveguide with a raw Franson visibility of 89.84\%. These results establish driving-field statistics as a design dimension for quantum light, broadening the horizons for research into higher-order correlations and nonlinear physics.

</details>


### [162] [Strong matchgate designs in nearly optimal depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26677)
*Maxwell West,M. Cerezo,Martin Larocca*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在一般量子比特连通图上生成matchgate群（费米子高斯操作）的近似随机酉算符（design）所需的资源。作者证明，当连通图的routing number为 rt(G) 时，可获得相对误差意义下的强 ε-近似 matchgate k-design，深度为 O(k² rt(G) log n log(n/ε))；对全连接图 rt(G)=2。构造仅需matchgate群上的随机游走且无需辅助比特。作为技术副产品，改进了费米子路由，得到 O(rt(G) log n) 深度的路由器；对 k=3 得到精确强matchgate 3-design，深度 O(rt(G) log n)，在全连接下均为最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息论中一个自然目标是理解在各类群上生成近似随机酉算符所需的资源。就design这一近似概念而言，已知全酉群可由最近邻2-local门的一维电路在对数深度内近似；然而令人惊讶的是，具有这种连通性的电路无法在亚线性深度内形成matchgate群上的design，即存在"戏剧性减速"。本文旨在探究这种减速能否通过使用更一般的量子比特连通图来消除，并量化其随连通性的依赖关系。

Method: 作者考虑路由数为 rt(G) 的一般连通图 G 上的电路模型，构造了一个在matchgate群上的随机游走，不使用辅助比特（ancillae），从而生成强 ε-近似相对误差的 matchgate k-design。同时设计了一个改进的费米子路由器，用于在图上实现费米子模式的置换路由，作为主要构造的技术组件。

Result: 在一般连通图 G 上，可在深度 O(k² rt(G) log n log(n/ε)) 内获得强 ε-近似相对误差的 matchgate k-design；对全连接图 rt(G)=2。费米子路由达到 O(rt(G) log n) 深度，改进了现有最优结果。对 k=3，可在 O(rt(G) log n) 深度内获得精确强 matchgate 3-design，且无需辅助比特；在全连接连通下，费米子路由器与3-design均为最优。

Conclusion: 一般连通图上的连通性可以消除一维电路在matchgate群design生成上的亚线性深度障碍。特别地，需要从matchgate 3-design采样的费米子层析量子算法，在全连接量子计算机上相对于严格一维对应版本可获得指数级加速。该结果将design生成深度与图的路由数紧密联系起来，并给出了最优的全连接情形构造。

Abstract: Understanding the resources required to generate approximately random unitaries over various groups is a natural goal of quantum information theory. With respect to one notion of approximation, that of a design, it is known that the full unitary group can be approximated in logarithmic depth by one-dimensional circuits of nearest-neighbour 2-local gates. On the other hand, remarkably, circuits with this connectivity cannot form designs over the matchgate group in sublinear depth. Here we show that this dramatic slowdown can disappear when using a general qubit connectivity graph $\mathsf{G}$ of routing number ${\rm rt}(\mathsf{G})$. Indeed, in this setting one can obtain (strong) $\varepsilon$-approximate relative error matchgate $k$-designs in depth $\mathcal{O}(k^2{\rm rt}(\mathsf{G})\log n\log(n/\varepsilon))$. For all-to-all connectivity, ${\rm rt}(\mathsf{G})=2$. Our construction is conceptually simple, involving a random walk on the matchgate group, and no ancillae. As a technical byproduct, we improve upon the state of the art for fermionic routing, obtaining an $\mathcal{O}({\rm rt}(\mathsf{G})\log n)$ depth router. Additionally, for $k=3$, we obtain an exact strong matchgate design in $\mathcal{O}({\rm rt}(\mathsf{G})\log n)$ depth, again without ancillae. Under all-to-all connectivity, our fermionic router and 3-designs are optimal. Notably, our results imply that quantum algorithms for fermionic tomography which require drawing from a matchgate 3-design may be exponentially sped up on quantum computers with all-to-all connectivity, relative to their strictly one-dimensional counterparts.

</details>


### [163] [A quantum lower bound for path finding in welded trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26712)
*Joseph Carolan,Andrew M. Childs,Matthew Coudron,Amin Shiraz Gilani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：在独立匹配的焊接树图中，任何量子算法找到根到根路径都需要指数级查询，尽管量子游走能指数级加速根到根的导航。这表明"利用叠加探索指数多路径实现加速、但无法找到任一具体路径"是可证明的固有现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 焊接树问题中量子游走可指数级快于经典算法从根导航到根，但已知高效量子算法无法给出根间路径，因为记录路径会破坏相长干涉从而丧失加速。作者希望判定这一困难是算法设计不足还是本质限制。

Method: 采用压缩置换预言机（compressed permutation oracle）技术记录量子算法查询图的进度，证明压缩数据库在小误差内保持"无路径"（path-free）状态；通过控制该误差并界定每次压缩预言机查询带来的进度，建立查询下界。

Result: 证明在高度为 n 的独立匹配焊接树图中，任何量子算法以常数成功概率找到根间路径都需 Ω(2^{n/12}) 次查询，即指数级查询下界。

Conclusion: 找到一个焊接树路径在量子计算上可证明为难解问题；它与量子游走导航的指数加速形成对比，说明"通过叠加探索指数多路径获得加速"与"可定位任一具体路径"之间存在本质分离。

Abstract: In the welded tree problem, an algorithm is tasked with navigating a graph formed from two binary trees joined at the leaves through a ``weld'' of connecting edges. A quantum walk can navigate from root to root exponentially faster than any classical algorithm. However, known efficient quantum algorithms cannot find a path between the roots, as recording the path destroys constructive interference and thus the speedup. We prove that this is inherent: any quantum algorithm needs exponentially many queries to find a path between the roots of an independently matched welded tree graph. This provides an example of a problem that a quantum computer can solve exponentially faster than any classical algorithm by exploring exponentially many paths in superposition, but where it is provably intractable to find any such path. The proof uses compressed permutation oracles to record the progress of a quantum algorithm as it queries the graph. We show that the compressed database remains path-free up to a small error. By controlling such errors and bounding the progress of the algorithm with each compressed oracle query, we show that $Ω(2^{n/12})$ queries are required to find a path in a height-$n$ tree with constant success probability.

</details>


### [164] [Good Quantum Locally Testable Codes from Product Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26735)
*Mitali Bafna,Anqi Li,Quynh T. Nguyen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在Bafna与Vyas关于Reed-Solomon码的乘积展开猜想变体假设下，构造了具有常数码率、距离、可靠性和局域性的量子局部可测试码（LTC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LTC的构造通常难以同时实现常数码率、距离、可靠性和局域性，本文旨在借助高维展开与Reed-Solomon局部码的性质突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 采用Dinur、Lin与Vidick的高维展开框架，实例化为Rungtanapirom、Stix与Vdovina的非阿贝尔立方复形，并为复形配备对称性相容的Reed-Solomon局部码。

Result: 在所设猜想变体成立的前提下，成功得到常数码率、常数距离、常数可靠性和常数局域性的量子LTC。

Conclusion: 该工作将高维展开框架与非阿贝尔立方复形及Reed-Solomon码相结合，为常数参数量子LTC的构造提供了新途径。

Abstract: We construct quantum locally testable codes (LTCs) with constant rate, distance, soundness and locality under a variant of a product expansion conjecture of Bafna and Vyas about Reed-Solomon codes. In particular, we use the high-dimensional expansion framework of Dinur, Lin and Vidick for constructing quantum LTCs, instantiated with the non-Abelian cubical complexes of Rungtanapirom, Stix and Vdovina. Our code is obtained by equipping the complex with carefully chosen Reed-Solomon local codes whose symmetries are compatible with those of the complex.

</details>


### [165] [A Syndrome-Extraction Framework for Distributed Lattice Surgery on Arbitrary Rotated Surface-Code Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26736)
*Pritesh Thakur,Daniel Dilley,Zain Saleem*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种"缝合"构造，将任意合并的旋转表面码布局拆分为具有均匀边界的子块，并结合权重-2稳定器、Bell对、三量子比特CXX门与定制探测器标注器，使连接处的高噪声被限制在缝合面而保护码距与体区，实现更低且稳定的逻辑错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化量子计算是规模化容错量子计算的重要路线。不同模块间的跨模块门比本地门更嘈杂；且在格点手术中，合并布局会在多个方向暴露钩错误（hook errors），从而降低容错距离。现有避免钩错误的调度方案要么增加综合征提取电路深度，要么让链路故障传播进入体区（bulk）。因此需要一种既能保持钩错误规避调度、又能将高链路错误率限制在连接处而不污染体区的方案。

Method: 提出一种"缝合（seam）"构造：可将任意合并的旋转表面码布局拆分为具有均匀边界的小块，各块可保留自身的钩错误规避N/Z形调度。在缝合处将权重-4稳定器拆分为经Bell对介导的成对权重-2稳定器，并在CNOT深度为4内完成测量；同时使用三量子比特CXX门进行缝合处的宇称测量以维持最小CNOT深度。该构造将升高的链路错误限制在缝合处，保护体区免受更高错误率影响。此外设计了一个定制探测器标注器（detector annotator），用于在Stim电路中寻找探测器空间、标注探测器，并确保其适用于匹配型解码器。

Result: 将该构造实现于逻辑Pauli-XX测量的格点手术以及十字形空间结（cross-shaped spatial junction）中。在链路错误率从体区固定错误率的10倍扫描至100倍时，与本文比较的其它框架相比，所提构造给出了最低且最稳定的逻辑错误率。

Conclusion: 通过缝合构造将模块间高链路噪声局域化于缝合处、保护体区，并在维持最小CNOT深度的同时保留钩错误规避调度与容错距离，配合定制探测器标注器保证可解码性；实验证明其在不同链路错误率下均具有更低且更稳定的逻辑错误率，适用于模块化表面码的格点手术与空间结等操作。

Abstract: Modular quantum computing is a leading candidate for scalable fault tolerant quantum computation. Performing lattice surgery using surface code patches in different modules requires the use of inter-module gates that are noisier than the local gates. Furthermore, the merged layouts during lattice surgery expose multiple directions in which hook errors can propagate to reduce the fault distance. Existing hook-avoiding schedules either increase the syndrome extraction circuit depth, or let link faults propagate into the bulk. We introduce a seam construction that allows splitting arbitrary merged rotated surface code layout into smaller patches with uniform boundaries that could keep their own hook-avoiding N/Z shaped schedule. At the seam we split weight-4 stabilizers into pairs of weight-2 stabilizers that are mediated through a Bell pair and measured within a CNOT depth of 4. We also employ the use of three-qubit $\texttt{CXX}$ gates for parity measurements at the seam to maintain the minimum CNOT depth. Through this seam construction, we confine the elevated link error at the seam, protecting the bulk from higher error rate. We also give a custom detector annotator for such seams that can find the detector space, annotate detectors in the Stim circuits, and ensure that they are suitable to use by matching decoders. We implement our construction for the logical Pauli-$XX$ measurement lattice surgery and a cross-shaped spatial junction and sweep the link error rate from $10$ times to $100$ times a fixed bulk error rate, and show that our construction gives the lowest and most stable logical error rate than the other frameworks compared here.

</details>


### [166] [Quantum Broadcast Channels with Mutually Confidential Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26750)
*Paula Belzig,Sukanya Ghosal,Farzin Salek,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子广播信道中两条相互独立的经典机密消息的传输问题，每条消息需对另一接收方保密。作者在经典输入、量子输出情形下，建立了平均可靠性下且满足条件强保密性的经典Marton型内界，并给出了多字母容量刻画，以及对若干信道的机密容量区域的精确评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子广播信道中同时传输两条需互相保密的经典消息，是经典信息论机密通信问题在量子框架下的自然延伸。该问题的核心困难在于：编码器从独立生成的码本中选取码字对时会产生依赖关系，而现有经典或量子resolvability理论难以直接处理这种依赖并同时保证保密性。因此需要建立适配该场景的保密性论证工具，并厘清机密经典容量与量子容量之间的关系。

Method: 1) 针对经典输入、量子输出的广播信道，在平均可靠性标准与条件强保密性标准下，构造经典Marton型内界；编码器使用归一化似然权重从独立生成的码本中选取码字对。2) 通过分布变换（change-of-distribution）论证可靠性。3) 证明一个双部经典-量子resolvability定理，用以处理码字对选取引入的依赖，并对同一编码器建立保密性。4) 给出多字母容量刻画，并将其扩展到任意量子输入（对另一接收方保密）并纳入Stinespring环境。

Result: 恢复了确定性经典信道与退化经典-量子信道的机密容量区域，并对经典Blackwell信道给出显式评估。对于单射确定性经典广播信道的相干等距扩展，证明其机密经典容量区域与对应经典信道相同。进一步比较机密经典通信与量子传输：对Blackwell信道的相干等距扩展，确定了无辅助量子容量区域，并证明存在可达到的机密经典速率对落在该区域之外；Platypus信道提供了另一例此类分离现象。

Conclusion: 论文建立了量子广播信道下双消息机密经典通信的Marton型内界与多字母容量刻画，关键工具是能处理码字对依赖的双部经典-量子resolvability定理。结果表明机密经典容量与量子容量可以出现分离，说明保密性约束与量子传输能力并非互相包含，从而刻画了两类容量区域之间的实质性差异。

Abstract: We study the transmission of two independent confidential classical messages over a quantum broadcast channel, one for each receiver. Each message must remain secret from the other receiver, including when that receiver knows its own message. For classical inputs and quantum outputs, we establish the classical Marton-type inner bound under average reliability and conditional strong secrecy. The encoder selects pairs of codewords from independently generated codebooks using normalized likelihood weights. We prove reliability through a change-of-distribution argument. Our main technical result is a bipartite classical-quantum resolvability theorem that accounts for the dependence created by pair selection and establishes secrecy for the same encoder. We also obtain a multi-letter capacity characterization and extend it to arbitrary quantum inputs under secrecy against the other receiver, together with the Stinespring environment. We recover confidential capacity regions for deterministic classical and degraded classical-quantum channels, with an explicit evaluation for the classical Blackwell channel. For coherent isometric extensions of injective deterministic classical broadcast channels, we show that the confidential classical capacity region equals that of the corresponding classical channel. We then compare confidential classical communication with quantum transmission. For the coherent isometric extension of the Blackwell channel, we determine the unassisted quantum-capacity region and show that some achievable confidential classical rate pairs lie outside it. The Platypus channel provides another example of this separation.

</details>


### [167] [Error Suppression in Distributed Quantum Computing with Heterogeneous-Distance Lattice Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26784)
*Daniel Dilley,Anastashia Jebraeilli,Rayat Roy,Shobhit Gupta,Alvin Gonzales,Zain Saleem*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出八数据块辅助介导（8-DAM）异质距离格点手术架构，通过仅放大跨QPU边界的中心辅助块、保持数据块距离d，实现低开销容错分布式量子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算中跨QPU链路的噪声显著高于本地门，统一提升码距虽能增强保护，却会同时增大本地存储与计算所需的数据块规模，带来过高的量子比特开销，因此需要一种资源高效的容错方案。

Method: 采用异质距离格点手术与8-DAM架构：放大跨QPU边界的中心辅助块，数据块保持距离d；在不等距块之间的merge/split操作中使用traveling stabilizers抑制hook errors；对旋转表面码进行电路级仿真，固定本地去极化噪声并变化链路噪声，与均匀距离实现及两个独立分布式逻辑CNOT进行对比。

Result: 逻辑读出错误率仅微弱依赖链路噪声，相对传统格点手术的优势随链路错误增大而增强；与均匀距离实现相比，在相当逻辑错误抑制下降低物理量子比特开销；8-DAM布局可用单个放大辅助块支持四个逻辑数据块间两个同时的分布式逻辑CNOT，在高链路噪声下比两个独立CNOT具有更低逻辑错误率和更高稳定性。

Conclusion: 选择性放大辅助块是一种资源高效的容错分布式量子计算方法，适用于量子比特开销受限的近期待量子器件。

Abstract: Distributed quantum computing requires fault-tolerant operations across inter-QPU links that can be substantially noisier than local gates. Uniformly increasing code distance provides additional protection but also enlarges data patches used for local storage and computation. Here, we introduce distributed heterogeneous-distance lattice surgery using an eight-data-patch ancilla-mediated (8-DAM) architecture, which will be useful for near-term quantum devices with less qubit overhead. In this architecture, the central ancilla spanning the inter-QPU boundary is enlarged while the data patches retain distance $d$. The protocol uses traveling stabilizers to suppress hook errors during merge and split operations between these unequal-distance patches. Circuit-level simulations of rotated surface codes at fixed local depolarizing noise show that logical-readout error rates depend only weakly on link noise. The resulting advantage over conventional lattice surgery grows as link errors increase. Comparisons with uniform distance implementations demonstrate comparable logical error suppression with reduced physical-qubit overhead. We also demonstrate how 8-DAM layouts support two simultaneous distributed logical CNOT operations between four logical data qubits using a single enlarged ancilla. At higher link noise, this construction yields lower logical error rates and more stability than two independent distributed logical CNOTs. These results support selective ancilla enlargement as a resource-efficient approach to fault-tolerant distributed quantum computing.

</details>


### [168] [Quantum Advantage for Distributed Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26788)
*Maxime Flin,Longcheng Li,Jukka Suomela*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个分布式量子算法，可在 O(1) 轮内高概率完成环的 3-着色，首次在 LOCAL 模型中给出具有渐近分布式量子优势的自然图问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前区分经典 LOCAL 与量子-LOCAL 的例子都是为展示量子优势而人为构造的问题，缺乏自然图问题上的量子优势证据。

Method: 设计分布式量子算法，在量子-LOCAL 模型中以 O(1) 轮、高概率对环进行 3-着色，并将其推广到经典 LOCAL 模型下轮复杂度为 O(log* n) 的局部可检验标记（LCL）问题。

Result: 环的 3-着色可在 O(1) 轮内高概率完成；所有经典 LOCAL 中 O(log* n) 轮的 LCL 问题（如有界度图上的极大独立集与极大匹配）均可在量子-LOCAL 中 O(1) 轮高概率求解。

Conclusion: 该工作首次给出 LOCAL 模型中具有渐近分布式量子优势的自然图问题，而非仅为展示量子优势而构造的人为问题。

Abstract: We present a distributed quantum algorithm that $3$-colors cycles in $O(1)$ rounds, with high probability. It follows that all locally checkable labeling problems (LCLs) that have round complexity $O(\log^* n)$ in the classical LOCAL model can be solved in $O(1)$ rounds in the quantum-LOCAL model, with high probability; this includes problems such as maximal independent set and maximal matching in bounded-degree graphs. This presents the first natural examples of graph problems with an asymptotic distributed quantum advantage for the LOCAL model; all prior examples that separate LOCAL and quantum-LOCAL are artificial problems constructed merely for the sake of demonstrating quantum advantage.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Elastic toroidal vector dark matter through a dark photon in the LUX-ZEPLIN high recoil window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25114)
*Imtiaz Khan,Salvatore Capozziello,G. Mustafa,Farkhod Botirov,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Farruh Atamurotov,Phongpichit Channuie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 针对LZ 2026年报道的核反冲候选事件，本文研究复矢量暗物质通过六维环状电磁矩弹性散射的物理解释，并给出多个关联观测量用于检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN 2026搜索在扩展至约270 keV的窗口内报道了一个248 keV的核反冲候选事件。作者希望考察中性复矢量暗物质通过其六维环状电磁矩进行弹性散射能否提供该反冲区域的可观谱权重，从而给出对该候选事件的物理解释与检验方案。

Method: 采用非相对论约化，发现O17与O20算符关联，将相干电荷分支与横向自旋张量分支相联系。通过暗光子解析相互作用，将高低反冲比与媒介子质量关联；构建可重整化的SU(2)_X×U(1)_D实现，用残余Z3规范对称性稳定复矢量，并通过携带物理CP相位的矢量样信使产生环状流。在基准点m_X=0.998 TeV、m_{A'}=0.450 GeV下计算GCN与JJ55谱比、同位素丰度、靶核与能量区间响应比、氩核缺失张量分支以及年度调制幅度。

Result: 对m_X=1 TeV，氙核在204.8–206.6 keV出现宽高反冲峰，并在248 keV保留该峰值的80.4–85.4%。基准点下R(248)/R(50)=1.43（GCN）、1.21（JJ55），奇A氙丰度保持在99.9%以上；350–590 keV与100–270 keV的响应水平比约为0.46。自旋为零的氩核无张量分支，年度调制维持在几个百分点量级。

Conclusion: 谱形、同位素、靶核、时间调制、媒介子及高能依赖等结果提供了对LZ反冲事件作弹性张量解释的一组关联检验条件。

Abstract: The 2026 LUX-ZEPLIN search reports one nuclear recoil candidate at $248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}~\mathrm{keV}$ in an extended window reaching about $270~\mathrm{keV}$. We consider elastic scattering of neutral complex vector dark matter through its dimension-six toroidal electromagnetic moment and find appreciable spectral weight in this recoil region. The nonrelativistic reduction correlates $\mathcal O_{17}$ and $\mathcal O_{20}$, relating a coherent charge branch to a transverse spin tensor branch. For $m_X=1~\mathrm{TeV}$, natural xenon exhibits a broad high recoil maximum at $204.8$ to $206.6~\mathrm{keV}$ and retains $80.4$ to $85.4\%$ of this maximum at $248~\mathrm{keV}$. Resolving the interaction through a dark photon relates the high-to-low recoil ratio to the mediator mass. A renormalizable $SU(2)_X\times U(1)_D$ realization stabilizes the complex vector by a residual $Z_3$ gauge symmetry and generates the toroidal current through vectorlike messengers carrying a physical CP phase. For a benchmark with $m_X=0.998~\mathrm{TeV}$ and $m_{A'}=0.450~\mathrm{GeV}$, $R(248)/R(50)=1.43$ for GCN and $1.21$ for JJ55, while the odd $A$ xenon fraction remains above $99.9\%$. The response level ratio between the $350$ to $590~\mathrm{keV}$ and $100$ to $270~\mathrm{keV}$ intervals is about $0.46$ at this benchmark. The tensor branch is absent for spin zero argon, whereas annual modulation remains at the few percent level. The resulting spectral, isotope, target, timing, mediator, and high energy dependences give correlated tests of an elastic tensor interpretation of the LZ recoil.

</details>


### [170] [System-size dependence of bottomonium suppression from Pb-Pb to light-ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25182)
*Nora Brambilla,Tom Magorsch,Antonio Vairo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在开量子系统输运框架下，对Pb-Pb、O-O与Ne-Ne碰撞中的底偶素压低进行系统尺寸依赖检验，微观输运系数沿用此前Pb-Pb分析，使用QTraj求解NLO pNRQCD Lindblad方程，成功重现O-O中观测到的顺序压低层级与量级，并量化了对输运系数和演化终止温度的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验底偶素压低是否在不同碰撞系统尺寸下具有统一的微观起源，即将Pb-Pb中提取的温度依赖输运系数推广至轻离子碰撞（O-O、Ne-Ne），以验证其在去禁闭物质中的普适性。

Method: 采用开量子系统输运框架，保留此前Pb-Pb分析得到的微观输运系数；使用QTraj在各向异性流体动力学背景中求解次领头阶（NLO）pNRQCD Lindblad方程，计算积分Υ双比值，并分析对输运系数及介质演化终止温度的敏感性。

Result: QGP诱导的演化重现了CMS和LHCb测得的O-O压低顺序层级与总体量级；Ne-Ne测量显示压低略强于理论预言，但当前实验不确定度较大；结果对输运系数和演化终止温度具有可量化的敏感性。

Conclusion: 从Pb-Pb扩展到轻离子（至少对冷核物质效应不敏感的可观测量）支持底偶素压低在去禁闭物质中具有共同微观起源，即尽管碰撞系统尺寸变化显著，仍由相同的温度依赖输运系数描述。

Abstract: We test the system-size dependence of bottomonium suppression using the same open-quantum-system transport framework for Pb-Pb, O-O, and Ne-Ne collisions. The microscopic transport coefficients are retained from previous Pb-Pb analyses. Using QTraj, we solve the next-to-leading-order pNRQCD Lindblad equation in anisotropic hydrodynamic backgrounds and calculate integrated $Υ$ double ratios. The QGP-induced evolution reproduces the observed sequential hierarchy and overall magnitude of the O-O suppression measured by CMS and LHCb. We quantify the sensitivity to both transport coefficients and to the termination temperature of the in-medium evolution. The Ne-Ne measurement favors somewhat stronger suppression than predicted, although the present experimental uncertainty is large. The extension from Pb-Pb to light ions supports, at least for observables less sensitive to cold nuclear matter effects, a common microscopic origin of bottomonium suppression in deconfined matter, characterized by the same temperature-dependent transport coefficients despite the large change in collision-system size.

</details>


### [171] [Cosmic Birefringence and Axiogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25202)
*Raymond T. Co,Lawrence J. Hall,Keisuke Harigaya,Taegyu Lee*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用轴子样场的旋转同时解释宇宙双折射信号与重子不对称，最小模型预言正的双折射角，并给出可证伪的预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期Planck与ACT的CMB数据分析报告了2-4σ的各向同性宇宙双折射迹象（β≈0.3°），对应极化面旋转，指向可能的宇称破缺新物理；同时宇宙重子不对称也需要解释。作者希望用统一机制同时解释这两个现象。

Method: 采用轴子样（axion-like）场的旋转作为核心机制：其旋转同时产生宇称破缺的极化面旋转（双折射）与重子不对称；并引入一种可在Affleck-Dine机制基础上指数增强旋转角动量的机制。

Result: 最小模型预言正的双折射角，与CMB信号一致；物质与kination主导在温度约O(3-50) MeV时结束，使模型可被天体物理探针证伪，并可能产生特征性引力波信号。

Conclusion: 轴子样场旋转可统一解释宇宙双折射与重子不对称；模型具有可证伪预言（MeV尺度主导结束）及潜在引力波信号；所提角动量指数增强机制进一步拓展了Affleck-Dine情景。

Abstract: Recent analyses of Planck and ACT CMB data report a $2\text{-}4σ$ hint of isotropic cosmic birefringence ($β\approx 0.3^\circ$), corresponding to a parity-violating rotation of polarization planes that points to potential new physics. We show that both the hint of cosmic birefringence and the observed baryon asymmetry can be simultaneously explained by the rotation of an axion-like field. The minimal setup predicts a positive birefringence angle, consistent with the CMB hint, and matter and kination domination ending around a temperature of $\mathcal O(3\text{-}50)$ MeV, making the model falsifiable by astrophysical probes and potentially giving rise to characteristic gravitational-wave signals. We also introduce a mechanism that can exponentially enhance the angular momentum of the rotation generated by the Affleck-Dine mechanism.

</details>


### [172] [Bin-to-bin correlations in the extraction of proton's transverse structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25204)
*Laurent Favart,Francesco Hautmann,Aleksandra Lelek,Louis Moureaux*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用LHC电弱玻色子产生高精度数据提取质子横向结构的非微扰QCD贡献，发现低横向动量区横动量bin间相关误差对该提取影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子横向结构相关的非微扰QCD贡献需要从实验数据中确定，而LHC电弱玻色子产生数据提供了高精度约束，但相关误差处理等系统效应可能严重影响参数提取的可靠性。

Method: 通过LHCb前向快度区Z玻色子谱的详细分析，并与CMS中心快度区测量对比；考察末态电磁辐射、光子诱导本底、前向区部分子分布大x贡献、微扰NLO匹配等对非微扰参数提取的影响。

Result: 低玻色子横向动量区的横动量bin间相关误差强烈影响非微扰QCD贡献的确定；末态电磁辐射、光子诱导本底、大x部分子分布及NLO匹配等效应均对参数提取产生影响。

Conclusion: 非微扰QCD贡献的提取对相关误差和多种系统效应高度敏感，需谨慎处理；结果与部分子簇射蒙特卡洛事件发生器底层事件调优结果进行了对比讨论。

Abstract: We investigate the determination of non-perturbative QCD contributions, associated with proton's transverse structure, from high-precision experimental data for electroweak boson production at the LHC. We demonstrate that these determinations are strongly affected by correlated uncertainties across transverse-momentum bins in the region of low boson's transverse momenta. We illustrate our results through a detailed analysis of LHCb measurements of $Z$-boson spectra at forward rapidities, and a comparison with CMS measurements at central rapidities. We further examine the influence on the extraction of the non-perturbative parameters from final-state electromagnetic radiation, photon-induced background, large-$x$ contributions to parton distributions in the forward region, matching to perturbative next-to-leading order. We comment on the comparison of our results with those from the underlying-event tuning of parton-shower Monte Carlo event generators.

</details>


### [173] [Limitations of quantum tomography in Higgs-boson decays to four leptons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25217)
*Morgan Del Gratta,Fabio Maltoni,Davide Pagani,Giovanni Pelliccioli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究Higgs玻色子衰变到四带电轻子中双qutrit量子层析的局限性：次领头阶标准重建可能给出非半正定算符，但秩二角向部分仍可稳健地给出平方concurrence的下界，从而在更高级阶可靠判定纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Higgs→4轻子过程中，量子纠缠的判定依赖量子层析重建密度算符。此前分析多停留在领头阶，需检验在次领头阶（NLO）下标准重建方法是否仍然有效，以及纠缠判定能否在辐射修正下保持可靠。

Method: 在次领头阶对Higgs衰变到四带电轻子的双qutrit量子层析进行分析，采用（辐射改进的）自旋分析幂，考察重建密度算符的半正定性。将失效根源追溯到光子与Z介导贡献对离壳双轻子系统的相干叠加，分析其产生与衰变结构差异对通用衰变分析器假设的破坏，并提取秩二角向部分。

Result: NLO下标准重建可给出非半正定算符，即使使用辐射改进的自旋分析幂也如此；问题源于光子和Z贡献的相干叠加，其不同的产生与衰变结构使通用衰变分析器假设失效。秩二角向部分仍然可及且微扰稳定，可给出平方concurrence的稳健下界。

Conclusion: 双qutrit层析在NLO下不完全适用，但通过秩二角向部分可对平方concurrence给出可靠下界，从而在超出领头阶的水平上稳健判定纠缠。

Abstract: We investigate the limitations of the two-qutrit quantum tomography in Higgs-boson decays to four charged leptons. At next-to-leading order, the standard reconstruction can yield an operator that is not positive semidefinite, even when radiatively improved spin-analysing powers are used. We trace this failure to the coherent combination of photon- and Z-mediated contributions to the off-shell dilepton system, whose distinct production and decay structures invalidate the assumed universal decay analysers. Nevertheless, the rank-two angular sector remains accessible and perturbatively stable, enabling a robust lower bound on the squared concurrence, and hence a reliable assessment of entanglement beyond leading order.

</details>


### [174] [A Unitary Fixed Point Away from Threshold: Momentum-Surface EFT for Strong Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25219)
*Lorenzo De Ros,Javier Reig Navarro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 非相对论辐射俘获即使底层跃迁相互作用弱，也可在有限动量k0附近因散射态与束缚态强混合而变得非微扰，破坏幺正性；将此增强因子化后得到强局域耦合，经RG流向幺正不动点，可用围绕|k|=k0的线性色散幺正费米子EFT系统处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为辐射俘获由弱电磁跃迁主导，可微扰处理。本文指出在非相对论体系中，即便跃迁相互作用弱，只要散射态与束缚态在有限动量附近发生强混合，也会出现非微扰效应并破坏幺正性，因此需要一个系统框架来描述这种情形。

Method: 构建含系统幂次计数的两通道量子力学模型；识别有限动量k0处散射-束缚态混合导致的微扰幺正性破坏；将增强贡献因子化，约化为自由散射态通过强局域耦合相互作用；对因子化标度做重整化群演化，并据此构造围绕|k|=k0面、线性色散关系的幺正费米子有效场论。

Result: 在因子化标度下重整化群演化显示局域相互作用趋于幺正不动点，类似于散射长度反常大的系统；有效场论的重求和恢复幺正性，并给出弹性散射与辐射俘获的系统展开。

Conclusion: 辐射俘获的非微扰行为可由有限动量面附近的强混合与因子化局域相互作用描述，其普适性由幺正不动点控制；围绕|k|=k0的线性色散幺正费米子EFT为弹性散射和辐射俘获提供系统性微扰展开。

Abstract: Radiative capture in a non-relativistic system can become non-perturbative even when the underlying transition interaction is weak. We demonstrate this mechanism in a simple quantum-mechanical two-channel model with a systematic power counting, where strong mixing between scattering and bound states develops in a narrow region around a finite momentum $k_0$, leading to a perturbative violation of unitarity. In this regime, the enhanced contributions factorize, reducing the dynamics to a free scattering state interacting through a strong local coupling. Under RG evolution in the factorization scale, this local interaction approaches a unitary fixed point, analogous to that of systems with an anomalously large scattering length. This motivates an EFT organized around the finite-momentum surface $|\mathbf{k}|=k_0$, describing fermions at unitarity with a linear dispersion relation. The resummation resulting from the EFT restores unitarity and provides a systematic expansion for both elastic scattering and radiative capture.

</details>


### [175] [Fast Surrogate for the Earth Matter Effect on Solar Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25229)
*Saeed Ansarifard*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种深度残差卷积网络代理模型，用于快速近似太阳中微子地球物质效应引起的跃迁概率，精度约2%，加速约60倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳中微子地球物质效应的精确计算需频繁进行数值求解，计算成本较高。作者希望用深度学习代理模型提供快速近似，并保持对振荡参数的依赖，从而加速太阳中微子分析。

Method: 构建残差卷积神经网络，以太阳中微子振荡参数为条件输入，在参数网格上生成的数值解上训练。网络学习地球穿越传播过程，预测中微子能量与天顶角下的地球诱导跃迁概率。代理模型仅学习地球穿越传播，可无需重训练应用于不改变该传播的新物理场景，并可用其他数值实现或扩展参数集重新训练。

Result: 以参考电子中微子存活概率为例，代理模型达到约2%的逐点相对精度，在笔记本CPU测试中实现约60倍加速。代码与模拟数据已公开。

Conclusion: 该代理模型为太阳中微子分析提供了一种灵活、计算高效的方法，可快速近似地球物质效应，并具备向新物理场景和扩展参数集迁移的潜力。

Abstract: We present a deep-learning surrogate for the Earth matter effect on solar neutrinos. The model uses a residual convolutional network conditioned on the solar neutrino oscillation parameters and is trained on numerical solutions computed over a grid of parameter values. It predicts the Earth-induced transition probabilities across neutrino energy and zenith angle, providing a fast approximation to the direct numerical calculation. For the reference electron-neutrino survival probability, the surrogate achieves a pointwise relative accuracy of approximately $2\%$ and a speed-up of about a factor of 60 in a laptop-CPU test. Since the network learns only the Earth-crossing propagation, it can be applied without retraining to new-physics scenarios that leave this propagation unchanged. The surrogate can also be retrained using alternative numerical implementations or extended parameter sets, providing a flexible and computationally efficient approach for solar-neutrino analyses. The code and simulation data are publicly available at \href{https://github.com/AI-Driven-HEP/NuMatterSurrogate}{\faGithub}.

</details>


### [176] [Cosmological stasis and the coupled dark sector of the Dark Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25230)
*Alexander Stewart*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究耦合暗物质-暗能量模型中的宇宙学"停滞"(stasis)现象：以暗维度为动机，将暗物质、暗能量、重子与辐射视为自治动力系统，证明精确停滞需要沿轨迹的指数斜率，跑动斜率是否可积决定精确停滞或漂移准停滞；停滞解对应耦合精质(quintessence)的定常解，具可计算寿命与退出；将宇宙学锚定于物质-辐射等量及今天的丰度后，停滞区在任何初始条件下都无法加速，总能状态方程永不为幽灵型;对暗维度，施加暗第五力约束后几何势的未来停滞捕获被排除，而标准模型膜上的暗物质产生支持标准情景无法达到的额外停滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合暗物质-暗能量模型中停滞现象值得研究，且暗维度提供了具体动机；停滞解可能提供不依赖初始条件的参数空间解析排除，这具有观测意义。

Method: 将暗物质、暗能量、重子与辐射作为自治动力系统处理，研究精确停滞所需的斜率条件，区分可积跑动（精确停滞）与不可积跑动（漂移准停滞）；识别常丰度解为耦合精质定常解并赋予寿命与退出；用物质-辐射等量及今日丰度锚定参数。

Result: 精确停滞需要指数斜率；跑动斜率若可积给出精确停滞，否则漂移准停滞；停滞区在任何初始条件下均不能加速；总能状态方程永不幽灵化，尽管ΛCDM拟合中的有效暗能量可看似幽灵；对暗维度，施加暗第五力约束后几何势的未来停滞捕获被排除，暗物质产生支持额外停滞。

Conclusion: 停滞解提供不依赖初始条件的参数空间解析排除，对耦合暗能量模型与暗维度情景具有判别力。

Abstract: We study cosmological stasis in coupled dark matter and dark energy models in which a scalar field drives the dark energy and sets the dark matter mass, with the Dark Dimension as the motivating realization. Treating dark matter, dark energy, baryons, and radiation as an autonomous dynamical system, we show that exact stasis requires exponential slopes along the trajectory, and that running slopes give exact stasis or a drifting quasi-stasis depending on whether the running is integrable. The constant abundance solutions are the stationary solutions of coupled quintessence, which we read as stasis epochs with computable lifetimes and exits. Anchoring the cosmology to the measured abundances at matter-radiation equality and today, we show that the stasis region cannot accelerate for any initial conditions, and that the total equation of state is never phantom although the effective dark energy of a $Λ$CDM fit can appear so. For the Dark Dimension, capture into stasis in the future is excluded for geometric sources of the potential once dark fifth-force bounds are imposed, and dark matter production from the Standard Model brane supports a further stasis which the standard scenario does not reach. The stasis solutions provide analytic exclusions of the parameter space which do not depend on initial conditions.

</details>


### [177] [Restoring minimal modular neutrino masses via Kähler $R$-parity violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25592)
*Anjan S. Joshipura,Ketan M. Patel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新分析了在模块对称框架下用单一模形式多重态描述中微子质量的可能性，指出在局域超对称下，Feruglio的模块A4最小模型中的超场通过Kähler势会产生额外的R宇称破坏贡献，从而影响中微子质量与混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Feruglio模型以模块A4对称性用单一模形式三重态Y(τ)描述中微子质量和混合，是最小化方案；但在局域超对称框架下，超场本身的Kähler势可能引入未被考虑的额外效应，因此需要重新审视该模型是否完整，并考察这些新贡献对中微子现象学的影响。

Method: 作者在局域超对称框架下分析模块A4模型，其中包含手征 flavon 三重态φ（原文"flavon"表述略有笔误），中微子质量与混合由模形式超场三重态Y(τ)决定。重点考察Kähler势贡献，导出R宇称破坏效应表现为有效超势中的双线性项ε_i L_i H_u，且ε_i由Y(τ)及其导数确定；同时Kähler势使中微子与φ的费米子分量混合，当这些费米子有质量时产生 seesaw 中微子质量。分两种情形分析：中微子通过Weinberg算符获得质量，或通过φ诱导的seesaw获得质量。

Result: 发现Kähler势导致的R宇称破坏和φ费米子混合会给出额外中微子质量贡献。与原始模型不同，加入R破坏贡献后，两种情形下均能精确复现中微子混合角和质量比，且对正序和倒序都适用；其中Weinberg算符情形预测的中微子质量之和接近宇宙学上限，而seesaw机制情形预测的质量之和则舒适地低于该上限。

Conclusion: 在局域超对称的模块A4最小模型中，仅靠Y(τ)描述中微子质量并不完整，Kähler势引入的R宇称破坏项ε_i L_i H_u及与φ费米子混合效应不可忽略；纳入这些贡献后模型可同时拟合正序与倒序的中微子混合角和质量比，且两种质量生成机制给出可区分的宇宙学中微子质量总和预言。

Abstract: The possibility of describing neutrino masses through a single multiplet of modular forms in a modular symmetric framework is reanalysed. The minimal model achieving this is Feruglio's model based on modular $A_4$ symmetry, which contains a chiral flavon triplet $φ$, with all neutrino masses and mixing determined by a superfield triplet of modular forms $Y(τ)$. We point out that, in local supersymmetry, these superfields by themselves allow an additional $R$-parity-violating contribution to neutrino masses originating from the Kähler potential of the model. The $R$ violation manifests through a bilinear term $ε_i L_i H_u$ in the effective superpotential, with parameters $ε_i$ determined in terms of $Y(τ)$ and its derivatives. The Kähler potential also mixes neutrinos with the fermionic component of $φ$, leading to seesaw neutrino masses when these fermions are massive. Two separate cases are analysed, with neutrinos obtaining mass through the Weinberg operator or through the $φ$-induced seesaw. Unlike the original model, adding the $R$-violating contribution allows accurate reproduction of neutrino mixing angles and mass ratios for both the normal and inverted orderings in either case, with the Weinberg operator predicting a sum of neutrino masses close to the cosmological limit and the seesaw mechanism predicting ones comfortably below it.

</details>


### [178] [Electroweak phase transition triggered by a long-lived scalar and LHC searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25601)
*Lei Wang,Xiaoting Zhaoa,Xiao-Fang Han,Hongxin Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究单态标量扩展标准模型中长寿命标量S触发的强一阶电弱相变，分析其引力波信号与LHC及远距离探测器灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究长寿命标量S引发强一阶电弱相变（SFOEWPT）的参数空间，并评估引力波探测与LHC探测器对该参数空间的探测能力。

Method: 在单态标量扩展SM框架下，通过分析有限温度有效势确定实现SFOEWPT的参数空间，计算引力波谱，并研究LHC主探测器（ATLAS）及远距离探测器（ANUBIS、CODEX-b、FACET、FASER2、MoEDAL-MAPP1/2、MATHUSLA）对长寿命S的灵敏度，主导产生道为gg→h*→SS。

Result: S与希格斯混合角极小使S寿命长；部分SFOEWPT参数空间可被LISA探测；主探测器与远距离探测器联合灵敏度（尤其ANUBIS和MATHUSLA）可在10^-10≤sinθ≤10^-5、m_S达250 GeV范围内探测大部分SFOEWPT参数空间。

Conclusion: 对于sinθ≤10^-11，SFOEWPT参数空间对主探测器和远距离探测器均不可及。

Abstract: We investigate a long-lived scalar $S$ that triggers a strongly first-order electroweak phase transition (SFOEWPT) in the singlet scalar extension of standard model (SM). A sufficient small mixing angle between $S$ and the SM-like Higgs boson $h$ naturally leads to a long lifetime for $S$. We focus on the mass range $m_S>m_h/2$, where the dominant production channel at the LHC is the Higgs-mediated process $gg\to h^*\to SS$ with an off-shell $h$. By analyzing the finite-temperature effective potential, we identify the parameter space realizing an SFOEWPT and calculate the associated gravitational-wave spectra, finding that part of the parameter space can be probed by LISA. We further study the LHC sensitivity to the long-lived $S$ using the ATLAS detector and proposed far detectors, including ANUBIS, CODEX-b, FACET, FASER2, MoEDAL-MAPP1 and MAPP2, and MATHUSLA. The combined sensitivity of the main detector and far detectors, particularly ANUBIS and MATHUSLA, allows a large fraction of the SFOEWPT parameter space to be probed for $10^{-10}\leq \sinθ\leq 10^{-5}$, with $m_S$ up to $250~\mathrm{GeV}$. For $\sinθ\leq 10^{-11}$, however, the SFOEWPT parameter space becomes inaccessible to both the main detector and the far-detector searches.

</details>


### [179] [Dark Diffraction at LZ from a Screened Neutral Composite Baryon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25723)
*Hyunjoo Jung,Seong Chan Park*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 复合暗物质的中性重子可通过其弹性形状因子在较高动量转移处产生硬核反冲，解释LZ实验248 keV候选事件，无需非弹性质量劈裂或加速暗物质族群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN实验报告了一个能量约248 keV的类核反冲候选事件，常规解释通常需要非弹性质量劈裂或加速暗物质族群，作者希望寻找更自然的物理机制来解释这一高能反冲。

Method: 将复合暗物质建模为N个暗夸克组成的电中性重子，利用其在低动量转移处被压低、高动量转移处恢复的电荷形状因子作为动量空间带通滤波器；用每个带电组分一个高斯函数描述形状因子，检验不同项数对硬反冲谱的影响；并以禁闭SU(4)_D×U(1)_D规范理论中由四个暗夸克组成的中性玻色重子作为具体实现。

Result: 证明两项形状因子无法产生硬反冲谱，三项在LZ信号窗口内事件过少，四项可以满足；在200 GeV基准质量、归一化到一个接受事件的条件下，预测约1.5个超出LZ当前感兴趣区域的真反冲事件；形状因子在固定动量转移处产生衍射节点，其对应反冲能量随靶核质量按1/m_A标度。

Conclusion: 复合暗物质的弹性形状因子可自然产生LZ观测到的高能类核反冲事件，无需引入非弹性劈裂或加速族群，且衍射节点提供了可跨不同靶核检验的预言特征。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment recently reported a nuclear-recoil-like candidate at $E_R=248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}$ keV. We show that such an energetic recoil can arise from the elastic form factor of composite dark matter, without invoking an inelastic mass splitting or a boosted population. A neutral baryon of $N$ dark quarks can have a charge form factor that is suppressed at low momentum transfer but recovers at higher momentum transfer, providing a momentum-space band-pass filter. Modeling the form factor with one Gaussian per charged constituent species, we prove that two terms cannot produce a hard recoil spectrum and find that three yield too few events in the LZ signal window, while four suffice. A confining $SU(4)_D\times U(1)_D$ gauge theory provides a concrete realization through a neutral bosonic baryon made of four dark quarks. For a 200 GeV benchmark normalized to one accepted event, we predict approximately 1.5 true recoils beyond the current LZ region of interest. The form factor also produces diffraction nodes at fixed momentum transfers, placing them at recoil energies that scale as $1/m_A$ across target nuclei.

</details>


### [180] [Impact parameter and transverse charge densities of the pion and kaon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25780)
*Fernando Chandra,Parada T. P. Hutauruk,Terry Mart*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在协变NJL模型下，利用非零skewness的矢量GPD研究π介子和K介子的价夸克分布，发现增大ξ使分布向小x移动、减小b⊥=0处中心幅值并展宽横向轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞参数与横向电荷密度可提供强子结构的空间分辨信息，并探测动力学手征对称性破缺与SU(3)味道对称性破缺之间的相互作用。该研究旨在深入理解π介子和K介子的空间结构，为非微扰QCD动力学提供严格检验，并与Jefferson Lab和电子离子对撞机的未来测量相关联。

Method: 采用协变Nambu–Jona-Lasinio（NJL）模型，使用Schwinger固有时（proper-time）方案正则化紫外发散，计算π介子和K介子在非零skewness ξ下的非极化矢量广义部分子分布（GPDs），进而得到价夸克分布。

Result: 随着ξ增大，价夸克分布向更小的x移动，b⊥=0处的中心幅值降低，横向剖面变宽。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了π介子和K介子的空间结构，为非微扰QCD动力学提供了严格检验，并对Jefferson Lab与电子离子对撞机的即将开展的测量具有参考意义。

Abstract: The impact parameter and transverse charge densities provide spatially resolved information on hadron structure and probe the interplay between dynamical chiral symmetry breaking and SU(3)-flavor symmetry breaking. We investigate the valence-quark distributions of the pion and kaon using their unpolarized vector GPDs at nonzero skewness $ξ$ within the covariant Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model, with ultraviolet divergences regulated by the Schwinger proper-time scheme. Increasing $ξ$ shifts the distributions toward smaller $x$, reduces their central magnitude at $b_\perp=0$, and broadens their transverse profiles. These results provide insight into the spatial structure of the pion and kaon and offer stringent tests of nonperturbative QCD dynamics, with relevance to forthcoming measurements at Jefferson Lab and the Electron-Ion Collider.

</details>


### [181] [Quasi-Gaussian Missing Higher Order Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25824)
*Marco Bonvini,Emanuele Bagnaschi,Lorenzo Paparella*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文综述了用于估计微扰预测中缺失高阶项不确定性的贝叶斯模型，并通过修改似然与先验，获得形状近似高斯的平滑概率分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微扰理论预测中缺失高阶项会引入不确定性，而常用的贝叶斯模型给出的概率分布形状往往与物理分析中普遍假设的高斯分布显著不同，这给实际分析带来不便。

Method: 系统回顾相关贝叶斯模型，并对其似然函数和先验分布进行适当修改。

Result: 修改后可得到平滑且呈准高斯形状的概率分布。

Conclusion: 通过调整贝叶斯模型的似然与先验，可使缺失高阶项不确定性所对应的概率分布更接近物理分析中常用的高斯形式。

Abstract: We review Bayesian models for estimating the uncertainty induced by missing higher orders in perturbative predictions. These models return probability distributions, which have peculiar shapes typically very different from the Gaussian distribution often assumed in physics analysis. In this work, we are able to obtain smooth probability distributions with quasi-Gaussians shapes by suitably modifying the likelihood and the priors of the models.

</details>


### [182] [Double integral representation in the invariant masses of a composite system and nonperturbative calculation of the asymptotics of light-meson electromagnetic form factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25840)
*A. F. Krutov,E. V. Razueva,V. E. Troitsky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于复合系统不变质量的双重积分表示，非微扰地推导了π介子和ρ介子电磁形状因子在大类空动量转移下的渐近展开，并发现当组分夸克质量趋于零且视为点粒子时，ρ介子纵向极化形状因子与π介子形状因子在螺旋度基下的渐近比值与微扰QCD（pQCD）预言在所用参数范围内一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在非微扰框架下，π和ρ介子电磁形状因子的大动量转移渐近行为能否与微扰QCD的预言相符，从而连接非微扰与微扰描述。

Method: 采用复合系统不变质量的双重积分表示来计算形状因子，模型参数沿用作者早期关于介子电弱性质所固定的取值，并取组分夸克质量趋于零、夸克视为点粒子的极限。

Result: 获得了π和ρ介子电磁形状因子在Q^2趋于无穷时的非微扰渐近展开；在零组分夸克质量极限下，ρ介子纵向极化与π介子形状因子在螺旋度基下的渐近比值，在所用参数范围内与pQCD预言一致。

Conclusion: 非微扰计算在特定极限和参数范围内可以重现pQCD对介子形状因子渐近比值的预言，表明所采用的双重积分表示模型具有一定的自洽性。

Abstract: We obtain nonperturbative asymptotic expansions of the electromagnetic form factors of the pi and rho mesons at large spacelike momentum transfer, Q^2->\infty. The calculation is based on a double integral representation in the invariant masses of the composite system. The model parameters are kept at the values fixed in the authors' earlier description of meson electroweak properties. We show that, in the limit of vanishing constituent-quark mass, with the constituent quarks taken to be pointlike, the asymptotic ratio of the longitudinally polarized rho-meson to pion form factors in the helicity basis is consistent with the prediction of perturbative QCD (pQCD) within the adopted parameter ranges.

</details>


### [183] [Chiral anomaly and nucleon spin decomposition in the chiral quark soliton model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25867)
*Josuke Minamiguchi,Tomoya Uji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过引入手征反常产生质量的味道单态赝标量场扩展手征夸克孤子模型，研究反常对核子自旋分解（夸克自旋与轨道角动量）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究手征反常如何影响核子自旋在夸克自旋与轨道运动之间的分解，即反常效应对自旋分解的贡献。

Method: 扩展手征夸克孤子模型，加入由手征反常生成质量的味道单态赝标量场；计算夸克与反夸克螺旋度及轨道角动量分布，并计入孤子旋转诱导的单态场；在固定其余模型参数条件下改变反常诱导质量，考察其对自旋分解的影响。

Result: 诱导场相对于仅含π介子的传统模型增强了夸克螺旋度贡献；增大反常诱导质量会抑制该增强并提高轨道贡献，而两部分之和仍能解释核子自旋；在所考察参数范围内，这种再分配幅度温和，未定性改变传统模型的自旋分解。

Conclusion: 手征反常引起的自旋再分配效应存在但较为温和，不改变传统模型自旋分解的定性图像。

Abstract: We investigate how the chiral anomaly influences the decomposition of nucleon spin between quark spin and orbital motion. We extend the chiral quark soliton model by including a flavor-singlet pseudoscalar field whose mass is generated by the chiral anomaly. Within this framework, we calculate the quark and antiquark helicity and orbital angular momentum distributions, accounting for the singlet field induced by the rotation of the soliton. We vary the anomaly-induced mass while keeping the remaining model parameters fixed to examine how the anomaly affects the spin decomposition. The induced field enhances the quark helicity contribution relative to the conventional pion-only model. Increasing the mass suppresses this enhancement and increases the orbital contribution, while the two contributions continue to account for the nucleon spin. This redistribution remains modest over the parameter values considered and does not qualitatively alter the spin decomposition of the conventional model.

</details>


### [184] [Big Bang Nucleosynthesis Confronts Domain Walls and First-Order Phase Transitions as the Pulsar Timing Array Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25950)
*Fatemeh Elahi,Diego Fortini,Yann Gouttenoire,Nicholas Leister,Pedro Schwaller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）探测到的纳赫兹引力波背景可由湮灭畴壁或一阶相变解释，其再加热过程会留下重子等曲率涨落，影响大爆炸核合成产额，从而缓解氘丰度张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTA探测到的纳赫兹引力波背景常被归因于湮灭畴壁（DW）或一阶相变（FOPT），但这些解释存在不确定性；同时宇宙学中存在氘丰度（D/H）张力问题。本文旨在利用再加热产生的重子等曲率涨落来检验并强化这些PTA解释。

Method: 分析湮灭畴壁或一阶相变再加热过程中产生的重子等曲率涨落 $S_B$。这些涨落存活至大爆炸核合成（BBN）时期，而BBN产额对 $S_B$ 呈非线性依赖，据此评估其对氘丰度的影响及对PTA参数空间的约束。

Result: 适度的 $S_B$ 涨落可在部分PTA偏好区域内缓解当前的氘丰度张力，从而增强畴壁和一阶相变解释的可信度；而过量的畴壁和过强的一阶相变会过度产生 D/H，因而被排除。

Conclusion: 重子等曲率涨落为连接PTA引力波背景与大爆炸核合成观测提供了新探针，既能缓解氘张力支持部分PTA解释，又能对畴壁和一阶相变模型给出约束。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) have detected a nanohertz gravitational-wave background, often attributed to annihilating domain walls (DWs) or first-order phase transitions (FOPTs). Their reheating imprints fluctuations in the baryon-to-photon ratio. These so-called baryon isocurvature fluctuations $S_B$ survive until Big Bang Nucleosynthesis, whose yields depend nonlinearly on it. Modest $S_B$ fluctuations can therefore alleviate the current deuterium tension in parts of the PTA preferred regions and therefore strengthen their interpretation. Overabundant DWs and too strong FOPTs overproduce $D/H$ and are ruled out.

</details>


### [185] [Effects of super-Gaussian pulse shape and relative phase on dynamically assisted pair production in spatially inhomogeneous electric fields with frequency chirping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26013)
*Abhinav Jangir,Anees Ahmed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在(1+1)维DHW框架下研究了超高斯脉冲形状对空间非均匀、含啁啾电场中动态辅助电子对产生的影响，发现啁啾、脉冲形状、空间非均匀性与动态辅助之间的耦合可显著调控对产生动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超高斯脉冲形状结合频率啁啾、空间非均匀性及动态辅助（双色场）对电子对产生动力学的调控作用，为在给定场参数范围内优化动态辅助对产生提供参考。

Method: 采用(1+1)维Dirac-Heisenberg-Wigner (DHW)形式化方法，分别研究单色场与动态辅助双色组合场，变化啁啾参数、超高斯脉冲形状及外场空间尺度，结合相对相位作为额外控制参数，分析约化动量分布与总粒子产额。

Result: 啁啾显著提高绝对对产额并重塑动量谱，弱分量啁啾尤为有效，两分量同时啁啾产额最大；超高斯阶数对强场产生影响较小，但增加脉冲平顶特性会增强弱场及动态辅助区（尤其含啁啾时）的谱结构与动量重分布；相对相位影响随空间尺度增大而更明显，导致动量空间结构显著变化。

Conclusion: 脉冲整形、空间非均匀性、频率啁啾与动态辅助的相互作用可有效调控对产生动力学，结果为在时空依赖电场中实现动态辅助对产生的优化控制提供了有用参考。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of super-Gaussian pulse shapes on dynamically assisted pair production in spatially inhomogeneous electric fields with frequency chirping within the framework of the (1+1)-dimensional Dirac-Heisenberg-Wigner formalism. The analysis is carried out for both single-color fields and dynamically assisted two-color combined fields by varying the chirp parameter, super-Gaussian pulse shape, and the spatial scale of the external field. Our results are presented in terms of the reduced momentum distribution and the total particle yield, revealing that the interplay between pulse shaping, spatial inhomogeneity, frequency chirping, and dynamical assistance can significantly modify the pair-production dynamics. In particular, chirping substantially enhances the absolute pair-production yield and reshapes the momentum spectrum, with chirping of the weak component being particularly effective, while simultaneous chirping of both components produces the largest yields. The super-Gaussian order has a relatively modest influence on strong-field production, while increasing the flat-top character of the pulse enhances spectral structures and momentum redistribution in the weak and dynamically assisted regimes, particularly under chirping. Finally, we show that the relative phase between the two field components provides an additional control parameter, with its influence becoming more pronounced for larger spatial scales leading to significant changes in the momentum-space structure. Overall, our results provide a useful reference for optimal control of dynamically assisted pair production in space- and time-dependent electric fields within a prescribed range of field parameters.

</details>


### [186] [Testable neutrino mass and TeV-scale leptogenesis in a $D_4$ inverse seesaw model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26027)
*Qiu Yan,Yakefu Reyimuaji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于D4味对称性自发破缺的逆跷跷板模型，可统一解释中微子振荡、TeV尺度轻子生成与带电轻子味破坏，兼具可检验性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中微子质量与混合机制需同时解释振荡数据、宇宙重子不对称以及可观测的带电轻子味破坏过程，本文旨在构建一个能统一关联低能中微子观测量与TeV尺度物理的可检验框架。

Method: 构建逆跷跷板模型，引入D4味对称性并使其自发破缺；结合唯象分析，检验正常与倒置质量排序下的中微子振荡数据、多TeV尺度最轻赝狄拉克中微子质量下的轻子生成，以及cLFV分支比预言。

Result: 模型在正常和倒置质量排序下均与当前中微子振荡数据一致；最轻赝狄拉克中微子质量处于多TeV尺度时可成功实现轻子生成；预言的cLFV分支比远低于当前实验上限，但处于下一代实验灵敏度范围内。

Conclusion: 该模型是可行且可检验的框架，将低能中微子观测量与TeV尺度轻子生成及cLFV唯象学联系起来。

Abstract: An inverse seesaw model for neutrino masses and mixing is proposed, based on the spontaneous breaking of a $D_4$ flavor symmetry. The model simultaneously accounts for the observed neutrino oscillation pattern, the baryon asymmetry of the Universe through TeV-scale leptogenesis, and potentially observable charged-lepton flavor violating (cLFV) processes.
