A phenomenological analysis shows that the model is consistent with current neutrino oscillation data for both normal and inverted mass orderings. Successful leptogenesis is realized for lightest pseudo-Dirac neutrino masses at the multi-TeV scale. The predicted cLFV branching ratios lie well below the current experimental upper limits while remaining within the sensitivity reach of next-generation experiments. These results establish the model as a viable and testable framework that links low-energy neutrino observables to TeV-scale leptogenesis and cLFV phenomenology.

</details>


### [187] [Nonthermal fixed point for massless scalar field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26491)
*Viktoria Noel,Chuang Huang,Aleksandr N. Mikheev,Jürgen Berges*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用大N展开至次领头阶，计算了远离平衡的无质量标量场论的普适标度指数与标度函数，得到能量与有效粒子数输运的幂律尾部指数，并证明相对论与非相对论理论不属于同一普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究远离平衡态无质量标量场论中红外区的非微扰输运行为，并厘清相对论与非相对论情况是否具有相同普适性。

Method: 采用大N展开至次领头阶（NLO），分析能量或有效粒子数向低频ω与低动量p̄输运所关联的非微扰红外行为；对无质量色散ω=p̄，确定分布函数的标度形式及其普适幂律尾部f_S(p̄)～1/p̄^κ。

Result: 在空间维度d=3时，能量输运给出κ=d+1，有效粒子数输运给出κ=d；相对论理论与非相对论理论在该情形下不属于同一普适类。

Conclusion: 结果为高能物理及具线性色散的（量子）多体物理中远离平衡现象开辟了新的应用方向。

Abstract: We compute universal scaling exponents and scaling functions for massless scalar field theories far from equilibrium. Using a large-$N$ expansion to next-to-leading order, we investigate the nonperturbative infrared behaviour associated with the transport of a) energy or b) effective particle number towards low frequencies $ω$ and momenta $\bar p$. For massless dispersion $ω= \bar{p}$, we determine the scaling form of the distribution function whose universal power-law tail $f_S(\bar p) \sim 1/\bar{p}^κ$ is found to be a) $κ= d+1$ and b) $κ= d$ in $d=3$ spatial dimensions. Our results establish that relativistic and nonrelativistic theories do not belong to the same universality class in this case, which opens up new applications to far-from-equilibrium phenomena in high-energy and (quantum) many-body physics with linear dispersions.

</details>


### [188] [Semileptonic Decays of Heavy Vector Mesons in Hard-Wall AdS/QCD with $N_{f}=5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26040)
*S. Momeni,M. Saghebfar,A. Ranjbar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在硬墙AdS/QCD框架下系统研究了多种重矢量介子的半轻子衰变，计算跃迁形状因子并给出分支比预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半轻子衰变中轻子流已知，强子部分编码于反映非微扰QCD动力学的跃迁形状因子中，是检验标准模型的干净环境，因此需要系统研究重矢量介子此类衰变的形状因子与分支比。

Method: 采用硬墙AdS/QCD模型，对涵盖c→d、b→c、c→s、b→u四类夸克层次跃迁的十四个衰变过程，计算作为动量转移q²函数的跃迁形状因子；利用该框架给出的介子波函数与衰变常数解析表达式作为输入，进而求微分与积分分支比。

Result: 得到各衰变模式的跃迁形状因子及对应的微分、积分分支比预言，并在可能情况下与其它非微扰方法的结果进行对比。

Conclusion: 该工作为重矢量介子半轻子衰变提供了基于硬墙AdS/QCD的系统性预言，覆盖广泛味道动力学，可与其它非微扰方法相互检验。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of semileptonic decays of heavy vector mesons within the Hard-Wall AdS/QCD framework. Semileptonic decays provide a clean environment for testing the Standard Model because the leptonic current is well understood, while the hadronic part is encoded in transition form factors that reflect non-perturbative QCD dynamics. The processes under consideration include: \( ψ\to D^{*-} ( D^{-} ) \ell^{+} ν_{\ell} \), \( D^{*0} \to π^{-} \ell^{+} ν_{\ell} \), \( D^{*+}_{s} \to K^{0} \ell^{+} ν_{\ell} \), \( D^{*0} \to ρ^{-} \ell^{+} ν_{\ell} \), \( B_c^{*+} \to B^{*0} \ell^{+} ν_{\ell} \), \( B_c^{*+} \to ψ\,\ell^{+} ν_{\ell} \), \( B^{*-} \to D^{*0} \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell} \), \( B^{*0} \to D^{*+}(D^{+}) \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell} \), \( B^{*0}_{s} \to D^{*+}_{s} (D^{+}_{s}) \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell} \), \( ψ\to D^{*-}_{s}(D^{-}_{s}) \ell^{+} ν_{\ell} \), \( D^{*0} \to K^{*-} (K^{-}) \ell^{+} ν_{\ell} \), \( B_c^{*-} \to D^{*0} \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell} \), \( \bar{B_{s}}^{*0} \to K^{*+} \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell} \), and \( \bar{B}^{*0} \to ρ^{+} \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell} \). These decays correspond to four different quark-level transitions: \(c\to d\), \(b\to c\), \(c\to s\), and \(b\to u\), allowing us to explore a broad range of flavor dynamics.\\ Using the Hard-Wall AdS/QCD model, we compute the relevant transition form factors as functions of the momentum transfer \(q^{2}\). The framework provides analytic expressions for meson wave functions and decay constants, which serve as inputs for the form-factor calculations. From these form factors, we evaluate the differential and integrated branching ratios for each decay mode. Where possible, we compare our predictions with results obtained from other non-perturbative approaches.

</details>


### [189] [Singlet Extended Mirror Standard Model World as Dark Matter and Gravitational Waves Imprints of a High Scale Mirror Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26271)
*Asterios T. Papadopoulos,V. K. Oikonomou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究一个包含高能标复制标准模型的镜像标准模型世界，在镜像希格斯与镜像单态标量耦合中引入六维非重整化算符，发现电弱相变是强一阶相变，其气泡碰撞产生的引力波可被LISA、BBO和DECIGO探测，且镜像粒子与原子可能构成宇宙全部暗物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索镜像标准模型扩展中电弱相变性质及引力波信号的可探测性，并考察镜像扇区作为暗物质的可能性。

Method: 构建含高能标标准模型复制品及单态镜像标量扩展的镜像世界，在镜像希格斯与镜像单态标量相互作用中加入六维非重整化算符，分析该镜像单态扩展世界的电弱相变，并对镜像单态丰度进行粗略估计。

Result: 该电弱相变为强一阶相变，其气泡碰撞产生的引力波信号可被LISA、BBO和DECIGO引力波实验探测；粗略估计显示宇宙全部暗物质可能由镜像粒子和原子组成。

Conclusion: 镜像单态扩展世界提供了强一阶电弱相变及可探测引力波信号，同时镜像扇区可作为暗物质候选，为未来引力波观测与暗物质研究提供了可行方向。

Abstract: We study a mirror Standard Model world which contains a high scale duplicate of the Standard Model, and includes a singlet mirror scalar extension coupled to the mirror Higgs particle. Among the interactions of the mirror Higgs to the mirror singlet scalar, we include dimension-six non-renormalizable operators. We examine the electroweak phase transition of this mirror singlet extended world and we show that the phase transition is a strong first order phase transition, the bubble collision of which can be detectable by LISA, the BBO and the DECIGO gravitational wave experiments. We also provide a rough estimate of the abundance of the mirror singlet and we show that the whole dark matter in the Universe may be comprised by mirror particles and atoms.

</details>


### [190] [A relook at a low reheating temperature: Freeze-in versus Freeze-out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26348)
*Debajyoti Choudhury,Vineet K. Jha,Rameswar Sahu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视低再加热温度 $T_{\rm RH}$ 促进暗物质遗迹丰度获得的范式，指出关键尺度并非暗物质质量本身，而是主导暗物质产生过程热效率最高的特征温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低再加热温度 $T_{\rm RH}$ 被视为获得正确暗物质遗迹丰度的手段，但存在流行误解——认为相关尺度是暗物质质量本身。作者希望澄清这一误解，并确定 $T_{\rm RH}$ 的下限约束。

Method: 在暴胀子衰变近似瞬时、热浴仅在较低温度出现的假设下，分析 $T_{\rm RH}$ 作为热产生可达到的最高温度，如何决定可达的产生历史阶段，并考察其对遗迹丰度的影响。讨论耦合改变是否能补偿该效应，无论机制为 freeze-in 还是 freeze-out。

Result: 降低 $T_{\rm RH}$ 倾向于减少遗迹丰度；该效应原则上可通过改变理论中的耦合来补偿，但存在一个由质量谱决定的最小 $T_{\rm RH}$，低于该值时遗迹丰度降至极小水平。

Conclusion: 与流行误解相反，决定暗物质遗迹丰度的相关尺度是主导产生过程热效率的特征温度而非暗物质质量本身；mass spectrum 决定了一个最小 $T_{\rm RH}$，低于此值遗迹丰度无论 freeze-in 或 freeze-out 都会塌缩至极小。

Abstract: A lowering of the post-inflation reheating temperature $T_{\rm RH}$ has attracted much attention as a means to facilitate obtaining the correct Dark Matter (DM) relic abundance. We take a relook at the paradigm under the assumption that the inflaton decay is essentially instantaneous, with the thermal bath coming into existence only at a relatively low temperature. We demonstrate that, contrary to the popular misconception, the relevant scale is not necessarily the DM mass itself, but the characteristic temperature at which the dominant DM production processes are thermally efficient. With $T_{\rm RH}$ setting the maximum temperature available for thermal production, and thereby determining the accessible stages of the production history, lowering $T_{\rm RH}$ tends to reduce the relic abundance. While this effect could, in principle, be offset by altering the couplings defining the theory, there exists a minimum $T_{\rm RH}$---determined by the mass spectrum---below which the relic abundance falls to minuscule levels, whether the mechanism be freeze-in or freeze-out.

</details>


### [191] [Semileptonic $\bar{B}^*_{c} \to (P, V) \ell^- \barν_{\ell}$ decays within the self-consistent light-front quark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26379)
*Qin Chang,Jianying Qian,Liting Wang,Xiaolin Wang,Shuai Xu,Jie Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在标准模型下研究 $\bar{B}^*_c$ 介子的半轻子衰变 $\bar{B}^*_{c}\to(P,V)\ell^-\bar\nu_\ell$，利用自洽协变光前夸克模型给出的形状因子预测分支比等可观测量，发现 Cabibbo 允许的 $\bar{B}^*_{c}\to\bar{B}^*_{s}e^-\bar\nu_e$ 和 $\bar{B}^*_{s}\mu^-\bar\nu_\mu$ 分支比可达 $10^{-6}$ 量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 ATLAS 合作组近期观测到 $B_c^*$ 介子及未来高亮度对撞机发现潜力的驱动，作者希望对 $\bar{B}^*_c$ 的半轻子衰变进行理论预言，为实验搜寻提供依据。

Method: 在标准模型框架下，采用自洽协变光前夸克模型计算相关形状因子，用于 $\bar{B}^*_{c}\to(P,V)\ell^-\bar\nu_\ell$（$P=D,B_{d,s},\eta_c(1S,2S,3S)$；$V=D^*,B^*_{d,s},\psi(1S,2S,3S)$）半轻子衰变的研究。

Result: 给出了分支比、分支比比值 $R_{(L)}$、末态矢量介子纵向极化分数、$\tau$ 轻子自旋不对称度及前后不对称度等物理量的理论预言；Cabibbo 允许的 $\bar{B}^*_{c}\to\bar{B}^*_{s}e^-\bar\nu_e$ 与 $\bar{B}^*_{s}\mu^-\bar\nu_\mu$ 衰变分支比达到 ${\cal O}(10^{-6})$ 量级。

Conclusion: 所预言的较大分支比表明这些衰变有望在未来高亮度大型强子对撞机上被首次观测到。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent observation of the $B_c^*$ meson by the ATLAS Collaboration and its promising discovery potential at future high-luminosity colliders, we investigate the semileptonic decays $\bar{B}^*_{c} \to (P, V) \ell^- \barν_{\ell}$ ($P=D$, $B_{d,s}$, $η_c(1S,\,2S,\,3S)$; $V=D^*$, $B^*_{d,s}$, $ψ(1S,\,2S,\,3S)$) in the Standard Model. Using the relevant form factors obtained in the self-consistent covariant light-front quark model, we present theoretical predictions for several physical observables, including the branching fraction, the ratios of branching fractions $R_{(L)}$, the longitudinal polarization fraction of the daughter vector meson, the $τ$ lepton spin asymmetry and the forward-backward asymmetry. It is found that the branching fractions for the Cabibbo-favored $\bar{B}^*_{c}$ $\to$ $\bar{B}^*_{s} e^- \barν_e$ and $\bar{B}^*_{s} μ^- \barν_μ$ decays reach the order of ${\cal O}(10^{-6})$, which might be firstly observed at the future high-luminosity Large Hadron Collider.

</details>


### [192] [The Super-Composite Higgs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26413)
*Kaustubh Agashe,Gian F. Giudice,Riccardo Rattazzi,Raman Sundrum*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"超复合希格斯"（Super-Composite Higgs）框架，将超对称与复合性结合，以单一动力学机制同时稳定希格斯质量并生成味结构，并在精密测量、宇宙学观测和高能对撞三个前沿给出可检验预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯质量的稳定性与味结构的起源通常需要不同的机制来解释。超对称可保护希格斯质量，复合性（部分复合）可生成汤川耦合，但两者各自存在缺陷。本文动机在于将二者整合，使彼此互补并缓解各自的问题。

Method: 构建"超复合希格斯"情景：希格斯质量由超对称保护，汤川耦合通过部分复合机制产生，从而在一个统一的动力学框架内同时处理质量稳定与味结构问题。

Result: 该框架在三个基本物理前沿给出可检验预言：精密测量前沿，电子电偶极矩（EDM）处于未来实验探测范围内；观测宇宙学前沿，超轻引力子（gravitino）预期在宇宙微波背景和大尺度结构中留下可测效应；高能前沿，由宇宙学考量导出的超对称粒子质量上限表明，标夸克（squarks）应轻于约10 TeV，处于FCC-hh的探测范围内。

Conclusion: 超对称与复合性的结合可相互增益并改善各自理论的不足，"超复合希格斯"提供了一个统一且具有多重可检验预言的动力学框架。

Abstract: We construct the Super-Composite Higgs, a scenario designed to stabilize the Higgs mass and generate the flavor structure within a single dynamical framework. The basic idea is to combine supersymmetry and compositeness, where the Higgs mass is protected by supersymmetry and Yukawa couplings are generated through partial compositeness. This combination offers mutual advantages and ameliorates several drawbacks of each individual theory. The Super-Composite Higgs makes experimentally testable predictions across three different areas of fundamental physics. At the precision frontier, the electron EDM is predicted to be within reach of future experiments. At the observational cosmology frontier, measurable effects in CMB and large-scale structures are expected from an ultra-light gravitino. At the high-energy frontier, an upper bound on supersymmetric particle masses, derived from cosmological considerations, indicates that squarks should be lighter than about 10 TeV, which is within reach of the FCC-hh.

</details>


### [193] [Three-Body Holonomy as a Toy-Model Mechanism for Family Triplication and Mass Hierarchy in (1+1) Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24022)
*Tadashi YOSHIKAWA*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在1+1维相对论Dirac体系中，三体（三重合点）非平凡和乐可被实现为三链环上的Peierls型相位，产生C_3对称有效质量谱 M^{[k]}=M_0+2Δcos((θ_3+2πk)/3)，可提升共轭通道简并并生成参数性轻分支；引入循环对称破缺后，Schur补消去重态留下的低能修正保留对完整三态环的记忆（∝cosθ_3），而相位敏感量 sinθ_3 本身并不单独意味着CP破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨真正三体和乐（而非两体接触相互作用）在相对论Dirac体系中的现象学后果：成对Sakamoto–Munakata–Ino接触相互作用在该点的净匹配是平凡的，因此希望用三体和乐作为新机制，构造一个低维“概念验证”，以关联家族式三重化、质量层级与红外记忆，而不声称是标准模型费米子代数的微观理论。

Method: 1) 移除质心坐标后，三重合点刺穿二维相对构型空间，允许非平凡U(1)缠绕相位；2) 固定内禀宇称，将六个粒子排序扇区约化为三维循环空间；3) 构造Hermitian C_3不变有效质量算符，并给出Peierls型实现（三链环上规范不变相位等于θ_3）；4) 解析求本征值；5) 允许循环对称破缺，使用一般Hermitian三态质量矩阵，通过Schur补精确消去两个重态，得到含rephasing不变环路乘积Re(t12t23t31)∝cosθ_3的低能修正，并讨论Im(t12t23t31)∝sinθ_3的相位敏感性与CP破坏关系。

Result: 得到质量本征值 M^{[k]}=M_0+2Δcos((θ_3+2πk)/3), k=0,1,2；和乐提升共轭通道简并，并可通过共同质量与和乐诱导位移之间的相消产生参数性轻分支。Schur补给出的低能有效质量包含 rephasing-invariant 环路乘积 Re(t12t23t31)∝cosθ_3，因此即使只有一个分支运动学可达，其有效质量仍保留完整三态环的有限记忆；互补不变量 Im(t12t23t31)∝sinθ_3 具相位敏感性，但单独并不蕴含CP破坏。

Conclusion: 该构造为家族式三重化、质量层级和红外记忆提供了一个低维现象学概念验证，展示了三体和乐可作为有效质量矩阵中非平凡相位结构的来源；但其定位并非标准模型费米子代的微观理论。

Abstract: We investigate the phenomenological consequences of a genuine three-body holonomy in a relativistic Dirac system in one spatial dimension. After removing the center-of-mass coordinate, the triple-coincidence point punctures the two-dimensional relative configuration space and permits a nontrivial $U(1)$ winding phase, whereas the pairwise Sakamoto--Munakata--Ino contact interactions give trivial net matching around this point.
  At fixed intrinsic parity, the six particle-ordering sectors reduce to a three-dimensional cyclic space. A Hermitian $C_3$-invariant effective mass operator admits a Peierls-type realization in which the gauge-invariant phase around the three links equals the three-body holonomy $θ_3$. Its eigenvalues are
  $$ M^{[k]}=M_0+2Δ\cos\!\left(\frac{θ_3+2πk}{3}\right), \qquad k=0,1,2. $$
  The holonomy lifts the conjugate-channel degeneracy and can generate a parametrically light branch through cancellation between the common mass and the holonomy-induced shift.
  We further allow cyclic-symmetry breaking and consider a general Hermitian three-state mass matrix. Exact elimination of two heavy states by the Schur complement yields a low-energy correction containing the rephasing-invariant loop product $\mathrm{Re}(t_{12}t_{23}t_{31})\propto\cosθ_3$. Thus, even when only one branch is kinematically accessible, its effective mass can retain finite memory of the complete three-state loop. The complementary invariant $\mathrm{Im}(t_{12}t_{23}t_{31})\propto\sinθ_3$ is phase sensitive but does not alone imply CP violation. The construction provides a low-dimensional phenomenological proof of concept for family-like triplication, mass hierarchy, and infrared memory, rather than a microscopic theory of Standard Model fermion generations.

</details>


### [194] [The compressed bino--wino dark matter in the GmSUGRA and the LHC soft-lepton searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26533)
*Imtiaz Khan,Ali Muhammad,Tianjun Li,Shabbar Raza,Mussawir Khan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在GmSUGRA边界条件下研究MSSM中压缩的bino-wino谱，发现其可自然实现与LHC软轻子搜索相关的质量劈裂，并同时满足Planck遗迹丰度与直接探测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC Run-2对电弱超对称"golden channel"的软双轻子与三轻子数据出现轻微超出，简化模型解释指向约200 GeV以上、质量差约20 GeV的压缩谱。作者希望在高能标MSSM框架下给出该谱的自然实现。

Method: 在MSSM中采用Generalized minimal Supergravity（GmSUGRA）边界条件，施加Higgs、味物理、对撞机、遗迹丰度及直接探测约束，对两个μ符号进行扫描，寻找与Planck实验相容的解。

Result: 对两个μ符号，相容解均给出质量约260–266 GeV的近纯bino χ̃₁⁰，以及约280 GeV的近简并wino型χ̃₂⁰/χ̃₁±对；质量劈裂Δm₂₁≈18.5–19.1 GeV，落在ATLAS与CMS软轻子搜索敏感区间，并使Z(*)/W(*)衰变低于在壳规范玻色子阈值。

Conclusion: 压缩的bino-wino层级使wino态在冻结期保持热布居，bino-wino共湮灭可给出与Planck一致的暗物质遗迹丰度，且解仍满足直接探测界限。因此GmSUGRA边界条件提供了LHC软轻子通道相关压缩bino-wino谱的高能标实现。

Abstract: The search for electroweak supersymmetric particles remains an important part of the LHC program since neutralinos and charginos with masses around a few hundred GeV are still compatible with the present constraints in compressed spectra. Run-2 searches for the ``golden channel'', $pp\to\widetildeχ_2^0\widetildeχ_1^\pm\to\widetildeχ_1^0 Z^{(*)}\widetildeχ_1^0 W^{\pm(*)}$, have reported mild excesses in parts of the soft two- and three-lepton data, with the corresponding simplified-model interpretations emphasizing $m_{\widetildeχ_2^0}\simeq m_{\widetildeχ_1^\pm}\gtrsim200$~GeV and a neutralino mass difference near $20$~GeV. We study this configuration in the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (MSSM) with the Generalized minimal Supergravity (GmSUGRA) boundary conditions, imposing the Higgs, flavor, collider, relic-density and direct-detection constraints relevant to the spectrum. For both signs of $μ$, the solutions compatible with the Planck experiment contain an almost pure bino $\widetildeχ_1^0$ with mass about $260$--$266$~GeV and a nearly degenerate wino-like $\widetildeχ_2^0/\widetildeχ_1^\pm$ pair near $280$~GeV. The resulting splitting, $Δm_{21}\simeq18.5$--$19.1$~GeV, lies in the mass-difference region probed by the ATLAS and CMS soft-lepton searches and places the $Z^{(*)}/W^{(*)}$ decays below the on-shell gauge-boson thresholds. The same compressed hierarchy keeps the wino states thermally populated during freeze-out, so bino-wino coannihilation yields a neutralino relic abundance consistent with the Planck experiment while the surviving solutions remain compatible with the direct-detection experimental bounds. Thus, the GmSUGRA boundary conditions provide a high-scale realization of the compressed bino-wino spectrum relevant to the LHC soft-lepton channel.

</details>


### [195] [Probing dark matter below the neutrino floor through NMSSM Higgs cascade decays at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26545)
*Yabo Dong,Kun Wang,Haijun Yang,Jingya Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究Higgs级联衰变在次约束NMSSM中探测低于中微子地板的neutralino暗物质，信号可达约0.16 fb，HL-LHC上统计显著性约6.1σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直接探测在neutralino质量约65–100 GeV且散射截面低于中微子地板时失效，需寻找对撞机互补探针。

Method: 在半约束NMSSM且gaugino质量非普适框架下，选取A1共振区（mA1>2mχ̃10），考虑共振增强湮灭与不可见A1衰变，施加理论与实验约束，分析pp→H2→A1A1→γγ+χ̃10χ̃10过程，并对代表benchmark点做Monte Carlo模拟。

Result: 存活样本在√s=14 TeV下级联信号截面约0.16 fb，即使relic密度重标后的自旋无关散射截面低于中微子地板；在3000 fb⁻¹下预期统计显著性约6.1σ（忽略系统误差）。

Conclusion: 双光子加缺失横向动量通道可作为neutralino暗物质的互补探测手段。

Abstract: We investigate the potential of Higgs cascade decays to probe neutralino dark matter below the neutrino floor in the semi-constrained Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model with nonuniversal gaugino masses. We focus on lightest-neutralino masses of 65 to 100 GeV and study the process $pp\to H_2\to A_1A_1\toγγ+\tildeχ_1^0\tildeχ_1^0$, where $H_1$ is the observed SM-like Higgs boson, $H_2$ is a heavier CP-even state, and $A_1$ is the lightest CP-odd state. The selected parameter region lies near the $A_1$ resonance, with $m_{A_1}>2m_{\tildeχ_1^0}$, allowing both resonantly enhanced neutralino annihilation and invisible $A_1$ decays. After imposing theoretical and experimental constraints, we find surviving samples with cascade signal cross sections of approximately 0.16 fb at $\sqrt{s}=14~\mathrm{TeV}$ even when their relic-density-rescaled spin-independent scattering cross sections lie below the neutrino floor. A Monte Carlo analysis of a representative benchmark point yields an expected statistical significance of approximately $6.1σ$ at the high-luminosity LHC with an integrated luminosity of $3000~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, neglecting systematic uncertainties. These results highlight the potential of the diphoton plus missing transverse momentum channel as a complementary probe of neutralino dark matter.

</details>


### [196] [A Simple Dark Matter Model to Explain the LZ Event and Galactic Center Excess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26570)
*Caleb Gemmell,Dan Hooper,Gordan Krnjaic*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出暗物质是质量约20-100 GeV的赝狄拉克费米子，通过强子亲和的矢量玻色子V（自发破缺重子数的规范玻色子）非弹性耦合到标准模型，可同时解释银河系中心伽马射线超额和LZ实验事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释长期存在的银河系中心伽马射线超额（GCE）以及LZ实验观测到的可能暗物质事件，寻找一种能同时符合两者的暗物质模型。

Method: 构建赝狄拉克费米子暗物质模型：两个本征态χ1和χ2在热冻结后获得近似相等丰度，通过χ1χ1→VV和χ2χ2→VV过程消耗；假设质量劈裂低于电子-正电子阈值且与Z玻色子混合足够小，使较重态寿命超过宇宙年龄，从而在银河系晕中两态均保持近似等丰度；采用热遗迹湮灭截面进行计算。

Result: 该模型在热遗迹湮灭截面下可产生与GCE观测特征相符的信号；当质量劈裂δ约1000 keV时，能解释GCE的同一参数空间也可通过χ2→χ1在氙核上的放热下散射解释LZ实验事件。

Conclusion: 赝狄拉克费米子暗物质经强子亲和矢量媒介子耦合的模型，能够统一解释银河系中心伽马射线超额与LZ实验事件，为暗物质探测提供了一种可行的理论框架。

Abstract: We propose that the dark matter (DM) is a pseudo-Dirac fermion with a mass of approximately $20-100$ GeV that couples inelastically to the Standard Model through a hadrophilic spin-1 mediator, $V$, the gauge boson of spontaneously broken baryon number. The two DM eigenstates, $χ_{1}$ and $χ_2$, acquire approximately equal abundances after thermal freeze-out and are depleted through the processes $χ_1 χ_1 \to VV$ and $χ_2 χ_2 \to VV$. Although the heavier state is unstable, for a mass splitting below the electron-positron threshold and sufficiently small mass mixing with the $Z$ boson, its lifetime can exceed the age of the universe, allowing both states to remain approximately equally abundant in the Galactic halo. For a thermal-relic annihilation cross section, this model can produce a signal that is consistent with the observed features of the longstanding Galactic Center Gamma-Ray Excess (GCE). If $δ\sim 1000$ keV, the same parameter space that explains the GCE can also explain the LZ event through exothermic $χ_2 \to χ_1$ downscattering off xenon nuclei.

</details>


### [197] [Model-Independent Sideband Constraints on Inelastic Dark Matter at the LZ High-Recoil Candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26698)
*David Delepine,Shaaban Khalil*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 对LZ实验单一核反冲事件进行模型无关分析，发现非弹性暗物质在特定质量劈裂δ*处可同时优化两个边带计数比，但整体张力仅约1.9σ–2.5σ，需专用高能搜索验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验在2.84吨·年曝光中报告了一个248 keV的核反冲事件（局部显著性3.4σ）。非弹性（吸热）暗物质因其运动学阈值可抑制低能反冲并将信号推向高能，是解释此类事件的天然候选，因此需要检验其与LZ数据的一致性。

Method: 采用模型无关分析，不指定紫外完备理论，适用于相干自旋无关核散射的非弹性暗物质。针对每个(χ质量m_χ, 质量劈裂δ)计算信号区[225,271] keV及低能边带[14,225) keV、高能边带(271,800] keV的预测计数比N_low/N_sig与N_hi/N_sig，利用两个比值随δ反向变化、在唯一劈裂δ*处同时最优的特性进行约束，并考虑Helm形状因子的额外压低。

Result: 对m_χ=1 TeV，δ*=339 keV时合并泊松张力约1.9σ；在500 GeV–3 TeV范围内，δ*处张力从≲1.3σ升至约2.5σ。Helm形状因子在248 keV处|F_Xe^Helm|²≈3×10⁻⁴，接近其第二衍射零点，带来额外信号压低。

Conclusion: 非弹性暗物质在唯一质量劈裂δ*处可同时满足两个边带约束，但对LZ单事件的解释张力仍有限（1.9σ–2.5σ）。在271<E_R<800 keV进行LZ专用搜索，将对δ≳330 keV的非弹性暗物质给出决定性检验。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN experiment has reported one nuclear-recoil event at $248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}\,{\rm keV}$ in a $2.84\,{\rm tonne\text{-}year}$ exposure, with a local significance of $3.4σ$. Inelastic (endothermic) dark matter is a natural candidate: its kinematic threshold suppresses low-energy recoils and shifts the signal toward higher recoil energies. We perform a model-independent analysis of the LZ data using two sidebands, without specifying an ultraviolet completion, so our results apply broadly to inelastic dark matter with coherent spin-independent nuclear scattering. For each $(m_χ,δ)$ we compute $N_{\rm low}/N_{\rm sig}$ and $N_{\rm hi}/N_{\rm sig}$---predicted count ratios in the sidebands $[14,225)\,{\rm keV}$ and $(271,800]\,{\rm keV}$ relative to the signal region $[225,271]\,{\rm keV}$. The two ratios vary in opposite directions with $δ$ and are simultaneously optimized at a unique splitting $δ^\star$. For $m_χ=1\,{\rm TeV}$, $δ^\star=339\,{\rm keV}$ with a combined Poisson tension of $\approx1.9σ$; over $500\,{\rm GeV}$--$3\,{\rm TeV}$, the tension at $δ^\star$ rises from $\lesssim1.3σ$ to $\approx2.5σ$. Additional suppression arises from the Helm form factor: $|F_{\rm Xe}^{\rm Helm}|^2\simeq3\times10^{-4}$ at $248\,{\rm keV}$, near its second diffraction zero. A dedicated LZ search over $271<E_R<800\,{\rm keV}$ would provide a decisive test of inelastic dark matter with $δ\gtrsim330\,{\rm keV}$.

</details>


### [198] [Tachyonic particle production in strongly supercooled phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26767)
*Mateusz Kulejewski,Bogumila Swiezewska,Jorinde van de Vis*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在强过冷宇宙相变中，标量场涨落到势能最小值以下，引发粒子产生，显著改变引力波谱并可能促成原初黑洞形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强过冷相变通常产生可观测的引力波背景，但标量场演化过程中的粒子产生效应及其对引力波和相变动力学的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: 研究标量场在相变后演化过程中导致的粒子产生，重点关注部分模式出现瞬时快子质量而高效产生粒子的机制。

Result: 相变释放的相当一部分能量密度可转化为粒子而非梯度能；这改变了气泡相互作用产生的引力波谱形状与振幅，并产生新的高频信号，同时影响气泡膨胀速度和状态方程。

Conclusion: 粒子产生效应是强过冷相变中不可忽视的物理过程，可改变引力波信号特征并促进原初黑洞形成。

Abstract: In strongly supercooled cosmological phase transitions, which generically lead to strong, observable gravitational-wave backgrounds, the scalar field typically transitions to a value well below the minimum of its potential. We study particle production caused by the subsequent evolution of the field. Because some modes feature temporarily tachyonic masses, they can be efficiently produced, and a significant fraction of the energy density released in the phase transition can get converted to particles rather than gradient energy. This has consequences for the shape and amplitude of the gravitational-wave spectrum caused by the interactions in bubbles and sources a new, high-frequency signal. Moreover, it affects the bubble expansion velocity and equation of state, which can facilitate the formation of primordial black holes.

</details>


### [199] [Resonant neutrino flavor conversion within dark matter spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26773)
*P. S. Bhupal Dev,Elisa Gaido,Alejandro Ibarra,Yago Porto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究AGN中心黑洞DM尖峰内中微子-暗物质相干前向散射引起的味转换，发现百TeV能区味组分显著偏离真空振荡预期，可作为探测中微子-暗物质相互作用的新探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能天体中微子的味组分通常由真空振荡预言，但若中微子与暗物质存在相互作用，AGN中心超大质量黑洞周围的暗物质尖峰可提供相干前向散射势，从而改变味组分。作者旨在量化这种效应并检验其可观测性。

Method: 计算中微子在AGN暗物质尖峰中传播时由相干前向散射产生的味依赖势，求解味转换方程，得到π衰变源与μ子阻尼源在地球处的味组分；并与IceCube味三角形（MESE 95%轮廓）测量比较，在弥散通量来自具有共同产生与暗物质尖峰性质的AGN这一假设下进行检验。

Result: 在约100 TeV，当耦合加权的净暗物质数密度满足|ε_α(n_χ−n_χ̄)|~10^18 cm^-3时，物质势开始与真空振荡竞争；在此条件下味组分明显偏离真空振荡。当暗物质诱导势作用于μ子味并在产生处主导真空项时，pγ μ子阻尼预言落在95% MESE轮廓之外。

Conclusion: 高能中微子味组分测量可对天体物理环境中的中微子-暗物质相互作用提供新颖探针，并可用于约束相关耦合与暗物质尖峰性质。

Abstract: We investigate how neutrino--dark matter (DM) interactions modify the flavor composition of high-energy neutrinos from active galactic nuclei (AGNs). Coherent forward scattering in a DM spike around the central supermassive black hole can generate a flavor-dependent potential, inducing flavor conversion as the neutrinos escape. We calculate the flavor composition at Earth for pion-decay and muon-damped sources and find substantial departures from vacuum-oscillation expectations. At neutrino energies around $100\,\mathrm{TeV}$, the matter potential begins to compete with vacuum oscillations when the coupling-weighted net DM number density reaches $|ε_α(n_χ-n_{\barχ})|\sim10^{18}\,\mathrm{cm}^{-3}$. We compare our predictions with IceCube flavor triangle measurements under the illustrative assumption that the diffuse flux originates from AGNs with common neutrino-production and DM-spike properties. Under this assumption, when the DM-induced potential acts on the muon flavor and dominates the vacuum terms at production, the $pγ$ muon-damped predictions lie outside the 95\% MESE contour. High-energy neutrino flavor measurements can therefore provide a novel probe of neutrino-DM interactions in astrophysical environments.

</details>
