# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Pseudo-Riemannian geodesic orbit nilmanifolds of signature $\boldsymbol{(n-3,3)}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25142)
*Zhiqi Chen,Shaoxiang Zhang,Yiyi Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究了度指标为三的伪黎曼测地轨道幂零流形，证明在$\n,\n]$非退化时$\n$为阿贝尔、2步或4步幂零（2步结论失效但3步不可能），并刻画4步情形；退化时给出invariant isotropic子空间与双扩张约化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测地轨道性质蕴含齐性，涵盖弱对称与自然约化流形等重要类；不定度情形比黎曼signature更精细，近年Lorentz与trans-Lorentz已有结构结果，本文推进指标三情形的结构分析。

Method: 研究$M=G/H$为signature $(n-3,3)$的测地轨道伪黎曼幂零流形，设reductive decomposition $\g=\h\oplus\n$且$\n$幂零；分别讨论度量在$[\n,\n]$上非退化与退化的情形，通过代数结构分析证明结论并构造例子验证sharpness。

Result: 非退化时$\n$为阿贝尔、2步或4步幂零（与黎曼、Lorentz、trans-Lorentz不同，2步结论失效，但3步仍不可能）；4步情形限于导代数Lorentz且正交补指标二，并强制该补中存在不变全等向二维平面，九维度signature $(6,3)$例子证明sharpness。退化时证明存在不变等向子空间中心化其正交补，给出到更小指标幂零Lie代数的双扩张约化，八维例子表明相对中心性不蕴含全Lie代数中心性。

Conclusion: 指标三的测地轨道伪黎曼幂零流形结构比黎曼及Lorentz情形更丰富，允许4步幂零与退化带来的双扩张约化；结果推进了测地轨道幂零流形的结构分类。

Abstract: The geodesic orbit property is useful and interesting in Riemannian geometry. It implies homogeneity and has important classes of Riemannian manifolds as special cases, such as weakly symmetric Riemannian manifolds and naturally reductive Riemannian manifolds. The corresponding results for indefinite metric manifolds are much more delicate than in Riemannian signature, but in the last few years important corresponding structural results were proved for geodesic orbit Lorentz and trans-Lorentz manifolds. %Here we carry out a major step in the structural analysis of geodesic orbit Lorentz nilmanifolds. Here we study pseudo-Riemannian geodesic orbit nilmanifolds of metric index three. Those are the geodesic orbit pseudo-Riemannian manifolds $M = G/H$ of signature $(n-3,3)$ such that a nilpotent analytic subgroup of $G$ is transitive on $M$. Suppose that there is a reductive decomposition $\g = \h \oplus \n$ (vector space direct sum) with $\n$ nilpotent. When the metric is nondegenerate on $[\n,\n]$ we show that $\n$ is abelian, $2$-step or $4$-step nilpotent. In contrast to the Riemannian, Lorentzian, and trans-Lorentz cases, the two-step conclusion therefore fails, but $3$-step nilpotency remains impossible. The $4$-step case is confined to a Lorentzian derived algebra with an orthogonal complement of index two, and its structure forces an invariant totally isotropic two-plane in that complement. An explicit nine-dimensional example of signature $(6,3)$ proves sharpness. When the metric is degenerate on $[\n,\n]$ we prove the existence of an invariant isotropic subspace that centralizes its orthogonal complement, yielding a double-extension reduction to a geodesic orbit metric nilpotent Lie algebra of strictly smaller index. An eight-dimensional example shows that this relative centrality need not imply centrality in the full Lie algebra.

</details>


### [201] [A gluing construction of $Z$-extremal Kähler metrics and new examples of Bismut Hermitian-Einstein $3$-folds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25250)
*Vestislav Apostolov,Abdellah Lahdili,Kuan-Hui Lee,Frédéric Rochon*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为Kähler流形/orbifold上一类6阶几何非线性PDE建立粘合（gluing）构造，应用于Bismut Hermitian Einstein复3维、Z-critical Kähler度量及超引力AdS解，得到去奇异化与点爆破定理及新例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究源于多个几何与物理问题的交汇：Bismut Hermitian Einstein复3维、Dervan的Z-critical Kähler度量，以及IIB型和D=11超引力中的AdS₃×Y₇、AdS₂×Y₉超对称解。需要构造新解并生成新拓扑类型的例子，而现有方法难以直接给出光滑流形上的解。

Method: 对Kähler流形或orbifold上一类6阶几何非线性PDE提出粘合构造。基于该粘合技术，证明两条定理：(1) 具crepant奇点且可解该PDE的orbifold的去奇异化定理；(2) 光滑复3维上可解该PDE时的点爆破定理。

Result: 得到新的Bismut Hermitian Einstein结构例子，位于光滑流形 3(S²×S⁴)♯4(S³×S³)、5(S²×S⁴)♯6(S³×S³) 和 S¹×9(S²×S³) 上；并得到无穷多拓扑类型的7维紧流形Y₇，给出超对称AdS₃×Y₇ IIB型与AdS₂×T²×Y⁷ D=11超引力模型的新解。

Conclusion: 粘合构造是处理此类6阶几何非线性PDE的有效工具，可用于orbifold去奇异化与点爆破，并将几何PDE的解与超引力/物理模型解联系起来，产出多种新拓扑上的新解。

Abstract: We present a gluing construction for a class of 6th order geometric non-linear PDE's on a Kähler manifold or orbifold, arising in the study of Bismut Hermitian Einstein complex $3$-folds, the supersymmetric ${\rm AdS}_3 \times Y_7$ and ${\rm AdS}_2 \times Y_9$ solutions of type IIB and $D=11$ supergravity models in physics, and in Dervan's Z-critical Kähler metrics. As applications, we obtain a desingularization theorem for orbifolds with crepant singularities admitting a solution of the PDE, as well as a blowing-up of points theorem for smooth complex $3$-folds admitting such solutions. These results yield new examples of regular Bismut Hermitian Einstein structures on the smooth manifolds $3(S^2\times S^4)\sharp 4(S^3\times S^3)$, $5(S^2\times S^4)\sharp 6(S^3\times S^3)$ and $S^1\times 9(S^2\times S^3)$, as well as infinite topological types of smooth compact $7$-dimensional manifolds $Y_7$, providing new solutions to the supersymmetric ${\rm AdS}_3 \times Y_7$ type IIB and ${\rm AdS}_2 \times T^2 \times Y^7$ type $D=11$ supergravity models.

</details>


### [202] [Futaki invariant on lcK manifolds with potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25268)
*Giacomo Perri*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了Futaki–Hattori–Ornea不变量在任意具有势的紧致局部共形Kähler流形上恒为零，从而回答了Ornea与Verbitsky提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ornea与Verbitsky提出一个问题：由Futaki、Hattori与Ornea引入的Futaki不变量在具有势的紧致局部共形Kähler流形上是否消失。该文旨在解决这一公开问题。

Method: 通过数学证明的方法，对任意紧致且具有势的局部共形Kähler流形上的Futaki–Hattori–Ornea不变量进行恒等性论证。

Result: 证明了该Futaki不变量在每一紧致具有势的局部共形Kähler流形上恒等于零。

Conclusion: Futaki–Hattori–Ornea不变量在所考虑的流形类上普遍消失，正面回答了Ornea与Verbitsky的问题。

Abstract: We prove that the Futaki invariant introduced by Futaki, Hattori, and Ornea vanishes identically on every compact locally conformally Kähler manifold with potential, thereby answering a question raised by Ornea and Verbitsky.

</details>


### [203] [Two Growth Filtrations for Drift Laplacians: Compatibility, Rigidity, and an Inverse Hermite/Laguerre Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25289)
*Elham Matinpour*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究非紧流形上漂移Laplace算子谱与特征函数增长阶的对应关系，在多种结构假设下证明λ_k与γ_k的相容关系，并给出反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画漂移Laplace算子L_f=Δ-⟨∇f,∇·⟩在L^2(M,e^{-f}dV)上的谱性质，建立谱序λ_k与特征函数关于f^{1/α}水平集的增长阶γ_k之间的定量联系（相容性λ_k≍γ_k^{(2α-2)/α}），并澄清其适用范围与失效情形。

Method: 在f、|∇f|、Δf的多项式界（H1-H3）及γ_k→∞（H4）假设下，比较增长滤过与特征值滤过。对α>1证明离散性与加权Agmon估计，导出矩估计以控制集中半径；利用下局部化条件给出比较的一个方向。对带一维基的精确warped product施加径向增长与径向漂移正则性；对一般(M,g)改用水平集的传递等距对称性及不变扇区增长条件；另对不变特征函数的精确多项式性讨论归约方程（Hermite或广义Laguerre）。

Result: α>1时证明离散性与加权Agmon估计，矩估计以增长阶和有限尺度前因子界定集中半径，下局部化给出谱比较的一个方向。给出显式旋转对称反例，说明即使α=2，全谱的相容性关系也可能失效。在warped product（径向条件+正则性）下径向扇区得到α=2且λ_k≍γ_k；在传递等距对称下得到α=2且λ_k≍γ_k≍k；不变特征函数精确多项式时无需漂移正则性即得α=2。论证不使用曲率界或soliton方程。

Conclusion: 谱-增长相容性λ_k≍γ_k^{(2α-2)/α}并非普遍成立，需要额外的几何或对称性条件；在warped product、传递等距对称及精确多项式等假设下可得到α=2的线性对应，方法不依赖曲率界或soliton方程。

Abstract: Let $(M^n,g)$ be a complete, connected, noncompact Riemannian manifold, and let $f\ge0$ be a proper $C^2$ weight. We assume polynomial bounds on $f$, $|\nabla f|$, and $Δf$ (Assumptions H1-H3), with growth exponent $α>0$. We study the drift Laplacian $L_f=Δ-\langle\nabla f,\nabla\cdot\rangle$ on $L^2(M,e^{-f}dV)$. We assume that its eigenfunctions have finite polynomial growth orders $γ_k$, measured on the level sets of $f^{1/α}$. We also assume that $γ_k\to\infty$ in spectral order (Assumption H4). We compare this growth filtration with the filtration by eigenvalue $λ_k$. For $α>1$, we study the relation $λ_k\asympγ_k^{(2α-2)/α}$, called compatibility. An explicit rotationally symmetric example shows that this relation can fail for the full spectrum, even when $α=2$.
  We prove discreteness and weighted Agmon estimates for $α>1$. A moment estimate bounds the concentration radius in terms of the growth order and a finite-scale prefactor. A lower localization condition gives one direction of the spectral comparison. For exact warped products with a one-dimensional base, we impose the radial growth condition and a regularity assumption on the radial drift. These give $α=2$ and $λ_k\asympγ_k$ in the radial sector. For general $(M,g)$, we assume a transitive isometric symmetry of the level sets instead of a warped-product structure. The invariant-sector growth condition and radial-drift regularity then give $α=2$ and $λ_k\asympγ_k\asymp k$. Finally, exact polynomiality of the invariant eigenfunctions implies $α=2$ without the drift regularity assumptions. The reduced equation is then Hermite or generalized Laguerre after normalization. The arguments use neither curvature bounds nor soliton equations.

</details>


### [204] [The dHYM equation on crepant resolutions of Calabi-Yau cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25309)
*Eder M. Correa*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究非紧 Calabi-Yau 流形 $Z={\rm{Tot}}(\mathbf{K}_X)$（$X$ 为有理齐性簇）上的形变 Hermitian-Yang-Mills (dHYM) 方程，利用 Calabi ansatz 的余一对称性将完全非线性 PDE 约化为标量渐近自治 ODE，给出解的全局存在性条件，并构造首个非环面非紧 Calabi-Yau 上非 dHYM 的 Hermitian-Einstein 连接的显式非平凡例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dHYM 方程源自镜像对称中的特殊拉格朗日子流形，是复几何中的核心完全非线性方程，但现有构造的显式例子主要局限于环面或特殊情形，针对非紧 Calabi-Yau 流形上 dHYM 方程的系统研究仍然不足，尤其是非环面情形缺乏具体例子与一般性构造。

Method: 取 $X$ 为 Fano 有理齐性簇，$Z={\rm{Tot}}(\mathbf{K}_X)$ 在反典范极化 $\mathbf{L}=\mathbf{K}_X^{-1}$ 下是仿射锥 ${\rm{Aff}}(X)$ 顶点奇点的消解。利用 Calabi ansatz 得到的 Ricci-flat Kähler 度量的 cohomogeneity-one 对称性，将 dHYM 的完全非线性 PDE 约化为标量、渐近自治的 ODE；通过分析该 ODE 的渐近行为确定拓扑相位条件，并用 ODE 方法研究解的坍缩与刚性。

Result: 给出了保证 dHYM 方程全局解存在的拓扑相位精确条件；证明 $Z$ 上任意全纯线丛在总相位落入由 Lie 论数据确定的显式开区间时，都存在光滑全局 Hermitian 连接满足 dHYM 方程；证明刚性定理刻画 dHYM 解退化为经典 HYM 连接的确切几何条件；构造出非环面非紧 Calabi-Yau 流形上线丛之上首个非 dHYM 的 Hermitian-Einstein 连接的显式非平凡例子。

Conclusion: 本文推广了此前已有的构造，提供了一类重要的新例子，并表明可通过 ODE 方法研究 dHYM 解的性态，为在非紧 Calabi-Yau 流形上系统研究 dHYM 方程及其与 HYM 方程的关系提供了统一框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the deformed Hermitian-Yang-Mills (dHYM) equation on the non-compact Calabi-Yau manifold $Z = {\rm{Tot}}({\bf{K}}_{X})$, where $X$ is a rational homogeneous variety. Since $X$ is a Fano variety, $Z$ is a resolution of the singularity at the vertex of the affine cone ${\rm{Aff}}(X)$, provided the cone is built using the anticanonical polarization ${\bf{L}} = {\bf{K}}_{X}^{-1}$. Using the cohomogeneity-one symmetry of the Ricci-flat Kähler metric obtained via the Calabi ansatz on $Z$, we reduce the fully nonlinear PDE underlying the dHYM equation to a scalar, asymptotically autonomous ordinary differential equation (ODE). From this, we determine the exact condition on the topological phase that guarantees global existence of solutions. As an application, we show that every holomorphic line bundle over $Z$ admits a smooth, globally defined Hermitian connection solving the dHYM equation, provided the total phase lies in an explicit open interval determined by the Lie-theoretic data. Also, we prove a rigidity result classifying the exact geometric conditions under which the dHYM solution collapses into a classical Hermitian-Yang-Mills (HYM) connection. The results established generalize previous constructions and provide a substantial new class of examples. Furthermore, the approach presented allows one to study the behavior of the dHYM solutions through ODE methods. Using this approach, we construct the first explicit non-trivial example of a Hermitian-Einstein connection on a line bundle over a non-toric Calabi-Yau manifold which is not dHYM.

</details>


### [205] [A continuous Positive Mass Theorem for perturbations of Euclidean space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25550)
*Paula Burkhardt-Guim*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了在Ricci流意义下具有非负标量曲率的 $C^0$-渐近平坦黎曼度量的正质量定理，其质量非负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典正质量定理通常要求度量具有较高的正则性（如 $C^2$ 或 $W^{2,p}$），而低正则性（$C^0$）情形下的正质量定理尚不完善，作者希望将其推广到 $C^0$-渐近平坦且为欧氏空间 $C^0$ 扰动的度量。

Method: 对充分一致接近欧氏空间的 $C^0$-渐近平坦黎曼度量，采用Ricci流意义下的弱非负标量曲率条件，并使用作者此前引入的经典ADM质量的 $C^0$ 类比来定义质量。

Result: 证明了此类度量在Ricci流意义下具有非负标量曲率时，其 $C^0$ 类比质量是非负的。

Conclusion: 将正质量定理推广至低正则性（$C^0$）渐近平坦度量情形，拓展了经典结果的适用范围。

Abstract: We prove a Positive Mass Theorem for $C^0$-asymptotically flat Riemannian metrics with nonnegative scalar curvature in a weak sense that are sufficiently uniformly close to Euclidean space. More precisely, we show that a $C^0$-asymptotically flat Riemannian metric that is a $C^0$ perturbation of Euclidean space with nonnegative scalar curvature in the sense of Ricci flow has nonnegative mass, where the mass is given by a $C^0$ analog of the classical ADM mass previously introduced by the author.

</details>


### [206] [Quaternionic LVM Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26269)
*Alberto Verjovsky*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了LVM流形的四元数类比——LVMQ流形，研究其拓扑性质、Pontryagin类、四元数toric结构，并讨论了互补Poincaré域与迹叶状结构及秩一问题中的Hopf模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVM流形由实二次曲面交的商构造给出且具有丰富的几何拓扑性质；作者希望在四元数（而非复数）框架下建立相应构造，获得新的紧流形族并刻画其连通性、特征类与四元数toric性质，同时理解线性可积场对Hopf模型的刚性约束。

Method: 取可容许配置Λ⊂ℍ^m（n>4m），构造实二次曲面交Z_ℍ(Λ)⊂ℍ^n，其Sp(1)^n轨道空间为简单凸多面体；利用正实坐标伸缩作用的全局归一化横截面，再做Sp(1)约化得到紧商N_ℍ(Λ)。以四元数矩-角多面体积(D^4,S^3)^{K_{P_Λ}}描述全空间；计算连通性、H^4、第一Pontryagin类；在一类无穷球积族上分析Kraines形式限制与4-plectic结构；研究互补Poincaré域及迹叶状结构；对四元数秩一问题考察可逆线性场的Frobenius可积性与非线性扰动。

Result: LVMQ流形均为2-连通；在无可约坐标时3-连通且H^4(N_ℍ(Λ);ℤ)≅ℤ，p_1(TN_ℍ(Λ))=2(n-2)e（e为Sp(1)主丛的Euler类）。对显式无穷球积族，环境Kraines形式限制为4-plectic形式，所得LVMQ流形在Gentili–Gori–Sarfatti意义下为四元数toric。秩一情形中，可逆线性场的Frobenius可积性强制标量Hopf模型，小的非线性可积扰动给出Hopf型S^3纤维化，其底流形同伦等价于四元数射影空间。

Conclusion: 该工作成功建立了LVM流形的四元数对应物LVMQ流形，给出其多面体积描述、连通性与Pontryagin类公式，并证明特定族具有四元数toric结构；同时对互补Poincaré域与秩一可积性问题给出Hopf型刚性结论，拓展了四元数几何与toric拓扑的交叉研究。

Abstract: We study a quaternionic counterpart of the quotient construction underlying LVM manifolds. From an admissible configuration \[ Λ=(λ_1,\ldots,λ_n)\subset{\mathbb H}^m\simeq{\mathbb R}r^{4m}, \qquad n>4m, \] we obtain a smooth compact intersection of real quadrics $Z_{\mathbb H}(Λ)\subset{\mathbb H}^n$ whose $\text{Sp}(1)^n$ orbit space is a simple convex polytope. A canonical action by positive real coordinate dilations has $Z_{\mathbb H}(Λ)$ as a global normalized transversal and, after projectivization, gives the compact quotient \[ N_{\mathbb H}(Λ)=Z_{\mathbb H}(Λ)/\text{Sp}(1), \] which we call an LVMQ manifold.
  The total space is the quaternionic moment-angle polyhedral product $(D^4,{\mathbb S}^{3})^{K_{P_Λ}}$. Every LVMQ manifold is $2$-connected; in the absence of indispensable coordinates it is $3$-connected and \[ H^4(N_{\mathbb H}(Λ);\Zz)\cong\Zz, \qquad p_1(TN_{\mathbb H}(Λ))=2(n-2)e. \] Here $p_1$ denotes the first Pontryagin class and $e$ is the Euler class of the principal $\Sp(1)$-bundle $Z_{\mathbb H}(Λ)\to N_{\mathbb H}(Λ)$. For an explicit infinite sphere-product family, the ambient Kraines form restricts to a $4$-plectic form and the resulting LVMQ manifolds are quaternionic toric in the sense of Gentili--Gori--Sarfatti. We also study the complementary Poincaré domain and its trace foliations. For the quaternionic rank-one problem, Frobenius integrability of an invertible linear field forces the scalar Hopf model, while small nonlinear integrable perturbations give Hopf-like ${\mathbb S}^{3}$-fibrations over smooth manifolds homotopy equivalent to quaternionic projective space.

</details>


### [207] [Divisorial Rigidity and Regularity of Boundary Cases for the Supercritical LYZ Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25553)
*Jixiang Fu,Shing-Tung Yau,Dekai Zhang,Ziyi Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在紧Kähler流形上研究稳定性边界的超临界LYZ方程，在数值半稳定条件下证明修改nef锥上的定量正性估计，推出失稳素除子构成有限例外族；在光滑半下解假设下证明相交形式负定，从而构造对数奇异下解并得到有界Bedford–Taylor解（在补集上光滑）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超临界LYZ方程在稳定性边界处的行为，理解数值半稳定条件下解的存在性与奇异性结构，刻画失稳素除子的有限性与刚性。

Method: 采用修改nef锥上的定量正性估计；在假设光滑半(semi-)subsolution存在时分析相关相交形式的正负定性；利用该刚性构造沿失稳素除子并集的对数奇异下解；进而得到有界Bedford–Taylor解。

Result: 在数值半稳定下得到修改nef锥上的定量正性估计，证明失稳素除子构成有限例外族；在光滑半下解假设下，相交形式在这些除子类张成空间上负定；构造对数奇异下解并得到有界Bedford–Taylor解，且在补集上光滑。

Conclusion: 稳定性边界处的超临界LYZ方程具有受控的奇异性与刚性：失稳除子有限、相交形式负定，并可在其并集外获得光滑的有界解。

Abstract: We study the supercritical LYZ equation on compact Kähler manifolds at the boundary of stability. Under numerical semistability, we prove a quantitative positivity estimate on the modified nef cone. It implies that the destabilizing prime divisors form a finite exceptional family. Assuming the existence of a smooth semisubsolution, we prove that the associated intersection form is negative definite on the span of classes of these divisors. Using this rigidity, we then construct a logarithmic singular subsolution along their union and obtain a bounded Bedford--Taylor solution which is smooth on its complement.

</details>


### [208] [Hamiltonian-Type Spectral Functionals for Time-Dependent Perturbed Hodge--de Rham Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25676)
*Sining Wei,Yong Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究偶数维定向流形上光滑Riemann度量族下Dirac型算子 $D_t=d+\delta_{g_t}+\Psi_t$ 的Hawkins哈密顿作用量的标量加权残差泛函，通过符号计算与分部积分给出体项与边界项的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hawkins的哈密顿作用量以Dirac型算子表达，其残差泛函在度量变动下如何分解、边界如何贡献尚需明确，尤其在有界流形与warped collar情形下需要显式边界残差公式。

Method: 取算子表达对标量权重的残差泛函，用系数导数 $\partial_tD_t$ 对度量族求导；通过直接符号计算与分部积分，将lapse修正化为散度项与Dirichlet项；在带warped collar的紧流形上给出加权KKW项与交换子修正的相容因子分解，并显式计算边界残差。

Result: 速度贡献仅依赖 $\partial_tg_t$；lapse修正化为散度与Dirichlet项，非平凡测试函数经分部积分后保留混合梯度项；collar导数与扰动贡献在加权KKW因子分解中相消，剩余边界项涉及标量权重的法向导数；边界为空时恢复闭流形公式。

Conclusion: 该残差泛函具有清晰的体-边分解结构，其截断/边界行为由标量权重的法向导数控制，闭流形结果为边界项消失的特例。

Abstract: Let $g_t$ be a smooth family of Riemannian metrics on an oriented manifold of even dimension $n\geq4$, and let $D_t=d+δ_{g_t}+Ψ_t$ on the fixed exterior bundle, with $Ψ_t$ self-adjoint. We evaluate a scalar-weighted residue functional obtained from the operator expression in Hawkins' Hamiltonian action using the coefficient derivative $\partial_tD_t$. The velocity contribution depends only on $\partial_tg_t$. A direct symbol calculation reduces the lapse correction to a divergence and a Dirichlet term; for a nonconstant test function, integration by parts retains a mixed gradient term. For compact manifolds with a warped collar, we specify compatible factorizations of the weighted KKW term and of the commutator correction. Their boundary residues are computed explicitly. The collar-derivative and perturbation contributions cancel in the weighted KKW factorization, and the remaining boundary terms involve normal derivatives of the scalar weights. The closed formula is recovered when the boundary is empty.

</details>


### [209] [Kähler Rigidity for Constant Chern Holomorphic Sectional Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25762)
*Xiangsen Qin,Yuanhong Tian*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了常Chern全纯截面曲率紧Hermitian流形的两个刚性定理：非正情形下为Kähler且度量刚，正情形三folds为复射影空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究常Chern全纯截面曲率紧Hermitian流形的刚性，放宽平衡或pluriclosed假设，并刻画曲率符号与Kähler性、双全纯同构的关系。

Method: 非正情形使用Kähler背景上的积分Chern-Lu恒等式；负曲率借助twisted Kähler-Einstein体积下界、加权Stokes恒等式与体积比矩不等式；零曲率用张量Bochner论证；平衡三folds用微分相容性与挠率能量恒等式，及有理矩阵分解得到强制性估计。

Result: Fujiki类中常非正Chern全纯截面曲率Hermitian度量必为Kähler。负情形流形为射影、典范丛ample，度量为归一化负Kähler-Einstein度量，万有覆叠为复双曲空间；零情形度量平坦，存在复环面的有限étale覆叠。常正曲率紧平衡三fold双全纯等距于带缩放的Fubini-Study度量的复射影三维空间。

Conclusion: 常Chern全纯截面曲率的紧Hermitian流形在非正与正情形下均呈现强刚性：非正情形由流形几何与典范丛决定，正情形三folds唯一为复射影空间。

Abstract: We prove two rigidity theorems for compact Hermitian manifolds with constant Chern holomorphic sectional curvature. On a manifold in Fujiki's class $\mathcal C$, every Hermitian metric of constant nonpositive Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is Kähler, without a balanced or pluriclosed assumption. In the negative case, the manifold is projective with ample canonical bundle, and the given metric is the normalized negative Kähler--Einstein metric, with complex hyperbolic universal cover. In the zero case, the metric is flat and the manifold admits a finite étale cover by a complex torus. We also prove that a compact balanced threefold of constant positive Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is holomorphically isometric to complex projective three-space with a scaled Fubini--Study metric. The nonpositive argument uses an integrated Chern--Lu identity on a Kähler background. For negative curvature, a lower bound for twisted Kähler--Einstein volumes yields canonical ampleness, and weighted Stokes identities and volume-ratio moment inequalities identify the given metric. At zero curvature, a tensor Bochner argument gives parallelness on a Ricci-flat background. For balanced threefolds, differential compatibility and the torsion energy identities yield a coercive estimate, proved by a rational matrix decomposition valid at every torsion rank.

</details>


### [210] [Compact Vaisman and l.c.K. manifolds with nonnegative bisectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25880)
*Jiangtao Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有非负双截面曲率的紧 Vaisman 流形与 l.c.K. 流形的结构，证明了紧 Vaisman 流形的单值化定理，并对具有非负 Chern 双截面曲率的紧 l.c.K. 流形给出结构三分定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mok 在 Kähler 情形下已有单值化定理，本文希望将其推广到 Vaisman 流形这一非 Kähler 的局部共形 Kähler 特例，并进一步刻画更一般的 l.c.K. 流形在非负曲率条件下的结构。

Method: 在紧 Vaisman 流形与紧 l.c.K. 流形上，引入非负双截面曲率（以及非负 Chern 双截面曲率）条件，结合单值化与结构分析技术进行研究。

Result: 第一，得到紧 Vaisman 流形的单值化定理，推广了 Mok 的 Kähler 单值化定理；第二，得到紧 l.c.K. 流形在非负 Chern 双截面曲率下的结构三分：其底层复流形要么容许 Kähler 度量，要么容许 Vaisman 度量，要么其万有覆盖共形等价于一个非完备 Kähler 流形与正维复欧氏空间的乘积。

Conclusion: 非负曲率条件下的 Vaisman 与 l.c.K. 流形具有高度受限的结构，其分类可归约为 Kähler 情形、Vaisman 情形或乘积型结构。

Abstract: We study the structure of compact Vaisman and l.c.K. manifolds with nonnegative bisectional curvature in this paper. Our first main result (cf. Theorem 1.3) is a uniformization theorem for compact Vaisman manifolds, extending Mok's uniformization theorem in the Kähler setting (cf. [Mok88]). Our second result (cf. Theorem 1.6) gives a structural trichotomy for compact l.c.K. manifolds with nonnegative Chern bisectional curvature: the underlying complex manifold admits a Kähler metric, admits a Vaisman metric, or its universal cover is conformally equivalent to the product of an incomplete Kähler manifold and a positive-dimensional complex Euclidean space.

</details>


### [211] [Weakly Einstein and weakly $η$-Einstein structures on almost contact metric three-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26024)
*Sun Hyang Chun,Yunhee Euh*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究近切触度量三维流形上的弱Einstein与弱η-Einstein结构，给出Ricci算子的逐点结构描述，并分类了单连通齐性切触度量三维流形中的弱Einstein情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱Einstein与弱η-Einstein是比经典Einstein条件更弱的结构，在三维近切触度量几何中其Ricci算子的可能形态尚缺乏系统刻画，作者希望明确这些结构的逐点分类及切触条件下的限制。

Method: 从逐点分析Ricci算子Q入手，对弱η-Einstein近切触度量三维流形刻画Q的结构形态；在切触度量情形下研究Qξ=0条件并确定Q的秩；通过构造反例说明切触假设的必要性；最后对单连通齐性切触度量三维流形进行弱Einstein分类。

Result: 逐点看，Q要么呈η-Einstein形式、要么满足Qξ=0、要么度量为弱Einstein。若切触度量三维流形满足Qξ=0，则Q=0或rank Q=1，故度量或平坦或是弱Einstein但非Einstein。存在满足Qξ=0且rank Q=2的近切触度量三维流形，说明切触假设不可去除。并完成单连通齐性切触度量三维流形中弱Einstein的分类。

Conclusion: 弱η-Einstein近切触度量三维流形的Ricci算子具有清晰的逐点三分结构；切触条件显著限制Qξ=0时的Ricci算子秩，进而导出平坦或弱Einstein（非Einstein）的二分结论，且该限制对一般近切触度量不成立；单连通齐性切触弱Einstein三维流形已获分类。

Abstract: We study weakly Einstein and weakly $η$-Einstein structures on almost contact metric three-manifolds. We first obtain a pointwise structural description of the Ricci operator $Q$ on a weakly $η$-Einstein almost contact metric three-manifold. More precisely, at each point, either $Q$ has the $η$-Einstein form, $Qξ=0$, or the metric is weakly Einstein. We then prove that if a contact metric three-manifold satisfies $Qξ=0$, then either $Q=0$ or $\operatorname{rank}Q=1$. Hence, such a metric is either flat or weakly Einstein but not Einstein. We also construct examples showing that the contact metric assumption is essential: there exist almost contact metric three-manifolds satisfying $Qξ=0$ whose Ricci operators have rank two. Finally, we classify simply connected homogeneous contact metric three-manifolds that are weakly Einstein.

</details>


### [212] [Balanced Bismut torsion-parallel manifold with constant holomorphic sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26089)
*Qingsong Wang,Fangyang Zheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明了平衡BTP Hermitian流形上的常全纯截面曲率猜想在所有维数成立，并构造反例否定了满秩B猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非Kähler几何中的长期猜想断言：具常全纯截面曲率的紧Hermitian流形必为Kähler或Chern平坦。该猜想在2维成立，3维及更高维仅在若干特殊情形下被验证，本文针对平衡BTP流形推进此问题。

Method: 研究Bismut挠率平行的紧Hermitian流形（BTP）。在Chen-Zheng对非平衡BTP情形及3维平衡BTP情形、作者近期对4维平衡BTP情形的基础上，进行一般维数的推广论证，并通过构造具体的紧平衡BTP五维流形来检验满秩B猜想。

Result: 证明了平衡BTP流形上的常全纯截面曲率猜想在所有维数成立；同时构造出一个紧平衡BTP五维流形，其B为满秩且既非Kähler亦非Chern平坦，Chern全纯截面曲率非常数。

Conclusion: 常全纯截面曲率猜想在平衡BTP流形范畴内对所有维数得到确认，但满秩B猜想被否证。

Abstract: A long-lasting conjecture in non-Kähler geometry says that any compact Hermitian manifold with constant holomorphic sectional curvature must be either Kähler or Chern flat. The conjecture is known to be true in dimension 2 but remains open in dimensions 3 or higher, except in several special cases. In this article we consider compact Hermitian manifolds that are Bismut torsion-parallel (BTP for brevity), which means that the Bismut connection has parallel torsion. For BTP manifolds that are not balanced, the conjecture was proved by Chen and Zheng. They also confirmed the conjecture for balanced BTP manifolds in dimension 3, utilizing a classification result for such threefolds by Zhao and Zheng. In a recent work, we confirmed the conjecture for balanced BTP manifolds in dimension 4 through a detailed case-by-case analysis. In this article, we establish the conjecture for balanced BTP manifolds in all dimensions. We also disprove the full-rank $B$ conjecture by constructing a compact balanced BTP fivefold with full-rank $B$ that is neither Kähler nor Chern flat; its Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is nonconstant.

</details>


### [213] [Curvature property on Hermitian Lie algebras with abelian ideals of codimension two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26191)
*Shuwen Chen,Fangyang Zheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究余维数为2的交换理想下的酉模Hermitian李代数的曲率，证明常Chern全纯截面曲率蕴含Chern平坦，并刻画其他典范度规联络的常全纯截面曲率情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究具有交换理想（实余维数2）的单模Hermitian李代数的曲率性质，特别是Chern全纯截面曲率与Chern平坦性之间的关系，以及一般典范度规联络的常全纯截面曲率对其几何结构的约束。

Method: 设 (𝔤,J,g) 为单模Hermitian李代数，含实余维数2的交换理想 𝔞。考察度规 g 的曲率行为：分析Chern联络下常Chern全纯截面曲率的条件；并对除Chern联络外的每一典范度规联络 D_s^r，分析其常全纯截面曲率条件。

Result: 若 g 具有常Chern全纯截面曲率，则 g 必为Chern平坦。对于除Chern联络外的任意典范度规联络 D_s^r，若 D_s^r 具有常全纯截面曲率，则 g 为Kähler平坦，且此时 𝔤/𝔞 为交换李代数。

Conclusion: 常Chern全纯截面曲率在余维数2交换理想的单模Hermitian李代数上强制Chern平坦；其他典范度规联络的常全纯截面曲率则强制Kähler平坦，并诱导商代数 𝔤/𝔞 交换。

Abstract: Let $(\mathfrak g,J,g)$ be a unimodular Hermitian Lie algebra containing an abelian ideal $\mathfrak a$ of real codimension two. We study the curvature behaviour of $g$ and show that, if $g$ has constant Chern holomorphic sectional curvature, then it must be Chern flat. We also show that, for every canonical metric connection $D_s^r$ of $g$ other than the Chern connection, if $D_s^r$ has constant holomorphic sectional curvature, then $g$ is Kähler flat, and in this case $\mathfrak g/\mathfrak a$ is abelian.

</details>


### [214] [An optimal pinching theorem on compact minimal submanifolds in the Euclidean spheres via eigenvalues of fundamental matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26309)
*Huimin Liu,Ling Yang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 利用对称矩阵李括号平方范数之和的上界不等式，通过基本矩阵特征值（第二基本形式平方范数在所有法向上的临界值）建立了欧氏球面中紧致极小子流形的刚性定理，结论在任意维数下最优且不受余维数限制，给出了广义Clifford环面与Veronese流形的新刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小子流形的刚性问题是子流形几何的核心课题之一。经典结果中，对球面内紧致极小子流形的刻画常依赖较强的维数或余维数条件，例如余维数限制或特定维数假设，适用范围受限。本文旨在寻找一种更普适、能突破余维数限制的刚性刻画工具。

Method: 建立关于对称矩阵李括号平方范数之和的上界不等式；以此为基础，引入基本矩阵——即第二基本形式平方范数沿所有法向方向取到的临界值——并考察其全部特征值；利用这些特征值构造刚性条件，把几何量转化为线性代数中矩阵谱的问题。

Result: 得到了欧氏球面中紧致极小子流形的一个刚性定理，其条件由基本矩阵的特征值给出。所得结论在所有维数下都是最优的，且不附加任何余维数限制。该刚性定理同时给出了广义Clifford环面和Veronese流形的新刻画。

Conclusion: 本文借助李括号不等式与基本矩阵特征值，统一刻画了广义Clifford环面与Veronese流形这两类典型极小子流形，成为其在任意维数、无余维数限制下的新特征，拓展了极小子流形刚性理论的适用范围。

Abstract: Based on an inequality on the upper bound of the sum of squared norms of Lie bracket of symmetric matrices, we establish a rigidity theorem for compact minimal submanifolds in the Euclidean spheres via eigenvalues of fundamental matrices, which are the critical values of the squared norms of the second fundamental form on all normal directions. This conclusion is optimal for all dimensions without the restriction of the codimension, giving a new characterization for generalized Clifford tori and Veronese manifolds.

</details>


### [215] [The $J$-Null Locus and Local Regularity of Weak Solutions of the Semistable $J$-Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26340)
*Hao Fang,Biao Ma*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明半稳定 $J$-方程的数值 $J$-零轨迹与 Murakami 弱解的 $C^2$-奇异轨迹重合，进而给出该奇异轨迹的解析结构与弱解的正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确半稳定 $J$-方程弱解奇异集的几何刻画，将数值不变量（$J$-零轨迹）与分析奇异轨迹对应起来，并刻画其解析性质与正则性。

Method: 两个核心分析工具：一是奇异 $J$-子解的 regularization 定理，证明 Demailly 全局正则化保持定量严格锥条件，从而用光滑严格子解在极点集外替代具指定解析极点的奇异严格子解；二是相对先验估计，包括由行列式控制得到的相对 $L^\infty$ 估计与加权二阶估计，给出正则轨迹紧子集上一致 $C^2$ 界。

Result: 数值 $J$-零轨迹与弱解的 $C^2$-奇异轨迹相等；该奇异轨迹为真解析子集，具有限个不可约分支；弱解在 $J$-零轨迹外局部光滑。

Conclusion: 通过正则化与相对先验估计，建立了 $J$-零轨迹与奇异轨迹的等价性，刻画了弱解奇异集的解析结构与外部的光滑性。

Abstract: For the semistable $J$-equation, we prove that the numerical $J$-null locus coincides with the ambient $C^2$-singular locus of Murakami's weak solution. Consequently, this singular locus is a proper analytic subset with finitely many irreducible components, and the weak solution is locally smooth outside the $J$-null locus.
  The proof relies on two analytic ingredients. First, we establish a regularization theorem for singular $J$-subsolutions, showing that Demailly's global regularization preserves quantitative strict cone conditions. Thus, singular strict subsolutions with prescribed analytic poles can be replaced by smooth strict subsolutions away from their pole sets. Second, we derive relative a priori estimates adapted to these subsolutions: a relative $L^\infty$-estimate from determinant control and a weighted second order estimate yielding uniform $C^2$-bounds on compact subsets of the regular locus.

</details>


### [216] [Integral of Scalar Curvature: Sharp Asymptotic Bounds and Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26357)
*Guoyi Xu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 对非负截面曲率的完备非紧流形（3≤n≤6），建立总数量曲率积分上界，涉及渐近体积比AVR，并给出刚性、等号分类以及闭流形的尖锐总量曲率积分界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非负截面曲率流形上数量曲率整体积分与其渐近几何（渐近体积比AVR）之间的定量关系，寻求类似Colding体积收敛与数量曲率下界之间的刚性刻划。

Method: 对完备非紧流形利用体积比较与数量曲率积分（r^{2-n}∫_{B_p(r)}Sc dV）的大r极限分析；有极点情形另用距离球面的独立证明；对闭流形则采用总量曲率积分方法得到尖锐界。

Result: 在3≤n≤6的非负截面曲率完备非紧流形上获得 lim r^{2-n}∫_{B_p(r)}Sc dV ≤ 8πω_{n-2}(1-AVR)，并给出等号刚性刻划；正维灵魂情形同样成立。存在极点时得到更强界 4πω_{n-2}(1-AVR)。同时给出闭流形的尖锐总量曲率积分上界。

Conclusion: 渐近体积比AVR控制总数量曲率积分的极限上界，等号对应刚性结构；极点情形可通过距离球面方法得到更强估计，闭流形亦有尖锐积分界。

Abstract: For any complete noncompact manifolds $(M^n, g)$ of nonnegative sectional curvature, with $3\le n\le6$, we obtain $\displaystyle \lim_{r\to\infty}r^{2-n}\int_{B_p(r)}\Sc_g\,dV_g\le8πω_{n-2}(1-\AVR(M^n,g))$ and the corresponding rigidity. The latter bound and its equality classification hold in $(M^n, g)$ with positive-dimensional souls. With a pole, $\displaystyle \lim_{r\to\infty}r^{2-n}\int_{B_p(r)}\Sc_g\,dV_g\le4πω_{n-2}(1-\AVR(M^n,g))$ follows from an independent distance-sphere proof. We also prove the sharp total scalar-integral bound for closed manifolds whose c

</details>


### [217] [Lower bounds for the critical CR GJMS operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26371)
*Yuya Takeuchi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文建立了闭伪埃尔米特流形上临界CR GJMS算子的下界：在维数2n+1且假设可嵌入时，得到模L²项的n+1阶Heisenberg Sobolev范数下界；在三维情形无需可嵌入假设证明CR Paneitz算子有下界，并给出了Rossi球上负特征值仅以零为聚点的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究临界CR GJMS算子的下有界性（下界估计）问题，刻画其在核的正交补上的行为，并探讨CR Paneitz算子的谱性质与闭值域性质，同时比较可嵌入与不可嵌入情形下的差异。

Method: 将Heisenberg Calculus与基于Heisenberg群上次Laplacian与Reeb向量场的联合泛函演算的平方根构造相结合。

Result: 在维数2n+1、假设可嵌入时，得到核的正交补上模L²项的n+1阶Heisenberg Sobolev范数下界；在三维情形无需可嵌入假设，证明CR Paneitz算子有下界。作为应用，证明Rossi球上CR Paneitz算子的无穷多个负特征值仅以零为聚点，从而该算子不具有闭值域。

Conclusion: 临界CR GJMS算子与CR Paneitz算子具有上述下界性质，其中三维结果不依赖可嵌入性；Rossi球上的CR Paneitz算子因负特征值以零为唯一聚点而不具有闭值域。

Abstract: We establish lower bounds for the critical CR GJMS operator on closed pseudo-Hermitian manifolds. In dimension $2n+1$, assuming embeddability, we obtain a lower bound involving the Heisenberg Sobolev norm of order $n+1$, modulo an $L^{2}$ term, on the orthogonal complement of the kernel. In dimension three, we prove that the CR Paneitz operator is bounded below without assuming embeddability. The proofs combine the Heisenberg calculus with a square-root construction based on the joint functional calculus of the sub-Laplacian and the Reeb vector field on the Heisenberg group. As an application, we show that the infinitely many negative eigenvalues of the CR Paneitz operator on the Rossi sphere have zero as their only accumulation point. Consequently, this operator does not have closed range.

</details>


### [218] [Type changing, compact generalized Calabi--Yaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26387)
*Gil R. Cavalcanti*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 利用Fei在扭量空间分支覆盖上构造非Kähler Calabi–Yau流形的方法，首次给出紧致型变广义Calabi–Yau流形的例子，其类型由type 1变为type 3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前缺少紧致的"型变"广义Calabi–Yau流形具体例子，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以亏格三的超椭圆曲面与四维环面的乘积Σ_3 × T^4为底空间，采用Fei在扭量空间分支覆盖上的非Kähler Calabi–Yau构造，并从一个非Kähler Calabi–Yau结构出发进行构造。

Result: 得到紧致的广义Calabi–Yau结构，其类型一般为type 1，并在某些条件下转变为type 3。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了首个紧致的型变广义Calabi–Yau流形实例，表明广义Calabi–Yau结构可在同一流形上发生类型变化。

Abstract: We provide the first compact examples of type-changing generalized Calabi--Yau manifolds using Fei's construction of non-Kähler Calabi--Yau manifolds on branched covers of twistor spaces \cite{Fei16}. The structure, which is generically of type 1 and changes to type 3, is constructed on the product of a hyperelliptic surface of genus three and a four-torus, $Σ_3 \times \mathbb{T}^4$, with starting point a non-Kähler Calabi--Yau structure.

</details>


### [219] [Kählerian oxidation and reduction of Lie algebras with Kähler structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26417)
*Mustapha Bachaou,Ignacio Bajo,Mohamed Louzari*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究李代数上的Kähler结构，重点讨论幂零情形，建立了一维与二维理想上的Kähler约化与氧化代数框架，并证明高维不可分解拟幂零Kähler幂零李代数可由有限次Kähler中心氧化归纳重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kähler结构在复几何与李代数理论中具有基础性地位，而幂零李代数上的Kähler结构分类与结构刻画尚不完善，需要代数化的约化与氧化工具来揭示其内在构造规律。

Method: 作者在实李代数g上定义Kähler结构为相容的2-余循环ω与复结构J（J关于ω反对称），引入关于一维和二维理想的Kähler约化（reduction）与氧化（oxidation）代数框架，并通过归纳构造分析不可分解拟幂零Kähler幂零李代数。

Result: 主要结构定理表明，任意维数大于四的不可分解、拟幂零Kähler幂零李代数都可通过有限次以平面（planes）进行的Kähler中心氧化归纳重构；该约化序列源于一个基代数，其要么为{0}、R^2，要么具有强非幂零复结构。若复结构为幂零，则所有中间约化也具有幂零复结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为幂零李代数上的Kähler结构提供了统一的归纳构造与分类框架，表明高维情形可由低维基代数经中心氧化逐步生成，并揭示了复结构幂零性在约化过程中的保持性质。

Abstract: A Kähler structure on a real Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$ is a pair $(ω, J)$ of a 2-cocycle and a complex structure on $\mathfrak{g}$ which are compatible in the sense that $J$ is skew-symmetric with respect to $ω$. This paper investigates Kähler structures on Lie algebras, with a particular focus on the nilpotent case. We introduce an algebraic framework for the Kählerian reduction and oxidation of Lie algebras by one-dimensional and two-dimensional ideals. Our main structural result demonstrates that every indecomposable, quasi-nilpotent Kählerian nilpotent Lie algebra of dimension greater than four can be inductively reconstructed through a finite sequence of Kählerian central oxidations by planes. This reduction sequence originates from a base algebra that is either $\{0\}$, $\mathbb{R}^2$, or one admitting a strongly non-nilpotent complex structure. Moreover, if the complex structure is nilpotent, all intermediate reductions also have a nilpotent complex structure.

</details>


### [220] [ALH Gravitational Instantons and Rational Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26439)
*Yifan Chen,Chunhui Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在光滑有理曲面上反典范椭圆曲线的补集上构造了完备ALH超凯勒度量，正面回答了Filip–Tosatti的存在性问题；并反向证明每个ALH超凯勒引力瞬子均可由有理曲面中的反典范椭圆曲线紧化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Filip–Tosatti提出的存在性问题，并与Haskins–Hein–Nordström所提出的图景相呼应。

Method: 在光滑有理曲面上选取自交数为零的反典范椭圆曲线，对其补集构造完备的ALH（渐近局部双曲）超凯勒度量；并以紧化视角对ALH引力瞬子进行分类刻画。

Result: 构造出所要求的完备ALH超凯勒度量，解决存在性问题；同时证明每个ALH超凯勒引力瞬子对每个诱导复结构都可实现为有理曲面中反典范椭圆曲线的紧化。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了ALH超凯勒引力瞬子与有理曲面上反典范椭圆曲线紧化之间的一一对应关系，实现了正反两个方向的刻画。

Abstract: We construct complete ALH hyperkähler metrics on complements of anticanonical elliptic curves with zero self-intersection in smooth rational surfaces. This answers the existence question of Filip--Tosatti, related to the picture proposed by Haskins--Hein--Nordström. Conversely, we prove that every hyperkähler ALH gravitational instanton admits, for each induced complex structure, a compactification by an anticanonical elliptic curve in a rational surface.

</details>


### [221] [The optimal diameter estimate for positive Yamabe metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26455)
*Kazuo Akutagawa,Mijia Lai,Chao Li,Harish Seshadri,Zhixin Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了连通闭流形上正 Yamabe 度量的最优直径估计，可视为经典 Myers 直径估计与 Cheng 最大直径定理在数量曲率情形下的类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Myers 定理与 Cheng 最大直径定理刻画了 Ricci 曲率下界对直径的控制及其刚性，但数量曲率情形下的相应直径估计尚待解决。

Method: 在连通闭流形上，针对正 Yamabe 度量（即所在共形类中存在正数量曲率度量）建立直径上界，并证明该估计是最优的。

Result: 得到了正 Yamabe 度量的最优直径估计。

Conclusion: 该结果是 Ricci 曲率下 Myers 直径估计与 Cheng 最大直径定理在数量曲率框架下的对应版本，表明 Yamabe 不变量为正时可获得类似的直径刚性结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the optimal diameter estimate for positive Yamabe metrics on a connected, closed manifold. It is a scalar curvature version of both classical Myers' diameter estimate and Cheng's maximal diameter theorem on Ricci curvature.

</details>


### [222] [Volume Growth of Self-Shrinkers of the $Q_k$-Flow in $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26559)
*Junior Tavares,Detang Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文在无曲率假设下，利用恰当函数与对称张量满足的微分不等式，建立完备黎曼流形的一般体积估计，并应用于欧氏空间中一类可定向超曲面的多项式体积增长与有限加权体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统体积估计多依赖曲率假设，而许多几何对象（如自收缩子、极小超曲面）未必满足这些条件，因此需要在不施加曲率假设的情形下建立普适的体积估计工具。

Method: 通过引入恰当函数（proper function）与对称张量，设定二者之间的合适微分不等式，在一般完备黎曼流形上推导体积估计，无需曲率条件。

Result: 证明欧氏空间中一类可定向、恰当浸入超曲面至多具有多项式体积增长，且具有有限加权体积；该类包括Q_k-流的某些自收缩子，以及位置向量法向分量有界的恰当浸入极小超曲面。

Conclusion: 建立了一个不依赖曲率假设的通用体积估计框架，并为自收缩子与极小超曲面等具体几何对象提供了体积增长与有限加权体积的结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a general volume estimate for complete Riemannian manifolds under suitable differential inequalities involving a proper function and a symmetric tensor, without imposing curvature assumptions. As an application, we prove that a class of orientable hypersurfaces properly immersed in Euclidean space has at most polynomial volume growth and finite weighted volume. This class includes, in particular, certain self-shrinkers of the $Q_k$-flow and properly immersed minimal hypersurfaces for which the normal component of the position vector is bounded.

</details>


### [223] [$L^2$ Curvature Bounds for Area-Minimizing Currents Mod(2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26592)
*Zachary Lihn*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明模2面积最小流第二基本形式的先验内部L²界，并推出有界面积下有限微分同胚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答 Liu (2020) 关于模2面积最小曲面分类的公开问题。

Method: 对任意维数 n≥2 与任意余维数的模2面积最小流，建立第二基本形式 A 的先验内部 L² 估计，界仅依赖维数、余维与质量（面积）上界。

Result: 得到 A 的普适内部 L² 界；作为推论，有界面积的模2面积最小曲面只落在有限多个微分同胚类中。

Conclusion: 该估计回答并解决了 Liu 2020 提出的反例相关问题，为模2面积最小流提供了正则性/紧性方面的定量控制。

Abstract: We prove \emph{a priori} interior $L^2$ bounds for the second fundamental form of area-minimizing currents modulo $2$ in every dimension $n\geq 2$ and arbitrary codimension. The bounds depend only on the dimension, codimension, and an upper bound for the mass. As a corollary, we show that area-minimizing surfaces modulo $2$ with bounded area lie in finitely many diffeomorphism classes. This answers a question of \cite{Liu2020WeakCounterexamples}.

</details>


### [224] [Immersed hypersurfaces with positive first Newton transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26665)
*Jiangcheng You,Heng Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了具有正定第一牛顿变换的闭余定向超曲面浸入可界定一个紧致浸入流形，结合Ros与Pinkall的刚性结果推出常标量曲率闭超曲面必为圆球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究常标量曲率闭超曲面浸入的刚性分类问题，试图通过填充（filling）定理与已有刚性结果结合来刻画其几何形状。

Method: 对R^{n+1}（n≥2）中闭余定向超曲面浸入，在具有正定第一牛顿变换的条件下，构造并证明其可界定一个紧致浸入流形，且保持给定的边界映射与外向余定向。

Result: 建立了该填充定理；将其与Ros和Pinkall的刚性结果结合，证明R^{n+1}中任意闭连通常标量曲率超曲面浸入必为圆球。

Conclusion: 常标量曲率闭超曲面浸入的刚性可由正定第一牛顿变换下的填充性质导出，从而完全分类为圆球。

Abstract: We prove that every closed cooriented hypersurface immersion in $\R^{n+1}$, $n\ge 2$, with positive definite first Newton transformation bounds a compact immersed manifold with the prescribed boundary map and outward coorientation. Combined with the rigidity results of Ros and Pinkall, this filling theorem implies that every closed connected hypersurface immersed in $\R^{n+1}$ with constant scalar curvature is a round sphere.

</details>


### [225] [Minimal equatorial fillings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26701)
*Jacob Bernstein,Daniel Ketover*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了 S³ 中的大圆可作为边界,界定无穷多个具有无界亏格及任意非零欧拉数的嵌入不可定向极小曲面,从而回答了 Hardt–Rosenberg (1990) 的问题,并首次给出界定无穷多不同拓扑型嵌入极小曲面的实解析 Jordan 曲线例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: R. Hardt 与 H. Rosenberg (1990) 提出的问题涉及大圆能否界定嵌入的不可定向极小曲面,以及这些曲面的亏格与欧拉数取值范围。此前缺乏对实解析 Jordan 曲线界定无穷多个不同拓扑型嵌入极小曲面的例子。

Method: 构造方法基于 S³ 中大圆作为边界的嵌入不可定向极小曲面构造,通过控制曲面的亏格(无界)与欧拉数(任意非零)来实现。所得曲面可视为 R⁴ 中不可定向极小超曲面边界奇点的链(links)。

Result: S³ 中大圆界定嵌入非定向极小曲面,亏格无界,且可取到所有非零欧拉数;由此得到首个界定无穷多个不同拓扑型嵌入极小曲面的实解析 Jordan 曲线例子;这些曲面可作为 R⁴ 中不可定向极小超曲面边界奇点的链出现。

Conclusion: 该工作完整回答了 Hardt–Rosenberg 的问题,并揭示了实解析 Jordan 曲线在极小曲面边界问题中的丰富拓扑结构。

Abstract: We show that a great circle in $\mathbb{S}^3$ bounds embedded non-orientable minimal surfaces of unbounded genera and all possible non-zero Euler numbers. This answers a question of R. Hardt and H. Rosenberg (1990). It also gives the first example of a real analytic Jordan curve bounding infinitely many embedded minimal surfaces of distinct topological types. Such surfaces may arise as the links of boundary singularities of non-orientable minimal hypersurfaces in $\mathbb{R}^4$.

</details>


### [226] [Compact Kähler surfaces with semipositive anticanonical bundle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26716)
*Yifan Chen,Simion Filip,Song Sun,Valentino Tosatti,Junsheng Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对具有半正反规范丛的紧Kähler曲面进行分类，并解决了Demailly–Peternell–Schneider提出的问题：光滑三次曲线上9点处P²的任意爆破具有半正反规范丛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Demailly–Peternell–Schneider曾提出关于半正反规范丛的紧Kähler曲面分类问题，特别是光滑三次曲线上9点爆破P²的情形尚待解决，本文旨在完成这一分类工作。

Method: 对紧Kähler曲面的反规范丛半正性条件进行分析与分类论证。

Result: 给出了半正反规范丛紧Kähler曲面的完整分类，并证明光滑三次曲线上9点处P²的任意爆破均具有半正反规范丛。

Conclusion: 解决了Demailly–Peternell–Schneider提出的问题，完善了半正反规范丛紧Kähler曲面的分类理论。

Abstract: We classify compact Kähler surfaces with semipositive anticanonical bundle. In particular, we resolve a question of Demailly--Peternell--Schneider: any blowup of $\mathbb{P}^2$ at 9 points on a smooth cubic has semipositive anticanonical bundle.

</details>


### [227] [Scalar curvature growth on nonnegatively curved three-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26717)
*Qixuan Hu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文在完备连通非紧无边界、非负截面曲率的三维流形$(M^3,g)$上，用水平集方法给出了标量曲率积分渐近上界的证明。单端情形得到 $\limsup_{r\to\infty}\frac1r\int_{B_p(r)}\operatorname{Scal}\,dV\le 8\pi(1-V_M)$，不假设极点、非坍缩或标量曲率界；双端情形由分裂定理得实际上界 $16\pi$。两个界均可被达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非负截面曲率三维流形上标量曲率积分的渐近行为，希望在尽量弱的假设（不设极点、不设非坍缩条件、不设标量曲率界）下获得标量曲率积分增长速度的上界，并探讨其最优性。

Method: 采用水平集论证：构造几乎凸的光滑穷举，并配合对距离函数的独立光滑逼近；证明水平集的负曲率误差项可积。双端情形借助分裂定理处理。

Result: 单端情形得到 $\limsup_{r\to\infty}\frac1r\int_{B_p(r)}\operatorname{Scal}\,dV\le 8\pi(1-V_M)$；双端情形得到实际极限且上界为 $16\pi$。单端界对任意规定的渐近体积比 $V_M\in[0,1]$ 均可达到，双端常数也可达到。

Conclusion: 给出了非负截面曲率三维流形标量曲率积分渐近增长的最佳上界；单端结论为上限估计，未断言对应极限存在；双端由分裂定理给出实际极限。

Abstract: Let $(M^3,g)$ be complete, connected, noncompact, and without boundary, with nonnegative sectional curvature. We give a level-set argument for an asymptotic upper bound on the scalar-curvature integral. In the one-ended case the argument yields \[
  \limsup_{r\to\infty}\frac1r\int_{B_p(r)}\operatorname{Scal}\,d V
  \le 8π\bigl(1-{V_M}\bigr). \] No pole, noncollapsing condition, or scalar-curvature bound is assumed. The proof uses an almost convex smooth exhaustion together with a separate smooth approximation to the distance function. The negative curvature errors of the level sets are integrable. In the two-ended case the splitting theorem gives an actual limit bounded by $16π$. The one-ended bound is attained for every prescribed asymptotic volume ratio in $[0,1]$; the two-ended constant is also attained. The one-ended conclusion is an upper-limit bound; existence of the corresponding limit is not asserted.

</details>


### [228] [A proof of the Willmore-type conjecture in $\mathbb{C}P^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26721)
*Peng Wang,Zhenxiao Xie,Chen Zhao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在Lagrangian情形下证明了Montiel-Urbano猜想，并在一般情形下否定了该猜想：Clifford环面是Lagrangian环面中W^-泛函的极小者，但存在非Lagrangian变分使W^-严格下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2002年Montiel与Urbano提出猜想：C P^2中的Clifford环面在W^-=∫(2+|H|^2)dA泛函下，或在全体环面中、或在Lagrangian环面中为极小。该猜想的两种情形此前未被区分验证，作者意在分别判定其真伪。

Method: 将问题限制在Lagrangian曲面情形，利用Lagrangian几何与W^-泛函的结构性质，对C P^2中任意亏格g≥1的定向闭Lagrangian曲面建立能量下界；同时沿非Lagrangian方向构造Clifford环面的形变，计算W^-的变化。

Result: 证明了C P^2中任意亏格g≥1的定向闭Lagrangian曲面的W^-能量不低于Clifford环面的能量，即猜想在Lagrangian情形成立；并构造出使W^-严格减小的非Lagrangian形变，说明该猜想在一般情形不成立。

Conclusion: Montiel-Urbano猜想仅在Lagrangian范围内正确：Clifford环面是Lagrangian曲面类中的W^-极小者，但在全体曲面的一般情形下并非极小，其非Lagrangian形变可进一步降低W^-能量。

Abstract: In 2002, Montiel and Urbano conjectured that the Clifford torus in $\mathbb{C}P^2$ minimizes the Willmore-type functional $ \mathcal{W}^-=\int_{T^2}(2+|H|^2)\,dA,$ either among all tori or among all Lagrangian tori. In this paper, we confirm this conjecture in the Lagrangian setting and disprove it in the general setting. We establish that every oriented closed Lagrangian surface of genus $g\geq 1$ in $\mathbb{C}P^2$ has $\mathcal{W}^-$-energy no less than that of the Clifford torus. Moreover, we construct non-Lagrangian deformations of the Clifford torus along which $\mathcal{W}^-$ strictly decreases.

</details>


### [229] [On the Willmore energy of Möbius bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26745)
*Jacob Bernstein,Daniel Ketover*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中以大圆为边界的 Möbius 带中，最小 Willmore 能量由 Morse 指标为 2 的嵌入极小 Möbius 带实现；证明引入 2-参数"典范族"并利用 min-max 论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中以大圆为边界的非可定向曲面的 Willmore 能量极小化问题，并试图推进 Kusner 1989 年关于 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-不变 Klein 瓶的猜想。

Method: 对任意以大圆为边界的 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中非可定向曲面引入 2-参数"典范族"，该族可检测曲面的 Euler 数（受 Marques–Neves 检测可定向曲面亏格的 5-参数族启发），并应用 min-max 论证。

Result: 在 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中以大圆为边界的 Möbius 带中，最小 Willmore 能量由嵌入极小 Möbius 带实现且其 Morse 指标为 2。

Conclusion: 对 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-不变 Klein 瓶，将 Kusner 1989 年猜想（$\tau_{1,2}$ 在 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 浸入 Klein 瓶中极小化 Willmore 能量）归约为对 Lawson Möbius 带的若干猜想性刻画之一。

Abstract: We show that among Möbius bands in $\mathbb{S}^3$ bounded by a great circle, the minimal Willmore energy is realized by an embedded minimal Möbius band with Morse index two. To prove this, we introduce a $2$-parameter ``canonical family" associated to any non-orientable surface in $\mathbb{S}^3$ with boundary a great circle and apply a min-max argument. The canonical family detects the Euler number of the surface and is inspired by the $5$-parameter family discovered by F.C. Marques and A. Neves detecting the genus of an orientable surface in $\mathbb{S}^3$. For $\mathbb{Z}_2$-invariant Klein bottles, this reduces R. Kusner's 1989 conjecture that $τ_{1,2}$ minimizes the Willmore energy for a Klein bottle immersed in $\mathbb{S}^3$ to any of several conjectural characterizations of the Lawson Möbius band.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Monoidal Gröbner systems and categories of affine Brauer type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25159)
*Sigiswald Barbier,Léo Schelstraete*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入线性（超）幺半范畴的Gröbner基（即Bergman菱形引理）的类比——幺半Gröbner系统，基于高阶线性重写理论，为研究幺半理想与证明基定理提供系统方法。作者将该理论应用于仿射Brauer型范畴，给出判定准则，并将问题归约为有限局部计算，部分用FORM实现，从而得到仿射Brauer范畴、nil-Brauer范畴与仿射VW超范畴基定理的新组合证明及新例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿射Brauer型范畴（hom-基与仿射Brauer范畴相同但合成与张量积可能不同）的基定理通常需要繁琐的逐例验证，缺乏统一、系统的理论工具；同时希望构造新的范畴（如odd nil-Brauer范畴、量子化仿射VW超范畴）以支持范畴化猜想与高阶Schur--Weyl对偶。

Method: 1) 为线性（超）幺半范畴引入幺半Gröbner系统的概念，类比Gröbner基与Bergman菱形引理，基于高阶线性重写理论研究幺半理想；2) 提出仿射Brauer型范畴的判定准则，将判定问题归约为显式的局部计算清单；3) 在计算机代数系统FORM中部分实现这些局部计算；4) 对承认足够简单展示的仿射Brauer型范畴进行分类。

Result: 给出了仿射Brauer型范畴的判定准则；得到了仿射Brauer范畴、nil-Brauer范畴与仿射VW超范畴基定理的新组合证明，并构造出新例子：odd nil-Brauer范畴（秩一分裂$\imath$量子群的猜想性超范畴化）与量子化仿射VW超范畴（其非仿射部分为量子化periplectic Brauer范畴，预期满足高阶量子Schur--Weyl对偶）；最后对承认足够简单展示的仿射Brauer型范畴完成分类。

Conclusion: 幺半Gröbner系统为线性（超）幺半范畴提供了统一的基定理证明框架，并把仿射Brauer型范畴的判定化归为可计算的局部条件，兼具理论统一性与构造新范畴的效力。

Abstract: We introduce an analogue of Gröbner bases, or equivalently, Bergman's diamond lemma, for linear (super)monoidal categories. It gives a systematic way to study monoidal ideals and to prove basis theorems. The central notion is that of a monoidal Gröbner system and is based on higher linear rewriting theory. We then apply the theory to categories of affine Brauer type: linear (super)monoidal categories that have the same hom-basis as the affine Brauer category, but possibly distinct composition and tensor product. Our main result is a criterion for a category to be of affine Brauer type, reducing the question to an explicit list of local computations, which we partially implement in the computer algebra system FORM. This gives new combinatorial proofs of the basis theorems for the affine Brauer category, the nil-Brauer category and the affine VW supercategory, and yields new examples. In particular, we construct the odd nil-Brauer category, a conjectural supercategorification of the split $\imath$quantum group of rank one, and the quantized affine VW supercategory, whose non-affine part is the quantized periplectic Brauer category and which we expect to satisfy a higher quantum Schur--Weyl duality. Finally, we classify the categories of affine Brauer type admitting a sufficiently simple presentation.

</details>


### [231] [An irreducible decomposition of the Weil representation restricted to open compact subgroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25045)
*Yuta Takahashi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 利用Schrödinger模型，对任意剩余特征数的p进域，确定Weil表示限制到极大开紧子群与Iwahori子群上的不可约分解，并证明这些限制是重数自由的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weil表示在p进域上的限制分解是表示论与相关调和分析中的基础问题，但此前结果多受限于特定剩余特征数（如奇特征）或特定子群。作者希望统一处理任意剩余特征数，并给出明确的不可约分解结构。

Method: 采用Schrödinger模型具体实现Weil表示，并在此模型下分析其限制到极大开紧子群和Iwahori子群的作用，从而计算分解。

Result: 给出了Weil表示限制到极大开紧子群和Iwahori子群时的不可约分解；由这些分解进一步得出限制是重数自由的。

Conclusion: 通过Schrödinger模型可统一地刻画任意剩余特征数下Weil表示在紧子群上的限制，且限制具有重数自由性。

Abstract: For a $p$-adic field of any residual characteristic, we determine irreducible decompositions of the Weil representation restricted to the maximal open compact subgroups and the Iwahori subgroup by using the Schrödinger model. In particular, these decompositions show that the restrictions are multiplicity free.

</details>


### [232] [Brauer webs of finite and affine type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26498)
*Yaolong Shen,Linliang Song,Weiqiang Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文引入并构建了有限与仿射B-web范畴的整图框架，给出气泡初等图的整基与特征零域上的简化呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源自型BCD李代数的表示范畴，希望将先前的仿射web范畴推广为Brauer型结构。

Method: 发展整图（integral diagrammatic）框架，构造两个新的幺半范畴：有限与仿射B-web范畴。

Result: 建立了由气泡初等图组成的整基，并在特征零域上获得了这些范畴的简化呈现。

Conclusion: 该框架统一刻画了有限与仿射B-web范畴，可作为仿射web范畴的Brauer型推广。

Abstract: We introduce and develop an integral diagrammatic framework for two new monoidal categories, the finite and affine B-web categories. They arise naturally from representation categories of type BCD Lie algebras, and can be viewed as a Brauer type generalization of the affine web category which we introduced earlier. Integral bases consisting of bubbled elementary diagrams are established for the finite and affine B-webs. A simplified presentation of these categories over a field of characteristic zero is also obtained.

</details>


### [233] [Categorical representations of algebraic groups in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25148)
*Pramod N. Achar,Gurbir Dhillon,Simon Riche*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为有限型仿射群概形建立了范畴表示论的形式化框架（弱与强两种设定），适用于任意基域，并研究了其中的范畴迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为仿射群概形的表示论（含 Harish-Chandra 双模）提供统一的 $\infty$-范畴框架，使其适用于任意基域，并借助范畴迹刻画表示范畴的结构。

Method: 建立适合研究此类群概形表示与 Harish-Chandra（双）模的 $\infty$-范畴设定，以此作为该结构的基本例子与构件；在此基础上研究范畴迹。

Result: 证明在正特征代数闭域 $k$ 上连通约化代数群 $G$ 的表示 $\infty$-范畴中，恒等态射（相应 Frobenius 态射）的范畴迹在适当假设下等同于伴随商 $G/G$（相应 Frobenius 不动点 $G^{\mathrm{F}}$）上 Ind-相干层 $\infty$-范畴。

Conclusion: 该框架将范畴表示论推广至任意基域上的有限型仿射群概形，并通过范畴迹给出表示范畴与伴随商、Frobenius 不动点上 Ind-相干层范畴的等同。

Abstract: In this paper we present a formalism of categorical representation theory for affine group schemes of finite type over fields (both in the weak and strong settings) which applies to arbitrary base fields. This is based on the development of an appropriate $\infty$-categorical setting for the study of representations and Harish-Chandra (bi)modules of such group schemes, which constitute the building blocks and basic examples of such structures. We also study categorical traces in this context, and show in particular that the categorical trace of the identity morphism, resp.~Frobenius morphism, on the appropriate $\infty$-category of representations of a connected reductive algebraic group $G$ over an algebraically closed field $k$ of positive characteristic identifies (under suitable assumptions) with the $\infty$-category of Ind-coherent sheaves on the adjoint quotient $G/G$, resp.~of the fixed points $G^{\mathrm{F}}$ of the Frobenius.

</details>


### [234] [A geometric realization of socle-projective categories for posets of type $\mathbb{D}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25174)
*Gabriel Bravo Rios,Ralf Schiffler,Robinson-Julian Serna*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文引入由 D_n 型可容许 Dynkin 箭图及相容"异向箭"构成的 D 型偏序集，证明其 socle-投射表示范畴为有限表示型，并构造该范畴与穿孔 (n+3)-边形弧范畴子范畴之间的 k-线性等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续 A 型偏序集 socle-投射范畴几何实现的研究纲领，将这一框架推广至几何组合更为复杂的 D_n 型，为该类范畴提供几何模型并联系簇代数。

Method: 以 D_n 型可容许 Dynkin 箭图加上相容集合的异向箭刻画 D 型偏序集 P；基于已有的 D_n 型簇倾斜代数几何模型，取穿孔 (n+3)-边形弧范畴 C/T 的由特定 sp-弧构成的全子范畴 (C/T)_F，构造其与有限生成 socle-投射 kP-模范畴之间的 k-线性范畴等价 Θ。

Result: 建立范畴等价 Θ: (C/T)_F → mod kP（socle-投射部分）；得到 D 型偏序集的 socle-投射表示范畴为有限表示型；当异向箭集合为空时，由 sp-弧生成的簇子代数与完整簇代数重合。

Conclusion: D 型偏序集的 socle-投射范畴可由多边形的 sp-弧组合地实现，推广了 A 型的结果，并给出簇子代数与完整簇代数一致的一个充分条件。

Abstract: We introduce posets of type $\mathbb{D}$, a family of posets described by an admissible Dynkin quiver of type $D_n$ together with a compatible set of extra arrows (alien arrows), and show that their socle-projective representation category is of finite representation type. Continuing, in the same spirit, a program initiated for posets of type $\mathbb{A}$ [R. Schiffler, R-J. Serna, A geometric realization of socle-projective categories for posets of type $\mathbb{A}$, J. Pure Appl. Algebra 224 (2020), no. 12, 106436], we build on an existing geometric model for cluster-tilted algebras of type $D_n$, whose geometric combinatorics is more intricate than that of type $\mathbb{A}$. Our main result establishes a $\Bbbk$-linear categorical equivalence $Θ$ between a full subcategory $(\mathcal C/T)_F$ of the arc category of a punctured $(n+3)$-gon, whose objects are certain $sp$-arcs, and the category of finitely generated socle-projective $\Bbbk\mathscr{P}$-modules, for $\mathscr{P}$ a poset of type $\mathbb{D}$. As a consequence, when the set of alien arrows is empty, we conclude that the cluster subalgebra generated by the $sp$-arcs coincides with the full cluster algebra.

</details>


### [235] [Indecomposable characters of the wreath product of a torus by the infinite block-diagonal symmetric group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25275)
*Nikolay Nessonov,Nhok Tkhai Shon Ngo*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文完整刻画了半直积 T^N ⋊ S_N 的块对角归纳极限的所有不可分解特征标，并给出了无穷酉群块对角嵌入情形下不可分解特征标分类的另一证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为 T^N ⋊ S_N 的块对角归纳极限，其不可分解特征标（中心正定函数）的完整描述此前尚缺乏系统结果；同时希望借助 Vershik–Kerov 遍历方法给出无穷酉群块对角嵌入情形分类的新证明。

Method: 采用 Vershik–Kerov 遍历方法的一个变体，通过研究有限维子群特征标的弱极限来完成分类。

Result: 获得了 T^N ⋊ S_N 块对角归纳极限所有不可分解特征标的完整描述与分类；作为应用，给出了无穷酉群在块对角嵌入下不可分解特征标分类的另一证明。

Conclusion: 遍历方法变体是研究此类归纳极限特征标的有效工具，所得分类结果可统一处理并重新推导无穷酉群的相关分类。

Abstract: In this paper we obtain a complete description of all indecomposable characters (central positive-definite functions) of "block-diagonal" inductive limits of semidirect products $\mathbb{T}^N\rtimes\mathfrak{S}_{N}$ of the compact $N$-dimensional torus $\mathbb{T}^{N}$ with the symmetric group $\mathfrak{S}_{N}$. The classification is proved by means of a variant of the ergodic method of Vershik and Kerov, namely by studying the weak limits of characters of finite-dimensional subgroups. As an application of our results, we give another proof of the classification of indecomposable characters of the infinite unitary group with block diagonal embeddings.

</details>


### [236] [Frobenius functors and $n$-torsionfree objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25539)
*Zhibing Zhao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究阿贝尔范畴中的 n-无挠对象，证明 Frobenius 函子保持（忠实函子反映）n-无挠性；无挠滤过稳定蕴含弱 Gorenstein 性；在 Frobenius 扩张下转移 Auslander 型条件与 Auslander–Gorenstein 猜想，并构造无挠滤过在第二层稳定的非 Gorenstein 代数族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在足够投射对象的阿贝尔范畴中，n-无挠对象的滤过行为与 Gorenstein 同调性质之间存在联系；本文旨在揭示无挠滤过稳定性与弱 Gorenstein 性、Auslander 型条件及 Auslander–Gorenstein 猜想之间的关系，并通过 Frobenius（扩张）函子实现这些性质的transfer。

Method: 在具有足够投射对象的阿贝尔范畴中考察 n-无挠对象；利用 Frobenius 函子的保持性与忠实函子的反映性；证明无挠滤过稳定化蕴含弱 Gorenstein 性；对满足 generator 条件的 Frobenius 扩张，比较极小内射分解的各项，从而转移 Auslander 型条件与 Auslander–Gorenstein 猜想；并具体计算一类非 Gorenstein 代数。

Result: Frobenius 函子保持 n-无挠性，忠实 Frobenius 函子反映 n-无挠性；无挠滤过稳定化推出弱 Gorenstein 性；在 generator 条件下的 Frobenius 扩张中实现 Auslander 型条件与 Auslander–Gorenstein 猜想的transfer；给出一族非 Gorenstein 代数，其无挠滤过在第二层稳定，且含有显式的非投射 Gorenstein 投射模。

Conclusion: 无挠滤过的稳定性可作为弱 Gorenstein 性的判据，并可借助 Frobenius 扩张在代数间转移 Auslander 型条件与 Auslander–Gorenstein 猜想；所构造的非 Gorenstein 代数族为研究稳定层数与 Gorenstein 投射模提供了具体例子。

Abstract: We study $n$-torsionfree objects in abelian categories with enough projectives. Frobenius functors preserve $n$-torsionfreeness, and faithful ones reflect it. We prove that stabilization of the torsionfree filtration implies weak Gorensteinness. For Frobenius extensions satisfying a generator condition, we compare the terms of minimal injective resolutions and obtain transfer of Auslander-type conditions and of the Auslander--Gorenstein conjecture. We also compute a family of non-Gorenstein algebras whose torsionfree filtrations stabilize at level two and contain explicit nonprojective Gorenstein projective modules.

</details>


### [237] [Exact dg structures are classified intrinsically by bi-Serre subcategories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25583)
*Yasuaki Ogawa*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文把 Enomoto 关于精确结构的分类推广到 dg 层面，用三角范畴 $\mathsf{tr}(\mathscr{A})$ 中的 bi-Serre 子范畴对连通加法幂等完备 dg 范畴 $\mathscr{A}$ 上的精确 dg 结构进行内在分类，并由此得到偏序集同构与完备格结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Enomoto 的精确结构分类原本在加法范畴层面成立，作者希望将其提升到 dg（微分分次）层面，从而为精确 dg 结构提供内在刻画。

Method: 设 $\mathscr{A}$ 为连通加法幂等完备 dg 范畴。作者采用直接的 dg 论证，基于 3 项复形的 dg totalization 与 dg 对偶，而非化归到最大精确 dg 结构及其子结构的分类。

Result: 建立了 $\mathscr{A}$ 上精确 dg 结构与 $\mathsf{tr}(\mathscr{A})$ 中 bi-Serre 子范畴之间的双射；该双射是偏序集同构，并在精确 dg 结构类上诱导出完备格结构。特别地，给出了 Rump--Chen 最大精确 dg 结构的另一构造。

Conclusion: 本文成功将 Enomoto 分类提升至 dg 层面，得到精确 dg 结构的内在刻画，并统一了与 Rump--Chen 最大精确 dg 结构的关系。

Abstract: The aim of this article is to leverage Enomoto's classification of exact structures to the dg level. Let $\mathscr{A}$ be a connective additive idempotent complete dg category. We establish an intrinsic Enomoto-type classification of exact dg structures on $\mathscr{A}$ in terms of bi-Serre subcategories in $\mathsf{tr}(\mathscr{A})$. Our proof is a direct dg argument based on dg totalizations of $3$-term complexes and dg duality, rather than a reduction to the greatest exact dg structure and the classification of its substructures. As a consequence, we establish a bijection between exact dg structures on $\mathscr{A}$ and bi-Serre subcategories in $\mathsf{tr}(\mathscr{A})$. Moreover, this bijection is an isomorphism of posets and yields a complete lattice structure on the class of exact dg structures on $\mathscr{A}$. In particular, we provide another construction of Rump--Chen's greatest exact dg structure on $\mathscr{A}$.

</details>


### [238] [On the Twisted Group Ring Isomorphism Problem for isoclinic groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25718)
*Sumana Hatui,Sahanawaj Sabnam*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究扭曲群环同构问题（TGRIP），即判定有限群扭曲复群代数是否同构。作者给出同构的两个同源群具有同构扭曲复群代数的充分条件，从而对同源群情形求解TGRIP，并应用于秩2特殊p群、单中心群及具有初等阿贝尔Schur乘子的2类幂零群，还给出了阶为p^6（p≥3）的秩2特殊p群的完整解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典群环同构问题关注复群代数是否同构，而同源群的复群代数总是同构，但扭曲复群代数未必同构，因此需要研究TGRIP以刻画同源群在扭曲情形下的行为。

Method: 在同源群框架下，建立两个同源群具有同构扭曲复群代数的充分条件；并将结论应用于秩2特殊p群、单中心群以及具有初等阿贝尔Schur乘子的2类幂零群。

Result: 给出了同源群情形下TGRIP的充分条件与求解方案；对上述几类群得到TGRIP结果；并给出实例，包括阶为p^6（p≥3）的秩2特殊p群TGRIP的完整解。

Conclusion: 扭曲群环同构问题在同源群及若干重要群类中可借助充分条件得到解决，且所给实例验证了主要结果的有效性。

Abstract: In this article, we consider a general version of the classical group ring isomorphism problem, called the twisted group ring isomorphism problem (TGRIP), which determines an isomorphism between the twisted complex group algebras of finite groups. Although for isoclinic groups $G$ and $H$ their complex group algebras are isomorphic, i.e, $\mathbb C G \cong \mathbb C H$, their twisted complex group algebras need not be isomorphic. Continuing this line of investigation, we establish sufficient conditions under which two isoclinic groups have isomorphic twisted complex group algebras, thereby providing solutions to (TGRIP) for isoclinic groups. As applications, we study (TGRIP) for special $p$-groups of rank $2$, unicentral groups, and nilpotent groups of class $2$ with elementary abelian Schur multipliers, all considered up to isoclinism. We also present several examples illustrating the main results, including a complete solution of (TGRIP) for special $p$-groups of rank $2$ of order $p^6(p \ge 3)$

</details>


### [239] [Graded Representation Theory of Equivariant Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25776)
*Mani Shayestehfar*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文研究非线性激活在不可约表示间产生的等变交互，用高斯次数分解将多项式次数推广到非线性映射，证明固定逐坐标等变层的每个次数可分解为由线性映射决定的多项式与由激活函数决定的标量之积，从而分离出对称性、坐标与激活三种障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性映射无法刻画不可约表示之间的等变交互，而非线性激活可以产生这类交互；因此需要一个能对非线性等变映射进行“次数”刻度的工具，以理解其表达能力受哪些因素限制。

Method: 采用高斯次数（Gaussian degree）分解，将普通多项式次数概念推广到非线性等变映射，并针对固定的逐坐标等变层分析其各次数分量的结构。

Result: 证明对于固定的逐坐标等变层，每个次数分量可唯一分解为两部分：由线性映射决定的多项式部分，以及由激活函数决定的标量部分。

Conclusion: 该分解将等变层表达能力的限制清晰地区分为三类相互独立的障碍来源：对称性、坐标与激活函数。

Abstract: Nonlinear activations can create equivariant interactions between irreducible representations that linear maps cannot. We use the Gaussian degree decomposition to extend ordinary polynomial degree to such nonlinear maps, and prove that for a fixed coordinatewise equivariant layer each degree factors into a polynomial determined by the linear maps and a scalar determined by the activation. This separates three distinct obstructions, coming from symmetry, coordinates, and activation.

</details>


### [240] [Slash indecomposability of Endopermutation Scott modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25967)
*Lin Wu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了内置换Scott模为斜不可分解的一个判别准则，并证明在适当假设下只需验证局部子群上的某些内置换Scott模的斜不可分解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定内置换Scott模何时为斜不可分解是模表示论与块理论中的重要问题，通常直接在整体群上验证较为困难，因此需要寻找可归结到局部子群的判别方法。

Method: 通过给出一个判别准则（criterion），将内置换Scott模的斜不可分解性判定转化为条件性命题；在适当假设下，把问题归约到局部子群上相应内置换Scott模的斜不可分解性验证。

Result: 建立了一个内置换Scott模为斜不可分解的判别准则，并得到归约结论：在合适假设下，只需证明局部子群上的某些内置换Scott模是斜不可分解的。

Conclusion: 该准则简化了斜不可分解性的判定，将整体问题局部化，为后续研究提供了有效的技术工具。

Abstract: This paper gives a criterion for an endopermutation Scott module to be slash indecomposable. In particular, under suitable assumptions, we only need to prove certain endopermutation Scott modules over local subgroups are slash indecomposable.

</details>


### [241] [The Brauer $A(\infty)$ condition and Navarro's conjecture on Brauer characters under coprime actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26441)
*Zhicheng Feng,Lin Wu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文对好的非定义素因子证明了 Brauer $A(\infty)$ 条件，并由此对大于 3 的素数证明了 Navarro 关于互素作用与 Brauer 特征标的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brauer $A(\infty)$ 条件与 Navarro 关于互素作用及 Brauer 特征标的猜想是有限群模表示论中的重要问题，前者为该猜想提供了关键的理论支撑。

Method: 采用理论证明的方法，对好的非定义素数（good non-defining primes）建立 Brauer $A(\infty)$ 条件，并以此为基础推导出 Navarro 猜想的相关结论。

Result: 对好的非定义素数成功证明了 Brauer $A(\infty)$ 条件，并进一步对大于 3 的素数证明了 Navarro 关于互素作用与 Brauer 特征标的猜想。

Conclusion: Brauer $A(\infty)$ 条件的成立为 Navarro 猜想在大于 3 的素数情形下提供了证明，深化了对互素作用与 Brauer 特征标关系的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the Brauer $A(\infty)$ condition for good non-defining primes. Consequently, we show a conjecture of Navarro on coprime actions and Brauer characters for primes larger than 3.

</details>


### [242] [Representation-tame Geiß-Leclerc-Schröer algebras and a revised GLS conjecture on root systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26503)
*Qiang Dong,Zengqiang Lin,Ming Lu,Shiquan Ruan*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文利用伽罗瓦覆盖理论与等变技术，按定义三元组 $(C,D,\Omega)$ 分类了所有连通的表示驯顺 GLS 代数；并通过将 $H(\widetilde{CD}_n)$ 实现为弦代数 $H(\widetilde{C}_{2n-2})$ 的 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-斜群代数的基本代数，引入扩展弦与扩展带刻画其 AR 箭图的连通分支，同时对仿射型表示驯顺 GLS 代数的不可分解 $\tau$-局部自由模进行分类，修正了 GLS 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GLS 代数是与簇代数、箭图表示论密切相关的重要代数类。此前对表示驯顺 GLS 代数缺乏基于定义三元组的完整分类，且对 $H(\widetilde{CD}_n)$ 这类 algebras 的 AR 箭图结构及仿射型下不可分解模的刻画尚不清晰，GLS 猜想在仿射型情形的表述也需要更精确的修正。

Method: 1) 采用伽罗瓦覆盖理论与等变技术，以定义三元组 $(C,D,\Omega)$ 为参数对连通表示驯顺 GLS 代数进行分类；2) 针对具有极小对称化子的 $H(\widetilde{CD}_n)$（$n\geq2$，$\widetilde{CD}_2=\widetilde{B}_2$），将其实现为弦代数 $H(\widetilde{C}_{2n-2})$ 的 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-斜群代数的基本代数；3) 引入扩展弦与扩展带，用以参数化 AR 箭图的连通分支并确定其形状；4) 分类仿射型表示驯顺 GLS 代数的不可分解 $\tau$-局部自由模，计算其秩向量。

Result: 给出了连通表示驯顺 GLS 代数基于 $(C,D,\Omega)$ 的完整分类；通过扩展弦与扩展带完整描述了 $H(\widetilde{CD}_n)$ 的 AR 箭图连通分支及其形状；证明了仿射型表示驯顺 GLS 代数不可分解 $\tau$-局部自由模的秩向量恰为根格正锥中的正根并附加显式描述的非根向量。

Conclusion: 本文对表示驯顺 GLS 代数给出了系统分类与模论刻画，并借助斜群代数与扩展弦/带方法厘清了 $H(\widetilde{CD}_n)$ 的 AR 箭图结构，从而对仿射型表示驯顺 GLS 代数的 GLS 猜想给出了精确修正。

Abstract: Using Galois covering theory and equivariant techniques, we classify all connected representation-tame Geiß--Leclerc--Schröer (GLS) algebras in terms of their defining triples $(C,D,Ω)$. We then study the GLS algebras $H(\widetilde{CD}_n)$ with minimal symmetrizers, where $n\geq 2$ and $\widetilde{CD}_2=\widetilde{B}_2$. By realizing these algebras as basic algebras of $\mathbb{Z}_2$-skew group algebras of the string algebras $H(\widetilde{C}_{2n-2})$, we introduce extended strings and extended bands to parameterize the connected components of the Auslander--Reiten quivers of $H(\widetilde{CD}_n)$ and determine their shapes. We further classify the indecomposable $τ$-locally free modules over representation-tame GLS algebras of affine type and show that their rank vectors form precisely the set of positive roots together with explicitly described non-root vectors in the positive cone of the root lattice. This yields a precise revision of the GLS conjecture for representation-tame GLS algebras of affine type.

</details>


### [243] [Multiplicity One Property for Archimedean Theta Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26630)
*Zhibin Geng,Kaidi Wu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 对任意阿基米德约化对偶对，证明小theta提升在全theta提升中的Jordan-Hölder重数为1，并给出基于最低次K-型的theta对应新描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画阿基米德theta对应中小theta提升在全theta提升中的出现方式，即确定其Jordan-Hölder重数，并寻求以最低次K-型为核心的新描述方式。

Method: 关键工具是证明全theta提升与小theta提升中最低次K-型重数相等这一等式，进而推导Jordan-Hölder重数结论。

Result: 对任意阿基米德约化对偶对，小theta提升在全theta提升中的Jordan-Hölder重数为1；并由此得到阿基米德theta对应基于最低次K-型的新描述。

Conclusion: 阿基米德theta对应可由最低次K-型有效刻画，小theta提升以重数1嵌入全theta提升。

Abstract: For any archimedean reductive dual pair, we prove that the Jordan-Hölder multiplicity of the small theta lift in the full theta lift is one. The main input is an equality between the multiplicities of the lowest degree $K$-types in the full and small theta lifts. We also deduce a new description of the archimedean theta correspondence in terms of lowest degree K-types.

</details>


<div id='math.SG'></div>

# math.SG [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Real Inertia, Phase Obstructions, and de Rham Algebras for Orientifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25064)
*Xiaobin Li*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限orientifold商的de Rham实现：在几乎复全局商的保向惰性上构造Chen-Hu代数的反线性对合，其不动子代数是Chen-Ruan代数的实形式；对真正反向固定扇区证明不存在坐标不变的单扇区分次龄。当Calabi-Yau体积线被保持时，相对相位定义上同调类，并将其等同于相干实相位提升的Bockstein障碍，证明其消失等价于相位平凡的orientifold作用。随后用相对Fredholm丛与行列式线拼接建立Euler-Gysin实现定理，并在相位平凡Calabi-Yau曲面上验证可容许性，得到包含Chen-Hu偶扇区与奇固定曲面的结合分次上同调代数；对具六阶二面体作用的Eisenstein阿贝尔曲面显式计算中心化子不变量代数，得到16维Frobenius代数且奇扇区乘法在平移分次下非交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限orientiffold商的de Rham实现及Chen-Ruan/Chen-Hu代数的实形式尚缺乏系统刻画；尤其需要理解反向固定扇区的分次龄障碍、体积线相位与上同调障碍之间的关系。

Method: 构造Chen-Hu代数上的反线性对合并取不动子代数；用相对相位定义上同调类并识别为Bockstein障碍；使用相对Fredholm丛与行列式线拼接证明Euler-Gysin实现定理；在相位平凡Calabi-Yau曲面上验证可容许性并显式计算Eisenstein阿贝尔曲面的中心化子不变量代数。

Result: 证明真正反向固定扇区不存在坐标不变单扇区分次龄；相位类等同于Bockstein障碍且其消失等价于（重相位体积形式后）相位平凡的orientifold作用；给出Euler-Gysin实现定理；在相位平凡Calabi-Yau曲面上得到结合分次上同调代数，包含Chen-Hu偶扇区与奇固定曲面；Eisenstein阿贝尔曲面在六阶二面体作用下的不变量代数为16维Frobenius代数，奇扇区乘法在平移分次下非交换。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了有限orientifold商的de Rham/Chen-Hu实形式框架，澄清了相位障碍与可容许性条件，并给出显式代数实例，同时与早期无向orbifold上同调构造相联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we study de Rham realizations for finite orientifold quotients. On the orientation preserving inertia of an almost complex global quotient we construct an anti-linear involution of the Chen-Hu algebra whose fixed subalgebra is a real form of the Chen-Ruan algebra. For genuine orientation reversing fixed sectors we prove that no coordinate invariant single sector fractional age exists.
  When a Calabi-Yau volume line is preserved, the relative phases define a canonical class $[μ_{\mathbb R}]\in H^2(\widehat G;\mathbb Z_ε)$. We identify this class with the Bockstein obstruction to a coherent real phase lift and prove that its vanishing is equivalent, after rephasing the volume form, to a phase trivial orientifold action. We then formulate a general Euler-Gysin realization theorem using relative Fredholm bundles and determinant-line sewing.
  For phase trivial Calabi-Yau surfaces we verify the admissibility conditions directly and obtain a canonically defined associative graded cohomology algebra containing the Chen-Hu even sector and the odd fixed surfaces. For an Eisenstein abelian surface with an order six dihedral action we compute the centralizer invariant algebra explicitly; it is a $16$ dimensional Frobenius algebra whose odd sector multiplication is noncommutative in the shifted grading. We also explain the relation with earlier unoriented orbifold cohomology constructions.

</details>


### [245] [KAM splittings and equidistributed periodic orbits for stable hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25465)
*Robert Cardona,Oliver Edtmair,Rohil Prasad*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 证明满足有理上同调条件的辛4-流形稳定超曲面可被闭特征等分布的（可能不稳定）超曲面逼近，证明结合KAM与Reeb流/保面积映射的定量闭合引理，并推广至3-流形上可测地体积保持向量场的逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究稳定超曲面上闭特征（周期轨道）的分布规律，特别是能否通过逼近方式实现等分布；Herman的著名反例表明上同调条件不可去除，因此需要在该必要条件下探讨逼近的可能性。

Method: 将KAM理论与近期关于Reeb流及保面积映射的定量闭合引理相结合，构造C^∞逼近；并通过类比推广到三维流形上的可测地体积保持向量场情形。

Result: 在辛形式上同调类限制为有理类倍数的条件下，任何稳定超曲面都可由闭特征等分布的（可能不稳定）超曲面C^∞逼近；此外，闭三维流形上每个可测地体积保持向量场都可由周期轨道等分布的体积保持向量场C^∞逼近。

Conclusion: 上同调条件是必要的，逼近结论在辛4-流形稳定超曲面及三维流形体积保持向量场两类情形下均成立。

Abstract: We show that any stable hypersurface of a symplectic $4$-manifold, on which the cohomology class of the symplectic form restricts to a multiple of a rational class, can be $C^\infty$-approximated by (possibly unstable) hypersurfaces whose closed characteristics equidistribute. The cohomological condition is necessary due to a famous example of Herman. The proof combines KAM theory with recent quantitative closing lemmas for Reeb flows and area-preserving maps. As a further application, we prove that every geodesible volume-preserving vector field on a closed three-manifold can be $C^\infty$-approximated by volume-preserving vector fields with equidistributed periodic orbits.

</details>


### [246] [Welschinger invariants and the Conway polynomial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26584)
*Vivek Shende,Wennan Zhang*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 将 Welschinger 的连通全纯圆盘计数不变量解释为一个全亏格曲线计数的首阶项，该全亏格计数以边界的 Conway 多项式为权重，并可视为 skein-值曲线计数的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Welschinger 通过"自环绕数"加权的不连通圆盘计数修正，使辛 6 维流形中带拉格朗日边界的连通全纯圆盘计数（至少满足一个边界约束）成为不变量。作者希望为这一修正不变量提供更本质的代数拓扑解释，将其纳入全亏格曲线计数框架。

Method: 构造一个全亏格曲线计数，其中每条曲线按其边界的 Conway 多项式加权；证明 Welschinger 不变量恰为该计数的首阶（最低阶）项。进一步说明该计数是 skein-值曲线计数的一个特化，且无需使用后者的 4-链与向量场结构。

Result: Welschinger 不变量等于所构造的全亏格、Conway 多项式加权曲线计数的最低阶项；该计数可独立于 skein-值框架中的 4-链与向量场进行定义。

Conclusion: Welschinger 的圆盘不变量获得了统一的全亏格、skein-型的解释，为带拉格朗日边界全纯曲线的计数提供了新的代数拓扑视角。

Abstract: Welschinger showed that counts of connected holomorphic disks with Lagrangian boundary in symplectic 6-manifolds, meeting at least one boundary constraint, can be made invariant by correcting them with counts of disconnected disks weighted by certain "self-linking" numbers. We show his invariant is the lowest order term in an all-genus curve count where curves are weighted by the Conway polynomials of their boundaries. This in turn is a specialization of the skein-valued curve count, but can be defined without the 4-chain and vector field used in that setup.

</details>
